[Scripts]
Galaith, the royal city. Its white towers shine over the rooftops.	Galaith，王都。它的白色塔楼在屋顶上方闪耀。
The arena! Step onto the sand to fight other players (right-click them: Attack). The paving is safe. Beaten, you lose nothing: you wake by the gate.	竞技场！踏上沙地就能和其他玩家战斗（右键点击他们：攻击）。铺路的地方是安全的。被打败了你什么也不会失去：你会在大门旁醒来。
A damp cellar. The far wall has fallen in, and something squeaks in the dark.	一间潮湿的地窖。远处的墙塌了，黑暗中有东西在吱吱叫。
Puddleford. You smell fresh bread.	Puddleford。你闻到了新鲜面包的香味。
Brinebottom. The tide hisses in the reeds.	Brinebottom。潮水在芦苇丛中嘶嘶作响。
Puddleford.	Puddleford。
The farmlands of Puddleford.	Puddleford 的农田。
Dullford.	Dullford。
Sulkhaven, on its island.	Sulkhaven，在它的岛上。
Cold air, flagstones, and a smell of old barrels.	冷空气，石板地，还有一股陈年木桶的味道。
Chillingham. Your breath steams in the cold.	Chillingham。你的呼吸在寒气中冒着白雾。
Sergeant Holt's men are through the camp's gate.	Holt 中士的人已经冲进了营地的大门。
The grotto under Muddlesey. Water drips into a black pool.	Muddlesey 下的洞穴。水滴进一个漆黑的水池。
Torchlight, bones and the stink of hobgoblin: their lair!	火把的光、骨头，还有大地精的臭味：它们的巢穴！
Wildwood.	Wildwood。
The Wizards' Court. The air tingles with magic.	巫师庭。空气中弥漫着魔法的刺痛感。
Nettlewick.	Nettlewick。
The wizard's study. Strange smells drift from the bubbling vials.	巫师的书房。冒泡的药瓶里飘出奇怪的气味。
The old iron mine. The air is cold and smells of rust.	旧铁矿。空气冰冷，有股铁锈味。
Something growls in the dark tunnels ahead.	前方黑暗的隧道里有东西在咆哮。
The hobgoblin warren! Bones crunch underfoot.	大地精洞窟！骨头在脚下嘎吱作响。
The Deep Hollow. Torchlight flickers ahead... and bones rattle in the dark.	深谷。前方火把的光在闪烁……黑暗中骨头咔咔作响。
King Rupert's Keep. The sea booms against the rocks below the walls.	鲁珀特王堡。大海在城墙下的岩石上轰鸣。
King Rupert's Keep, held by his Steward.	鲁珀特王堡，由他的总管掌管。
Dust, cold, and the clatter of old bones standing to attention.	尘土，寒冷，还有老骨头立正时的咔哒声。
The Cinder Peaks. The air shimmers over the lava, and somewhere above, men are chanting.	Cinder Peaks。熔岩上的空气在晃动，上面某处，有人在吟唱。
Emberwatch, the last town before the Cinder Peaks. Smoke on the wind.	Emberwatch，Cinder Peaks 前的最后一座小镇。风中有烟。
Dry air, old paper, and something shuffling between the shelves.	干燥的空气，陈旧的纸张，还有东西在书架间拖着脚步走动。
Dunerest, at the desert's edge.	Dunerest，在沙漠的边缘。
Drizzleport. Gulls wheel over the harbour.	Drizzleport。海鸥在港口上空盘旋。
Acornby.	Acornby。
Bramble's woodcutters are in the grove, axes ready.	Bramble 的伐木工已经在林子里了，斧头准备就绪。
Alchemy: pick herbs, berries and mushrooms, and brew potions at an alchemy table.	炼金：采摘草药、浆果和蘑菇，然后在炼金台上酿造药水。
The last of the three, burning blue like the others. In the ash round it there are footprints, a lot of them, going off towards the Hand's camp.	三座中的最后一座，和其他的一样燃着蓝火。它周围的灰烬里有脚印，很多脚印，朝灰烬之手的营地走去。
The Hand's shrine is lit again, but the flames are blue, and when you hold your hand in them it's only cold. That's $Variable.4 of the three.	灰烬之手的神龛又被点燃了，但火焰是蓝色的，你把手伸进去，只觉得冷。这是三座中的第 $Variable.4 座。
The Hand's shrine, lit again. The flames are blue, and give no warmth at all.	灰烬之手的神龛，又被点燃了。火焰是蓝色的，一点也不暖。
A burnt shrine of the Ashen Hand, cold now. A board by it says ETERNAL FLAME, and under that, in chalk, PLEASE REPORT ANY OUTAGES.	灰烬之手的一座烧毁的神龛，如今冰冷。旁边的告示上写着“永恒之火”，下面有人用粉笔补了一句：“如已熄灭，请告知。”
The barrow is out on the downs west of King Rupert's Keep: look for the broken walls.	古冢在鲁珀特王堡西边的高地上：找那些断墙。
The Barrow King has two skeleton guards. Bring food.	古冢之王有两个骷髅卫兵。带上食物。
Broken walls, open graves... and a crowned skeleton rising from its tomb. The Barrow King!	断墙，敞开的坟墓……一具戴着王冠的骷髅正从坟墓中升起。古冢之王！
Broken? You broke the anchor? In the Shardrealm? Ha! I said you were good. Did I say that? I meant to.\But you've a face like a wet week. What did you see down there?	击碎了？你击碎了锚？在碎片界里？哈！我就说你很厉害。我说过吗？我本来想说的。\可你的脸色像下了一个星期的雨。你在下面看到了什么？
The dragon. It laughed at my sword.	那条龙。它嘲笑我的剑。
Quest complete: The Shardgate! The barrier's anchor is broken; the Shardgate stays open to you.	任务完成：碎片之门！屏障之锚被击碎了；碎片之门为你保持敞开。
Laughed, did it? Then the barrier's only light and spite now, and what's behind it isn't. You're not ready for it, heir, and nor's that sword. Grow into it, then take the staff to the pass and look it in the eye.	嘲笑，是吗？那屏障现在只剩下光和怨恨了，而它后面的东西可不是。你还没准备好面对它，继承人，那把剑也没有。先成长起来，然后带着杖去山口，直视它的眼睛。
I will.	我会的。
It held? Of course it held. Oh, don't look at me like that: I only half thought it would.\Listen. The first king cut the dragon down, and the greater part of it didn't die. It went somewhere: the Shardrealm, where all broken magic goes. The barrier draws on it. It's anchored there, beyond the world.	它撑住了？当然撑住了。哦，别那样看着我：我只是有一半觉得它会撑住。\听着。第一位国王砍倒了那条龙，而它的大部分并没有死。它去了某个地方：碎片界，所有破碎的魔法都去那里。屏障从那里汲取力量。它被锚定在那里，在世界之外。
How do I get there?	我怎么去那里？
You build a door. Not a little one. Any old shardstone can reach the realm's rim, where it's calm and thin and small things hold. That homestead of yours, if you've one? The Stillwater, the settlers call it. It's a pocket of the rim, held by your anchor. Never wondered why it has no neighbours? But the barrier's anchor is at the core, a storm where all the broken magic pools, and you have to pierce that. Only a great anchor of your own will do it: a Shardgate. The old ring of standing stones on the hill north of this Court was one, once.\Here are my plans. Silver, sapphires, a Remnant's core, and Shards: it drinks them. And it must be steadied: wake the shardstones of Brambleton, eight at the least, so the ring has something to hold to while it drives through. Through the gate, find the anchor and break it with the staff. Something will be guarding it. Something always is.	你造一扇门。不是一扇小门。任何一块老碎片石都能到达界域的边缘，那里平静而稀薄，小东西能稳住。你的那个家园，如果你有的话？定居者们叫它静水。它是边缘的一小方天地，由你的锚稳住。你从没奇怪过它为什么没有邻居吗？但屏障之锚在核心，那是所有破碎魔法汇聚的风暴，你必须刺穿它。只有一个你自己的巨大锚点才能做到：一座碎片之门。这座巫师庭北边山上那圈古老的立石，曾经就是一座。\这是我的图纸。银、蓝宝石、一颗残迹核心，还有碎片：它会把它们喝掉。而且它必须被稳住：唤醒 Brambleton 的碎片石，至少八块，这样石环在冲进去时才有东西可以依靠。穿过那扇门，找到锚，用杖击碎它。会有东西守着它的。总是有东西守着。
I'll build it.	我会造出来的。
Bert gave you his plans for a Shardgate (Read them).	Bert 把他的碎片之门图纸给了你（阅读它们）。
The barrier's gone, yes, yes. But its anchor isn't: it still hums in the core of the Shardrealm, where all broken magic pools. I can feel it from here.\Build a Shardgate in the old ring of stones on the hill north of the Court: here are my plans. Wake eight of Brambleton's shardstones to steady it, pierce the core, break the anchor with the staff, and the realm's yours to wander.	屏障没了，是的，是的。但它的锚还在：它仍在碎片界的核心嗡嗡作响，所有破碎的魔法都汇聚在那里。我在这儿都能感觉到。\在巫师庭北边山上那圈古老的石环里造一座碎片之门：这是我的图纸。唤醒 Brambleton 的八块碎片石来稳住它，刺穿核心，用杖击碎锚，界域就任你漫游了。
Quest started: The Shardgate. Bert gave you his plans (Read them).	任务开始：碎片之门。Bert 把他的图纸给了你（阅读它们）。
The staff! The grimoire! And the ring... oh, look at it. Look at that glow.\He slips the Ember Ring on his finger and holds it up against his sleeve.\Purple and ember. I knew it. It goes with the robe perfectly.	杖！魔典！还有戒指……哦，看看它。看看那光芒。\他把余烬戒指戴到手指上，举起来贴着袖子比了比。\紫色配余烬色。我就知道。和长袍简直绝配。
And the spell?	那法术呢？
Ah. Yes. Well. Between ourselves, the spell doesn't need the ring. Never did. I wanted it because it suits me, and because the Ashen Priest is an insufferable windbag who deserved to lose it.\The grimoire's what matters. Listen, and remember: these are the words of Unbinding.	啊。是的。嗯。咱们私下说，法术不需要戒指。从来都不需要。我想要它是因为它适合我，也因为灰烬祭司是个让人受不了的吹牛大王，活该丢了它。\要紧的是魔典。听着，记住：这些就是解缚之言。
I'm listening.	我在听。
Bert reads from the grimoire. The words slide into your memory and stay there, humming.	Bert 读着魔典。那些话语滑进你的记忆，留在那里，嗡嗡作响。
Take the staff back: it's the King's, and the spell needs a king's staff. Hold it to the barrier and say the words, and it will fall.\But hear me. Behind the barrier waits the dragon, and no blade made by men will kill it. Only the Sword of Valour, driven into its heart. It lies on Dragon Island, out in the southern sea. Captain Rook, on the shore below the Keep, is the only sailor mad enough to take you there.	把杖拿回去：它是国王的，而这个法术需要国王的杖。把它举向屏障，说出那些话，屏障就会倒下。\但听我说。屏障后面等着那条龙，凡人打造的刀刃杀不死它。只有勇气之剑，刺进它的心脏，才行。它在龙岛上，在南方的海里。王堡下面海岸上的 Rook 船长，是唯一一个疯狂到肯带你去那里的水手。
Thank you, Bert.	谢谢你，Bert。
Quest complete: Bert's Three Treasures! You know the spell of Unbinding.	任务完成：Bert 的三件宝物！你学会了解缚之法。
It laughed? At the Sword of Valour? ...Oh. Oh, I should have seen it coming.\Every Shard the Hand slips it through the barrier, it wears. They cling to it like iron filings to a lodestone, and I don't like to think why.\And the sword's been asleep since the first king laid it down. It was made in three fires, heir, and it wants waking in them, in their order.	它笑了？笑勇气之剑？……哦。哦，我早该料到的。\灰烬之手每把一块碎片送过屏障，它就穿戴上一块。碎片像铁屑吸在天磁石上一样黏在它身上，我都不愿去想为什么。\而那把剑自从第一位国王把它放下后就一直在沉睡。它是在三团火中锻造的，继承人，它需要在这三团火中被唤醒，按它们的顺序。
Where are they?	它们在哪儿？
The first was the Ashen Foundry, in the Ashfall, where the old smiths worked before the ash came down. The warden at the outpost there keeps an eye on what's left of it. She'll know the rest, or know who does.\Don't look at me like that. I'm a wizard, not a smith.	第一团火是灰烬铸造厂，在 Ashfall，灰烬落下之前老铁匠们就在那里干活。那里前哨的守望者在照看它剩下的部分。她会知道剩下的，或者知道谁知道。\别那样看着我。我是巫师，不是铁匠。
I'll go.	我这就去。
Quest complete: The Sleeping Sword!	任务完成：沉睡之剑！
The great oak bears Bramble's mark. Go back to him.	大橡树上刻下了 Bramble 的记号。回到他那里去。
You cut Bramble's mark deep into the great oak's bark. His men will take it as leave to start.	你在大橡树的树皮上深深刻下了 Bramble 的记号。他的人会把这当作可以开工的许可。
Three hundred years old, at least. It would be a shame to cut it.	至少有三百岁了。砍掉它太可惜了。
You are too far away!	你离得太远了！
The horn's note goes out low over the fen and comes back from the hills. The black pool heaves, and something bobs up against the bank, streaming. The horn slips off its post into the reeds.	号角的声音低低地越过沼泽，又从山丘那边传回来。黑色的池子翻涌起来，有个东西漂上来靠在岸边，淌着水。号角从柱子上滑进了芦苇丛。
The spring runs again. Tell Elder Sabra.	泉水又流了。告诉 Sabra 长老。
You haul the stones aside and drag out the sacking. The spring runs again, down towards Dunerest.	你把石头搬到一边，拖出麻袋。泉水又流了，朝着 Dunerest 流去。
Someone's dammed the spring north of Dunerest, and the stones came on Corvin's cart. Pull the dam down, or see what Corvin has to say.	有人在 Dunerest 北边的泉水上筑了坝，石头是用 Corvin 的马车运来的。拆掉水坝，或者听听 Corvin 怎么说。
Stones and sacking, packed across the spring's channel, and cart tracks back to Dunerest: Corvin's cart, with his barrels on it.	石头和麻袋，塞满了泉水的水道，车辙一直通回 Dunerest：Corvin 的马车，上面装着他的水桶。
Corvin paid you to leave it be.	Corvin 付钱让你别管它。
Something comes up where she sank: a book in an oilskin, its pages stuck together.	她沉下去的地方冒上来一个东西：一本裹在油布里的书，书页粘在一起。
My mother said the Wardens listened so that nobody else would have to. Was it like that?\...No. Don't tell me. Sit down.	我母亲说，守卫们聆听，是为了让别人不必聆听。是那样的吗？\……不。别告诉我。坐下吧。
Goodbye, Bryony.	再见，Bryony。
Sit down, sit down. Nell's out on the rocks again. That girl will have the sea in her pockets one of these days.	坐，坐。Nell 又去礁石上了。那姑娘总有一天会把大海装进口袋里。
You bind the stones with silver, set the sapphires in their runes, and lay the Remnant's core at the ring's heart. Far off, the shardstones you woke answer, and the ring takes hold of them..	你用银把石头连在一起，把蓝宝石嵌进它们的符文，把残迹核心放在石环的中心。远处，你唤醒的碎片石作出回应，石环抓住了它们..
..and the Shards drain out of you into the stones, and light drives down between them, through the calm rim of the Shardrealm and into the storm at its core: a Shardgate.	..碎片从你体内流进石头，光在石头之间向下冲去，穿过碎片界平静的边缘，冲进它核心的风暴：一座碎片之门。
A Shardgate needs 4 silver bars, 2 sapphires and a Remnant's core, as Bert's plans say.	就像 Bert 的图纸上写的，一座碎片之门需要 4 根银锭、2 颗蓝宝石和一颗残迹核心。
The ring won't hold against the Shardrealm's core: it needs $Shards.Shardgate.Stones shardstones active to steady it, and you have $Shardstones.Active.	石环抵挡不住碎片界的核心：它需要 $Shards.Shardgate.Stones 块激活的碎片石来稳住它，而你有 $Shardstones.Active 块。
You need level $Shards.Shardgate.Level Shardcraft to bind a Shardgate.	你需要碎片术 $Shards.Shardgate.Level 级才能绑定一座碎片之门。
Old standing stones, worn smooth. A faint rune on each.	古老的立石，被磨得光滑。每块上面都有一个模糊的符文。
You bury the Bones!	你埋葬了骨头！
The Gods have blessed you with Gold!	众神赐予你金币！
Qadir's caravan was lost in the Sunscar Desert, west of Dunerest.	Qadir 的商队在 Dunerest 西边的 Sunscar 沙漠里失踪了。
Slay the scorpions at the wreck.	杀死残骸旁的蝎子。
Find the caravan's ledger and bring it to Qadir.	找到商队的账本，把它带给 Qadir。
The caravan! Bones in the sand... and scorpions. Find Qadir's ledger.	商队！沙子里的骨头……还有蝎子。找到 Qadir 的账本。
All three lambs caught: tell Abel.	三只羊羔都抓到了：告诉 Abel。
You catch the last lamb. It bleats, sorry at last, and runs off for home.	你抓住了最后一只羊羔。它咩咩叫着，终于认错了，跑回家去了。
You catch the lamb. It bleats at you, then runs off for home as if that had been the idea all along.	你抓住了羊羔。它冲你咩咩叫，然后跑回家去，好像那本来就是它的主意。
The lamb skips out of reach. It doesn't know you.	羊羔蹦蹦跳跳地躲开了。它不认识你。
Channeling: cast spells. The better you are, the harder they hit and the fewer Shards each cast drinks.	引导：施放法术。你越熟练，法术打得越重，每次施法消耗的碎片也越少。
You take it all in: the Brinewater, for Pell's chart.	你把一切都看在眼里：盐水湖，为了 Pell 的海图。
Every place is on the chart.	每个地方都在海图上了。
That's all five: back to Pell.	五个地方都齐了：回到 Pell 那里。
You take it all in: the Shardfall, for Pell's chart.	你把一切都看在眼里：碎片坠落地，为了 Pell 的海图。
You take it all in: the singing stone, for Pell's chart.	你把一切都看在眼里：那块会唱歌的石头，为了 Pell 的海图。
You take it all in: the Wardens' Hold, for Pell's chart.	你把一切都看在眼里：守卫要塞，为了 Pell 的海图。
You take it all in: Leviathan's Rest, for Pell's chart.	你把一切都看在眼里：利维坦安息地，为了 Pell 的海图。
The ice gives with a crack like a bell. You don't take him out, in the end: only what was in his hand, held out all these winters.	冰面发出一声像钟一样的脆响，裂开了。最后你没有把他拉出来：只拿走了他手里的东西，这么多个冬天一直伸着递出来的东西。
Chips fly. The ice over him cracks, but holds. Again.	冰屑飞溅。他上面的冰裂开了，但还撑着。再来。
He has nothing more in his hands. You leave him to the lake.	他手里什么都没有了。你把他留给了湖。
You need a pickaxe to chip at the ice.	你需要一把镐才能凿冰。
You swing your axe at the fallen log..	你挥斧砍向倒下的原木..
..and manage to get some logs!	..砍到了一些原木！
..but fail to get any logs!	..但什么原木也没砍到！
You need an axe!	你需要一把斧头！
You swing your axe at the oak..	你挥斧砍向橡树..
..and manage to get some oak logs!	..砍到了一些橡木原木！
You need level 5 Woodcutting to chop this oak.	你需要伐木 5 级才能砍这棵橡树。
You swing your axe at the tree..	你挥斧砍向这棵树..
You swing your axe at the willow..	你挥斧砍向柳树..
..and manage to get some willow logs!	..砍到了一些柳木原木！
You need level 10 Woodcutting to chop this willow.	你需要伐木 10 级才能砍这棵柳树。
You swing your axe at the yew..	你挥斧砍向紫杉..
..and manage to get some yew logs!	..砍到了一些紫杉原木！
You need level 18 Woodcutting to chop this yew.	你需要伐木 18 级才能砍这棵紫杉。
Chanting, and the smell of burning. In the yard, a priest in smouldering robes, a ring of fire on his finger, leads the Ashen Hand in a hymn to the dragon. Nobody seems to know the second verse.	吟唱声，还有燃烧的气味。院子里，一个长袍冒着烟、手指上戴着火焰戒指的祭司，正带领灰烬之手唱一首献给龙的圣歌。似乎没有人知道第二段。
You lift the hatch and climb down into the dark. Somewhere below, boots.	你掀开舱口，爬下去进入黑暗。下面某处，有靴子的声音。
The hatch is locked.	舱口锁着。
You climb down the ladder, into the caves under Muddlesey.	你顺着梯子爬下去，进入 Muddlesey 下的洞穴。
You go down the steps, into the cellar.	你走下台阶，进入地窖。
You climb down the ladder.	你顺着梯子爬下去。
The hatch is shut, and it isn't yours to open.	舱口关着，而且不是你能打开的。
You go down the steps, into the dark under the sand.	你走下台阶，进入沙子下面的黑暗中。
You climb down into a small stone room. The sea is very close, the other side of the wall.	你爬下去，进入一间小石室。大海非常近，就在墙的另一边。
The hatch won't move.	舱口纹丝不动。
You crawl the length of Meg's tunnel, and come up in her camp.	你爬完了梅格的整条地道，从她的营地里冒了出来。
You crawl the length of Meg's tunnel, and come up in a field outside Puddleford.	你爬完了梅格的整条地道，从 Puddleford 外的一片田里冒了出来。
The slab tips on a pin, and there are steps under it, cut in the rock. Down below, people are singing.	石板绕着一根轴翻转过来，下面有台阶，凿在岩石里。下面，有人在唱歌。
You go down the stairs.	你走下楼梯。
You promised Maren: not until Brother Aldous says you're ready.	你答应过 Maren：要等 Aldous 修士说你准备好了才行。
The trapdoor is locked.	活板门锁着。
The steps go down a long way, under the chapel and under the town. It gets colder all the way.	台阶往下延伸了很远，在礼拜堂下面，在小镇下面。越往下越冷。
You squeeze in through the window. Dust everywhere: nobody has been here for a long time.	你从窗户挤了进去。到处都是灰尘：已经很久没人来过这里了。
You slide back the bolt on the door: now you can come and go that way.	你拉开门上的插销：现在你可以从那边进出了。
You climb back out through the window.	你从窗户爬了出去。
You climb up the ladder, into the chapel cellar.	你顺着梯子爬上去，进入礼拜堂的地窖。
You climb the ladder and come up under a bedroll, inside a tent in the hobgoblins' camp.	你爬上梯子，从一个铺盖卷下面钻出来，在大地精营地的一顶帐篷里。
You climb up the ladder.	你顺着梯子爬上去。
You climb up the ladder into the loft.	你顺着梯子爬上阁楼。
You go up the stairs.	你走上楼梯。
You climb up through the barrel, and out by the lake.	你从木桶里爬上去，在湖边钻了出来。
You push up a hatch behind stacked cargo... a palisade, tents, a campfire: a smugglers' camp! Keep out of their sight.	你推开堆着的货物后面的一个舱口……一道栅栏、帐篷、一堆营火：一个走私者的营地！别让他们看见你。
You feel around in the straw...	你在稻草里摸索...
...and find a fresh egg!	...找到了一个新鲜的鸡蛋！
Crafting: smelt ore and forge tools at a furnace or anvil (the Puddleford smithy, Brokk's hall); shear sheep, then tan, spin and make jewellery at a workbench.	制作：在熔炉或铁砧上冶炼矿石和锻造工具（Puddleford 的铁匠铺，Brokk 的大厅）；剪羊毛，然后在工作台上鞣皮、纺线和制作珠宝。
You dug up Tobin's strongbox. Tell Salt Agnes.	你挖出了 Tobin 的保险箱。告诉 Salt Agnes。
Under the stones, a strongbox gone green at the corners. Tobin's. He'd want somebody to have it.	石头下面有一个角上已经发绿的保险箱。是 Tobin 的。他会希望有人拿到它。
Stones, on top of stones. Whoever piled them here meant them to stay.	石头，一块叠着一块。把它们堆在这里的人，是想让它们留在这里的。
You need a spade to dig.	你需要一把铁锹才能挖掘。
You dig into the sand...	你挖进沙子里...
...and fill a sack with sand.	...装了一袋沙子。
You need a spade to dig here.	你需要一把铁锹才能在这里挖。
You dig into the dune..	你挖进沙丘..
..and the sand slides away from a carved doorway, and steps going down.	..沙子从一个雕花门口滑开，露出向下的台阶。
You open your hand to the Fallen Shard and let it give..	你向坠落碎片张开手，让它给予..
..and $Shards.Shardfall.Draw Shards come away into you, warm, still singing.	..$Shards.Shardfall.Draw 枚碎片流进你体内，温暖，仍在歌唱。
The Shard's light is low: it has given what it will for now.	碎片的光很暗：它暂时已经给出了它愿意给的。
Its light pushes you back, cold as deep water.	它的光把你推了回来，冷得像深水。
The Sword of Valour slides from the stone as if it had been waiting for your hand. Quest complete: The Sword of Valour!	勇气之剑从石头里滑了出来，好像一直在等你的手。任务完成：勇气之剑！
The sword won't move. The guardian is watching you.	剑纹丝不动。守护者在看着你。
You drink the potion. It tastes of mint and mud.	你喝下药水。它尝起来有薄荷和泥巴的味道。
You drink the milk. Delicious!	你喝下牛奶。真好喝！
You drink the potion. Your arms tingle with strength!	你喝下药水。你的手臂因力量而发麻！
You drink the potion. It fizzes and glows!	你喝下药水。它嘶嘶冒泡，还在发光！
It burns all the way down, and the warmth spreads out to your toes.	它一路烧下去，暖意一直蔓延到你的脚趾。
An old man, more glass than man, turns from the great Shard, and his face lights up.\"One of the faithful! I remember your Shards in the Font, every one. Come in, come in. Have you come to sing with us?"	一个老人，玻璃多过血肉，从伟大的碎片前转过身来，脸上一下子亮了。\“一位信徒！我记得你倒进圣泉池里的碎片，每一块都记得。进来，进来。你是来和我们一起唱歌的吗？”
Walk closer.	走近些。
He opens his arms to you. He doesn't know why you've come.	他向你张开双臂。他不知道你为什么而来。
An old man, more glass than man, turns from the great Shard and squints at you.\"I don't know your face. Who let you in?" The choir's song changes. "Well. Everyone's welcome to hear it, at the end."	一个老人，玻璃多过血肉，从伟大的碎片前转过身来，眯着眼看你。\“我不认识你的脸。谁让你进来的？”唱诗班的歌声变了。“好吧。最后，每个人都欢迎来听。”
Draw your weapon.	拔出你的武器。
An old man, more glass than man, turns from the great Shard, and stops smiling.\"I know you. You're the one who'll not listen." The choir's song changes. "You will, though. Everyone does, at the end."	一个老人，玻璃多过血肉，从伟大的碎片前转过身来，不再微笑。\“我认识你。你就是那个不肯聆听的人。”唱诗班的歌声变了。“不过你会的。最后，每个人都会。”
You empty the contents of the bucket onto the floor.	你把桶里的东西倒在了地上。
You climb down into the sea cave. The sound of the waves goes on above you, and then it doesn't.	你爬下去进入海蚀洞。海浪声在你头顶继续着，然后就听不见了。
You lift the bedroll aside and climb down the hidden ladder, into the hobgoblins' lair.	你把铺盖卷挪到一边，顺着隐藏的梯子爬下去，进入大地精的巢穴。
Bedrolls, bones and the stink of hobgoblin. It's just an empty tent.	铺盖卷、骨头，还有大地精的臭味。这只是一顶空帐篷。
You duck into the cave. Somewhere ahead, water drips, and something squeaks.	你钻进洞穴。前方某处有水在滴，还有东西在吱吱叫。
You go down into the mine: gold glitters in the walls.	你下到矿井里：墙壁上有金子在闪光。
You duck into the tunnel. Water drips somewhere ahead, and something rustles.	你钻进隧道。前方某处有水在滴，还有东西在沙沙作响。
You duck into the tunnel. The fen's damp follows you in.	你钻进隧道。沼泽的湿气跟着你钻了进来。
'A stranger, found at the barrier's foot the winter it rose. The city buried him as its own.'	“一个陌生人，在屏障升起的那个冬天被发现倒在屏障脚下。这座城市把他当作自己人安葬了。”
'Agnes Reed. She mended every net on Muddlesey and swore at every gull.'	“Agnes Reed。她补过 Muddlesey 上的每一张网，骂过每一只海鸥。”
'Aldwin, shepherd of the downs. His flock outlived him by a winter.'	“Aldwin，高地的牧羊人。他的羊群比他多活了一个冬天。”
'Beatrix of the Harp. The Nobles' Quarter asked her to stop. She did not stop.'	“竖琴的 Beatrix。贵族区请她别弹了。她没有停。”
'Bram, smith. His hammer lies with him, so he may mend things on the other side.'	“Bram，铁匠。他的锤子和他葬在一起，好让他在另一边也能修东西。”
'Brother Edric, who kept this chapel fifty years and rang its bell every dawn.'	“Edric 修士，他看守这座礼拜堂五十年，每天黎明都敲响它的钟。”
'Captain Rusk of the Royal Guard. He held the east gate the day it shut.'	“皇家卫队的 Rusk 队长。城门关闭的那天，他守住了东门。”
'Cobb, digger of graves. He'd want you to know the soil here is good and soft.'	“Cobb，掘墓人。他会希望你知道这里的土又好又软。”
The stone has cracked clean in two. Someone has tied the halves together with rope.	石头干净利落地裂成了两半。有人用绳子把两半绑在了一起。
'Dame Ottilie, who taught three kings their letters and rapped all their knuckles.'	“Ottilie 夫人，她教三位国王识字，敲过他们每个人的指关节。”
'Elsa Moor. She walked the downs at night with a lantern, so the dead had light.'	“Elsa Moor。她夜里提着灯笼走在高地上，好让死者有光。”
'Fell at the ford, holding it.' No name: only a sword, scratched into the stone.	“在渡口倒下，守着它。”没有名字：只有一把剑，刻在石头上。
No name, no date. Fresh flowers lie in front of it, though no one lives near.	没有名字，没有日期。前面放着新鲜的花，尽管附近没人住。
'Gideon Sexton, bell-ringer. Deaf by forty; ringing still at eighty.'	“Gideon Sexton，敲钟人。四十岁就聋了；八十岁还在敲。”
'Godric Miller. The wind took his mill, and then the wind took him.'	“Godric Miller。风带走了他的磨坊，然后风带走了他。”
'Guildmaster Pryce. In life he kept the guild's accounts; in death, it is hoped, his temper.'	“行会会长 Pryce。生前他掌管行会的账目；死后，但愿他能管住自己的脾气。”
Two names in a ring of carved ivy: Hal and Bryony.	一圈雕刻的常春藤里有两个名字：Hal 和 Bryony。
'Hal, gatekeeper of the harbour. Twenty years he waited for a ship to let in.'	“Hal，港口的守门人。他等了二十年，等一艘可以放进来的船。”
'Hester Bell, midwife. Half Muddlesey came into the world through her hands.'	“Hester Bell，接生婆。Muddlesey 上一半的人是经她的手来到这个世界的。”
'I loved these hills. Bury me where I can see them.' The stone faces the sunrise.	“我爱这些山丘。把我葬在能看见它们的地方。”这块石头朝着日出。
'Hobb Vintner, who sold the city's worst wine for fifty years and is greatly missed.'	“Hobb Vintner，他卖了五十年全城最难喝的酒，人们非常怀念他。”
'Lady Isolde Ashby. She left her fortune to the town and her temper to her son.'	“Isolde Ashby 夫人。她把财产留给了小镇，把脾气留给了她儿子。”
'Jonas, mason, who cut every stone in this row and left his own till last. It shows.'	“Jonas，石匠，这一排的每一块石头都是他凿的，他把自己的留到了最后。看得出来。”
A small stone, carved with a hound. The old kings buried their dogs with honour.	一块小石头，刻着一只猎犬。古代的国王们以荣誉安葬他们的狗。
Runes, worn almost smooth: '...the king's shieldman, who stood at the door.'	符文，几乎被磨平了：“……国王的持盾人，他守在门口。”
A spear is carved on it. Beneath, in the old letters: 'He did not run.'	上面刻着一支矛。下面，用古老的文字写着：“他没有逃跑。”
'Here waits the king's steward, until his king has need of him.' The stone is cold to the touch.	“国王的总管在此等候，直到他的国王需要他。”石头摸上去冰冷。
'Lady Amice Thorne. She gave the orchard to the people, and the people have never returned it.'	“Amice Thorne 夫人。她把果园给了人民，而人民从未归还。”
'Little Wren, aged six. Gone to chase the butterflies.'	“小 Wren，六岁。追蝴蝶去了。”
'Marta of the Quays. She could gut a herring at forty paces.'	“码头的 Marta。她能在四十步外剖开一条鲱鱼。”
'Martha Lark, who sang at every burial in this yard but her own.'	“Martha Lark，她在这片墓地的每一场葬礼上都唱过歌，除了她自己的。”
'Mary Croft, beloved mother. She baked the best pies on Muddlesey.'	“Mary Croft，挚爱的母亲。她烤的馅饼是 Muddlesey 上最好的。”
So mossed over you can't read it. A wren has nested on top.	长满了苔藓，读不出来了。一只鹪鹩在上面筑了巢。
'Ned Pike, ferryman. Forty years across the strait, and never lost a passenger.'	“Ned Pike，渡船夫。在海峡上来回四十年，从没丢过一位乘客。”
A bow is carved on it, and one word in the old tongue: 'Steady.'	上面刻着一把弓，还有一个古语单词：“稳住。”
'Old Godwin, sexton of this yard. He mowed it for forty years and asks you to keep off the grass.'	“老 Godwin，这片墓地的教堂司事。他在这里割了四十年草，请你不要践踏草坪。”
'Old Wat, who lived alone and liked it. Leave him be.'	“老 Wat，他一个人住，而且喜欢这样。别打扰他。”
'Osric Raven, first gravekeeper of the Barrowdowns. He dug his own, and dug it well.'	“Osric Raven，Barrowdowns 的第一位守墓人。他给自己挖了墓，挖得很好。”
A crow is carved where a name should be: the mark of Crowgate, a town long gone.	本该刻名字的地方刻着一只渡鸦：Crowgate 的标记，一座早已消失的小镇。
'Here lies one who walked every road on the downs, and rests at last beside this one.'	“这里安息着一个走遍高地每一条路的人，最后在这条路旁安息。”
'The Rowan twins. Born together, buried together. One stone was always enough.'	“Rowan 双胞胎。一起出生，一起下葬。一块石头总是够用的。”
'Sister Oona, who nursed the city through the Grey Winter and caught it last of all.'	“Oona 修女，她在灰色寒冬中照顾全城的人，最后一个染上了病。”
'A traveller, name unknown, found on the downs in the snow. The town paid for this stone.'	“一位旅人，姓名不详，在高地的雪中被发现。小镇为这块石头付了钱。”
'Old Tam, thatcher. He built Rose Cottage the year the great storm took the pier.'	“老 Tam，盖茅草屋顶的人。大风暴卷走码头的那一年，他建了玫瑰小屋。”
'Maud and Mabel Fenn. They never agreed on anything, so they have a stone each, side by side.'	“Maud 和 Mabel Fenn。她们从来没在任何事上意见一致过，所以她们一人一块石头，并排立着。”
Three names, sisters by the look of it, and one date for all three. A hard winter.	三个名字，看样子是姐妹，三个人只有一个日期。一个难熬的冬天。
'Tobin, lamplighter. Every lamp on the avenue, every night, for sixty-one years.'	“Tobin，点灯人。大道上的每一盏灯，每一夜，六十一年。”
'Here lies Old Tom Barrow. He went to sea and the sea kept him.'	“这里安息着老 Tom Barrow。他出了海，大海留下了他。”
'Wilkin, baker. Rose early. Rises no more.' Somebody thought that was funny, and paid extra.	“Wilkin，面包师。起得很早。再也起不来了。”有人觉得这很好笑，还多付了钱。
'Will Hollis, shepherd.' A little stone dog is carved at the foot of it.	“Will Hollis，牧羊人。”石头脚下刻着一只小石狗。
A tiny stone. A wooden horse lies in front of it, bleached white by the sun.	一块很小的石头。前面放着一匹木马，被太阳晒得发白。
The name has worn away. The earth in front of it looks... disturbed.	名字已经磨掉了。它前面的泥土看起来……被翻动过。
'Young Perrin, aged seven. He fed the fountain's fish, of which there were none.'	“小 Perrin，七岁。他喂过喷泉里的鱼，而喷泉里一条鱼也没有。”
The plinth has been cut fresh, and it isn't anyone yet. Next year's crown is won in the arena, in the winter.	底座是新刻的，上面还没有任何人。明年的王冠要在冬天的竞技场里赢得。
The plinth says: $World.Champion, the heir who won the year's crown.	底座上写着：$World.Champion，赢得本年王冠的继承人。
The King's bedchamber: a great bed, a cold hearth, and a window over the boulevard to the east gate.	国王的卧室：一张大床，一个冷炉膛，一扇窗户朝着通往东门的林荫大道。
A trader in a sun-bleached robe. Every time you look away, he's standing a little further off.	一个穿着被太阳晒褪色长袍的商人。你每次移开视线，他就站得更远一点。
'Mary. Beloved mother.' The earth is still fresh, and the flowers on it.	“Mary。挚爱的母亲。”泥土还是新的，上面的花也是。
Queen Mary's bed, made up fresh every morning for twenty years. Nobody has slept in it.	Mary 王后的床，二十年来每天早上都重新铺好。没有人睡过。
A royal cot, carved with little dragons. Every heir of Galaith slept in it, for a while.	一张皇家婴儿床，刻着小龙。Galaith 的每一位继承人都在里面睡过一阵子。
'Alaric, firstborn of Queen Isaude. Taken in his nineteenth year.' There is nobody under this stone.	“Alaric，Isaude 王后的长子。在他十九岁那年被带走。”这块石头下面没有人。
'Bran, firstborn of King Edmund. He was eleven.' Somebody has left a wooden sword against it.	“Bran，Edmund 国王的长子。他十一岁。”有人在旁边靠了一把木剑。
'Corin, firstborn of King Aldred. Taken.' Just that. The mason ran out of things to say.	“Corin，Aldred 国王的长子。被带走了。”就这些。石匠没话可说了。
'Odile, firstborn of King Harald. She went up the pass on her own two feet.' Nobody under this one either.	“Odile，Harald 国王的长女。她用自己的双脚走上了山口。”这块下面也没有人。
'Sunniva, firstborn of Queen Ysolde. Taken on her wedding morning.' A ring of dried flowers, kept new.	“Sunniva，Ysolde 王后的长女。在她婚礼的早晨被带走。”一圈干花，一直换着新的。
A small new stone, and nothing cut in it yet. The grass round it is the shortest in the yard.	一块新的小石头，上面还什么都没刻。它周围的草是整片墓地里最短的。
The farmer waves his hat at you: "Come back any time those goblins do!"	农夫冲你挥着帽子：“那些哥布林什么时候再来，你就什么时候再来！”
The Puddleford Road: tell the Mayor the goblins are beaten.	Puddleford 之路：告诉镇长哥布林被打败了。
The cat eats the raw herring in neat little bites, then washes its whiskers.	猫小口小口地把生鲱鱼吃完，然后洗了洗胡子。
The cat eats the raw trout in neat little bites, then washes its whiskers.	猫小口小口地把生鳟鱼吃完，然后洗了洗胡子。
The cat eats the raw salmon in neat little bites, then washes its whiskers.	猫小口小口地把生鲑鱼吃完，然后洗了洗胡子。
The cat eats the cooked herring in neat little bites, then washes its whiskers.	猫小口小口地把熟鲱鱼吃完，然后洗了洗胡子。
The cat eats the cooked trout in neat little bites, then washes its whiskers.	猫小口小口地把熟鳟鱼吃完，然后洗了洗胡子。
The cat eats the cooked salmon in neat little bites, then washes its whiskers.	猫小口小口地把熟鲑鱼吃完，然后洗了洗胡子。
The cat eats the raw poultry in neat little bites, then washes its whiskers.	猫小口小口地把生禽肉吃完，然后洗了洗胡子。

Your hand on the seam, you feel it: this is where the dragon was cut. Every Shard in the world leans toward this place, and the dragon's last words come back to you: keep it shut, or wear what is left of it.	你的手放在矿脉上，你感觉到了：这就是龙被切开的地方。世界上的每一块碎片都倾向这里，龙最后的话又回到你耳边：守住它，或者穿上它剩下的部分。
Give your blood to hold the Cut shut: the Binding.	献出你的血，让切口保持闭合：缚印。
Set its piece in the Crown, and wear it.	把它的一块镶进王冠，戴上它。
Break the Seam. Unmake the Cut.	打破缝隙。消除切口。
You raise your hand to break it, and the Whisper at your neck cries out in your mother's voice: don't, $Player.Son, don't. Your hand won't do it.	你举起手要打破它，你脖子上的低语用你母亲的声音哭喊起来：别，$Player.Son，别。你的手做不到。
You take your hand away. The Cut stays shut, and the Binding holds, as it always has: the Crown's to keep now, and yours.	你把手拿开了。切口保持闭合，缚印像一直以来那样维持着：现在它归王冠守护，也归你。
The seam flares, and something warm settles into your palm: a piece of the dragon, set in a ring. It hums, faintly, and it knows your name. It is the Crown's to keep now, and yours.	矿脉闪耀起来，某个温暖的东西落进你的掌心：一块龙的碎片，镶在一枚戒指里。它微微嗡鸣，它知道你的名字。现在它归王冠守护，也归你。
You do not give your blood and you do not take the piece. You press both hands to the seam and say no, and the old cut breaks open like a held breath let go. Somewhere far below, something huge turns over in its sleep, and begins, very slowly, to fade. The storms will be calmer now, and the scraps will not come for you. The seam goes dark.	你没有献出你的血，也没有拿走那一块。你把双手按在矿脉上，说了不，那道古老的切口像一口憋住的气被放开一样裂开了。下面很远的地方，某个巨大的东西在睡梦中翻了个身，然后开始非常缓慢地消散。风暴会平静一些了，那些残片也不会再来找你了。矿脉暗了下去。
The seam is dark and still. Whatever lay under it is a long way down now, and going quiet.	矿脉黑暗而静止。不管它下面躺着的是什么，现在都已经在很深的下面，渐渐安静了。
The seam glows dimly: its Shards haven't grown back yet.	矿脉发着微光：它的碎片还没有长回来。
The seam is the Crown's.	矿脉归王冠所有。

You kneel and pray a while, and the hall is very still.	你跪下祈祷了一会儿，大厅里非常安静。
Click.	咔哒。
A bucket of icy water falls on your head from nowhere at all.	一桶冰水凭空浇在了你头上。
Confetti! A voice booms: 'CONGRATULATIONS: you are the one thousandth person to press the button!' A Shard drops into your hand.	彩纸！一个声音轰鸣道：“恭喜：你是第一千个按下这个按钮的人！”一枚碎片落进了你手里。
A goose appears. It looks at you. You look at it. It honks.	一只鹅出现了。它看着你。你看着它。它嘎嘎叫。
Here's what keeps me up at night. A scroll is ink and vellum: how does a Shard know to become a bolt of fire because some words told it to? The Shards remember shapes, I think. Bring me something that remembers its own: the core of a Shard Remnant, one that rose.	这就是让我夜里睡不着的事。卷轴就是墨水和羊皮纸：一块碎片怎么会因为几句话叫它变成火焰弹，它就变成火焰弹了呢？我想，碎片记得形状。给我带来一个记得它自己形状的东西：一个碎片残迹的核心，一个升起过的残迹。
I'll bring you one.	我会给你带一个来。
Quest started: The Spellwright.	任务开始：法术工匠。
Look at it: it's still holding the shape of the beast it was. Shape is the whole secret.\Now you. Pick up twenty more Shards where beasts fall, and pay attention as you do: what the magic feels like as it comes into you. Then tell me.	看看它：它仍然保持着它曾经是的那头野兽的形状。形状就是全部的秘密。\现在轮到你了。在野兽倒下的地方再捡起二十枚碎片，捡的时候要留心：魔法进入你体内时是什么感觉。然后告诉我。
A Remnant's core: they rise from a beast's magic, now and then, when it dies. Kill one and it leaves its core behind.	残迹的核心：野兽死时，它的魔法偶尔会升起成残迹。杀死一个，它就会留下它的核心。
I'll find one.	我会找到一个的。
Cold, then warm, then a sort of... leaning? Yes! That's the shape, wanting to be something.\You've felt it, so you can write it. The lectern by the throne: a page and Shard-ink. Choose what your spell does, how hard, how far, and the Shards will remember. Yours, no one else's.	先是冷，然后是暖，然后是一种……倾斜？对！那就是形状，想要成为某种东西。\你感觉到了，所以你能把它写下来。王座旁边的读经台：一页纸和碎片墨水。选择你的法术做什么、多强、多远，碎片就会记住。是你的，不是别人的。
Thank you, Magister.	谢谢你，大法师。
Quest complete: The Spellwright! Write a spell of your own at the Spellwright's Lectern.	任务完成：法术工匠！在法术工匠的读经台上写一个你自己的法术。
Twenty Shards from fallen beasts, and mind how they feel. You've gathered $Attr.RemnantsAbsorbed in all.	从倒下的野兽身上取二十枚碎片，要留心它们的感觉。你一共已经收集了 $Attr.RemnantsAbsorbed 枚。
I'll keep at it.	我会继续的。
The lectern's there whenever you want to write your spell again. Change it as you grow: a spell's a living thing.	你什么时候想重写你的法术，读经台都在那里。随着你的成长去改变它吧：法术是活的东西。
Ask me again when you've seen more of the world.	等你见识了更多的世界再来问我吧。
Goodbye.	再见。
The light slides off between your fingers, the way a fish does.	那道光从你指缝间溜走了，像鱼一样。
You reach again. It waits until you've nearly got it. You'd swear it's enjoying this.	你又伸手去抓。它等到你快抓住时才躲开。你敢发誓它很享受这样。
Your hand closes on something cold, and it's gone, up your arm and into you. The other lights back off and look at you differently.	你的手握住了某个冰冷的东西，它消失了，顺着你的手臂进入你体内。其他的光退开了，用不同的眼光看着你。
The lights keep just out of reach now. They've learned.	现在那些光总是刚好待在够不着的地方。它们学乖了。
Iron bars, set deep in the rock, too close for your hand. Behind them: a gold amulet!	铁栏，深深嵌在岩石里，间隙太窄，你的手伸不进去。铁栏后面：一枚金护身符！
Footsteps echo behind you: Brother Aldous, a lantern in his hand.	你身后响起脚步声的回音：Aldous 修士，手里提着一盏灯笼。
I said I'd not be far behind. Look here first.\He holds his lantern low: three bright splinters on the floor.\Something died here, a rat or a bat, and left its magic behind: Shards. I'd say a few words over it, but it was a rat.\Pick them up, and they're yours to spend. Every beast leaves its magic where it falls. And now and then, the magic won't lie still. The old books say every Shard is a scrap of something far greater, cut down long ago, and that scraps like that are hungry: take them in before they take root in you.	我说过我不会落后太远。先看看这里。\他把灯笼放低：地上有三块明亮的碎片。\有什么东西死在了这里，一只老鼠或一只蝙蝠，留下了它的魔法：碎片。我本想说几句悼词，可那是只老鼠。\把它们捡起来，它们就归你使用了。每只野兽都会把魔法留在它倒下的地方。而偶尔，魔法不肯安静下来。古书上说，每一块碎片都是某个大得多的东西的残片，很久以前被砍倒了，而那样的残片是饥饿的：趁它们还没在你体内扎根，就把它们吸收掉。
I'll remember.	我会记住的。
Now, the amulet. Your mother put it there, and the bars after it, so that no thief could ever take it. Nor anyone else, as it turns out. But a hand that isn't a hand can.\Here: a scroll of Grasp. Read it, and the spell is yours for good; each casting drinks a couple of Shards. Cast it at the bars, and an unseen hand will bring the amulet to you.	现在，护身符。你母亲把它放在那里，然后装上了铁栏，这样就没有小偷能拿走它。结果别人也都拿不走。但一只不是手的手可以。\给：一张抓取卷轴。读了它，这个法术就永远归你了；每次施放会消耗几枚碎片。对着铁栏施放它，一只看不见的手就会把护身符带给你。

THE LAST WILL AND TESTAMENT OF MARY\My beloved $Player.Son,\We have lived in poverty on this island since you were six months old; this is not what your life should have been like. You are the true heir of Brambleton, and its riches, lands and people are yours to protect.	Mary 的遗嘱\我亲爱的 $Player.Son：\从你六个月大起，我们就在这座岛上过着贫穷的日子；你的人生本不该是这个样子。你是 Brambleton 真正的继承人，它的财富、土地和人民都归你守护。
Read on.	继续读。
I could never tell you who we were while I lived: it was safer that you didn't know. I didn't leave Galaith with nothing. What I carried away, I hid, and the people of this island have kept my secrets for you. When you are ready, they will see that you find it.	我活着的时候永远不能告诉你我们是谁：你不知道会更安全。我离开 Galaith 时并不是两手空空。我带走的东西，我藏起来了，这座岛上的人一直为你守着我的秘密。等你准备好了，他们会让你找到它。
Your father, the king, still waits behind the walls of Galaith, and he doesn't know that I am gone. Go to him. The city has been shut since the day you were born, and I am the reason why. One day you will understand.\Be careful, and tell your father I never stopped loving him.\Mary	你的父亲，国王，仍在 Galaith 的城墙后面等待，他不知道我已经走了。去找他吧。从你出生那天起，这座城就关闭了，而原因就是我。总有一天你会明白的。\要小心，告诉你父亲，我从未停止爱他。\Mary
Fold the letter.	把信折起来。
Quest complete: The Last Will.	任务完成：遗嘱。
The door to your house is unlocked now, and Muddlesey lies before you.	你家的门现在开了，Muddlesey 就在你面前。
Edwin writes Muddlesey's wills and deeds, and he's never once asked to see mine. Maren checks the baby's breathing whenever she passes, as if she can tell something's been promised. Nobody here bows to me. I could stay for ever.	Edwin 给 Muddlesey 写遗嘱和地契，他一次也没要求看我的。Maren 每次路过都会看看宝宝的呼吸，好像她能看出有什么被许诺了出去。这里没有人向我鞠躬。我可以永远待下去。
The old men on the quay say the first king set a watch under the sea north of Puddleford: the Glass Wardens, keeping something asleep. Nobody's seen one in a hundred years. What did a king need watched as badly as that?	码头上的老人们说，第一位国王在 Puddleford 北边的海底下设了一支守卫：玻璃守卫，让某个东西一直沉睡。一百年来没人见过他们中的任何一个。一位国王需要什么东西被看守得这么紧？
I found the Pact in the Court's oldest book, in the first king's own hand: a firstborn of his line each generation, and the dragon keeps its peace. I read it three times. Then I went home and held my baby till morning.	我在巫师庭最古老的书里找到了契约，是第一位国王的亲笔：他的血脉每一代献出一个长子，龙就保持安宁。我读了三遍。然后我回了家，抱着我的宝宝直到天亮。
The sun priests sang it as a triumph: the first king cut the dragon down to something small enough to bind. Down, they sing. Never dead. So where did the rest of it go?	太阳祭司们把它当作一场胜利来歌颂：第一位国王把龙砍小到足以缚住。砍小，他们唱道。从来不是死去。那它其余的部分去哪儿了？
I held the Pact's seal tonight, for as long as I dared. It's a ring, and it's warm, like something asleep. It knows when you're afraid.	今晚我拿着契约的印章，拿了我敢拿的那么久。它是一枚戒指，是温暖的，像某个沉睡的东西。你害怕时它知道。
They'll come for my child. So I'll run, and I'll keep running, and what I took from the Seam will stay where no king ever thinks to look: somewhere small, and still, and full of sky.	他们会来抓我的孩子。所以我要逃，一直逃，我从缝隙里拿走的东西会留在一个国王永远想不到去找的地方：一个小小的、静止的、满是天空的地方。
I was crowned in this hall, and I left it by the kitchen door with you asleep in a basket of washing. If you're reading this, the wall is down and you came home. Forgive me. Look for still water on Muddlesey, where the sky looks up at itself.	我在这座大厅里加冕，又从厨房门离开了它，你睡在一篮待洗的衣服里。如果你在读这个，那屏障已经倒下，你回家了。原谅我。在 Muddlesey 上找静止的水，在天空仰望自己的地方。

Jimmy knows where.	你母亲的笔迹，小而急促，写在一张巫师庭的纸片上。墨水泛着蓝光：\天鹅绒爪胡须灵，\借 Jimmy 眼看屋形。\下面写着：给我的小家伙，到时候看。Jimmy 知道在哪儿。

Your mother's hand. Seven of them, wherever she went. Lay them edge to edge and it's a map. The edges meet at a little round shape in Muddlesey's north, and she's written one word there: still.	你母亲的笔迹。一共七张，她走到哪里就留到哪里。把它们边对边拼起来，就是一张地图。边缘交汇在 Muddlesey 北部一个小小的圆形上，她在那里写了一个词：静。
Mary's Notes\Folded small, in your mother's hand:\They told me at the Court that the Shards are only magic, splintered. They are not. They are scraps of the dragon, thrown off when the first king cut it down to a size he could bind. Most of it lies asleep beyond the world, and every scrap is hungry to grow; the Pact keeps the dragon quiet with the blood of every firstborn heir.\I will not pay it with my child. If the Cut could be kept shut some other way, or the dragon ended, the price would end. I go to find out how.\The place it was cut is under the mountain behind the city. Remember the Seam.	Mary 的笔记\折得小小的，是你母亲的笔迹：\他们在巫师庭告诉我，碎片只是碎裂的魔法。不是的。它们是龙的残片，是第一位国王把它砍小到可以缚住时甩落的。它的大部分在世界之外沉睡，每一块残片都饥饿地想要生长；契约用每一位长子继承人的血让龙保持安静。\我不会用我的孩子来偿还它。如果切口能用别的办法保持闭合，或者龙能被终结，代价就会终结。我去找出办法。\它被切开的地方在城市后面的山下。记住缝隙。

ODILE'S LOG, in an oilskin\Forty years I've cooked for the Wardens. Bread on the first day of the week, fish the rest. The cistern hums. The lake hums louder.\(The writing gets bigger, page by page.) It's warm in the water. I don't need the lamp. I hardly need to breathe.\(The last pages, in letters as tall as your hand.) THEY HEAR IT TOO, THE SAILORS. THEY COME DOWN THROUGH THE ROCK TO IT. I ONLY KEEP THEM FROM GOING TO IT. I'M SORRY. I'M SORRY ABOUT THE BOAT.	Odile 的日志，裹在油布里\我给守卫们做了四十年饭。每周第一天做面包，其余几天做鱼。蓄水池在嗡鸣。湖嗡鸣得更响。\（字迹一页比一页大。）水里很暖。我不需要灯了。我几乎不需要呼吸了。\（最后几页，字有你的手那么高。）他们也听到了，那些水手。他们穿过岩石下来找它。我只是不让他们走到它那里去。对不起。我很抱歉那条船的事。
THE GLASS WARDENS, a true history, by Pell Varrow\The first king set the Wardens to keep the Shard that fell into the sea. Their orders, kept under the chapel's altar, were to keep it asleep, and never let it be joined to the rest of itself.\For four hundred years they listened to it and never answered. Then Marshal Corvane asked it a question. Half the Order followed him; the cook walked into the lake; the last of them, Ysolde, kept the watch alone.\I don't know yet what 'the rest of itself' is. I'm not sure I want to.	玻璃守卫，一部真实的历史，Pell Varrow 著\第一位国王派守卫们看守那块落进海里的碎片。他们的命令藏在礼拜堂的祭坛下面，是让它保持沉睡，永远不让它与它自身的其余部分结合。\四百年来，他们聆听它，却从不回答。后来 Corvane 元帅问了它一个问题。一半的骑士团追随了他；厨娘走进了湖里；他们中的最后一人，Ysolde，独自守望。\我还不知道“它自身的其余部分”是什么。我不确定我想知道。

A SHARDGATE, by Bert, greatest wizard of the age.\In the old ring of standing stones on the hill north of the Court: bind the stones with four bars of silver, set two sapphires in the runes, and at the ring's heart lay a Remnant's core.\Then give it $Shards.Shardgate Shards of your own. Anything can slip onto the realm's calm rim; to pierce its core the ring must be steadied, so wake $Shards.Shardgate.Stones of Brambleton's shardstones first, and know your Shardcraft ($Shards.Shardgate.Level at least).\Then step through. Mind the Warden.	碎片之门，当代最伟大的巫师 Bert 著。\在巫师庭北边山上那圈古老的立石里：用四根银锭把石头连起来，把两颗蓝宝石嵌进符文，在石环的中心放上一颗残迹核心。\然后献给它 $Shards.Shardgate 枚你自己的碎片。任何东西都能滑到界域平静的边缘；要刺穿它的核心，石环必须被稳住，所以要先唤醒 Brambleton 的 $Shards.Shardgate.Stones 块碎片石，还要懂碎片术（至少 $Shards.Shardgate.Level 级）。\然后走进去。小心守护者。

WARDENS' JOURNAL: the hand of Ysolde\Year 211 of the watch. Minutes. Present: all. The Shard is quiet. Marshal Corvane says it sleeps. Brother Hale says it never sleeps, it only waits. Moved that Brother Hale scrub the cistern for saying so: carried.	守卫日志：Ysolde 的笔迹\守望第 211 年。会议记录。出席：全体。碎片很安静。Corvane 元帅说它在沉睡。Hale 修士说它从不沉睡，它只是在等待。动议：因为这么说，罚 Hale 修士去刷蓄水池。通过。
That's all nine of the Wardens' journals: back to Pell.	九本守卫日志都齐了：回到 Pell 那里。
WARDENS' JOURNAL: the hand of Brother Hale\It grows. We have measured it every year since the first: a hair a year, always. Marshal Corvane says it's the rock settling. Rock doesn't settle upwards.\(Struck from the minutes, at the Marshal's request.)	守卫日志：Hale 修士的笔迹\它在长大。从第一年起我们每年都量它：一年一根头发丝，一直如此。Corvane 元帅说那是岩石在沉降。岩石可不会往上沉降。\（应元帅要求，已从会议记录中删除。）
WARDENS' JOURNAL: the hand of Marshal Corvane\I have heard it now. It isn't asking to be used. It's asking to be listened to, and nobody has, in four hundred years of standing guard over it with our ears stopped.\The others will understand. I'll make them listen.	守卫日志：Corvane 元帅的笔迹\我现在听到它了。它不是在要求被使用。它是在要求被聆听，而四百年来没有人这么做过，我们塞着耳朵站岗看守它。\其他人会理解的。我会让他们听的。
WARDENS' JOURNAL: the hand of Odile Brask, cook\Year 302. The water in the cistern hums when I draw it. Not loud. Just a hum, like somebody in the next room. Some days it's my mother's songs, and my mother's been dead thirty years.\I've stopped telling the Marshal. He only says to drink less of it, and that the soup wants salt. It doesn't.	守卫日志：厨娘 Odile Brask 的笔迹\第 302 年。我打水时，蓄水池里的水在嗡鸣。不响。只是一阵嗡鸣，像隔壁房间里有人。有些日子那是我母亲的歌，而我母亲已经死了三十年了。\我不再告诉元帅了。他只会说少喝点，还说汤里盐不够。盐够了。
WARDENS' JOURNAL: the hand of Brother Hale\Year 344. Measured it again from the rim, though the meeting asked me not to. Two hairs this year, three in the summer. It's growing faster.\When I put the rule against it, something sighed. Not the wind: there's no wind down here. As if it had been waiting a long time for somebody to notice, and was glad.	守卫日志：Hale 修士的笔迹\第 344 年。会议不让我量，我还是又从边缘量了一次。今年两根头发丝，夏天三根。它长得更快了。\我把尺子贴上去时，有什么东西叹了口气。不是风：这下面没有风。好像它等了很久，等有人注意到，而且很高兴。
WARDENS' JOURNAL: the hand of Marshal Corvane\I asked it what it wanted. Four hundred years, and nobody had asked. It said: to be whole.\It knows my name. It knew my mother's. It only wants to be whole, and it's so small, and it's been alone so long. What sort of guard keeps a thing from its own body?	守卫日志：Corvane 元帅的笔迹\我问它想要什么。四百年了，没有人问过。它说：想要完整。\它知道我的名字。它知道我母亲的名字。它只是想要完整，它那么小，又孤独了那么久。什么样的守卫会让一个东西远离它自己的身体？
WARDENS' JOURNAL: the hand of Ysolde\The Order has split. Half the watch stand with Corvane, ears open. The rest of us keep the first king's orders, the ones under the altar: keep it asleep, and never, ever let it be joined to the rest of itself.\The rest of itself. I asked the Marshal once what that meant. He didn't know. He knows now, and I think that's worse.\(The split took a vote. The vote took eleven days.)	守卫日志：Ysolde 的笔迹\骑士团分裂了。一半的守卫站在 Corvane 一边，耳朵敞开着。我们其余的人遵守第一位国王的命令，祭坛下面的那些：让它保持沉睡，永远、永远不让它与它自身的其余部分结合。\它自身的其余部分。我曾经问过元帅那是什么意思。他不知道。他现在知道了，我觉得这更糟。\（分裂是投票决定的。投票花了十一天。）
WARDENS' JOURNAL: the hand of Odile Brask, cook\I'm going in. Into the lake, I mean. The humming's loudest there, and I want to see what's singing, and I'm too old to be frightened of water.\Whoever finds this: mind the sailors. It calls them too. I can hear it calling them, up through the rock, every night.\There's a stew on. Somebody stir it.	守卫日志：厨娘 Odile Brask 的笔迹\我要进去了。我是说，进湖里。那里的嗡鸣声最响，我想看看是什么在唱歌，而且我老得不会怕水了。\不管谁找到这个：小心那些水手。它也在呼唤他们。我能听到它在呼唤他们，每天夜里，穿过岩石往上传。\炖菜在火上。谁来搅一搅。
WARDENS' JOURNAL: the hand of Ysolde, the last\I'm the last who never answered it. (We meant to answer it once, all of us together. We spent forty years on the wording, and by then we knew better.) If you're warm, and reading this, then listen: you can hear it, and you can learn from it, but never answer. Answering is how it gets in.\There's a room under the Reliquary where we listened in turns. The four chimes keep it shut. The singing stone in the galleries remembers their order; it changes with the year, the way the old song does. I've built a cairn here, so somebody would stop.	守卫日志：最后的 Ysolde 的笔迹\我是最后一个从未回答过它的人。（有一次我们想一起回答。措辞商量了四十年，到那时我们都懂事了。）如果你是温暖的，正在读这个，那就听着：你能听到它，你能从它那里学到东西，但永远别回答。回答就是它进来的方式。\圣物堂下面有一个房间，我们在那里轮流聆听。四声钟鸣让它保持关闭。长廊里那块会唱歌的石头记得它们的顺序；它随着年份变化，就像那首老歌一样。我在这里堆了一座石冢，好让有人停下来。

SOUTH ROW\Houses of Wildwood.	南排\Wildwood 的住宅。
WILDWOOD\A hamlet.	Wildwood\一座小村庄。
DONNNG... The bell's voice rolls out over Muddlesey.	当——嗡……钟声在 Muddlesey 上空回荡。
The bell speaks once, low and green, and every frog in the marsh stops to listen.	钟响了一声，低沉而青绿，沼泽里的每一只青蛙都停下来聆听。
Out in the water something answers, a little flat, and what the ringing shook loose comes up out of the mud at your feet.	水里有什么东西回应了，有点走调，被钟声震松的东西从你脚边的泥里冒了出来。
The frogs start up again, louder, as if they've been told.	青蛙们又叫了起来，更响了，好像它们被告知了什么。
You row out to the sandbar.	你划船到沙洲上。
You row back to the cove.	你划船回到小海湾。
Under the straw, still warm: a pair of round gold spectacles. Pip's!	稻草下面，还是温的：一副圆圆的金边眼镜。是 Pip 的！
Straw, feathers and one warm egg. The hen watches you closely.	稻草、羽毛和一个温热的蛋。母鸡紧紧地盯着你。
Captain Rook casts off, and the coast falls away behind you..	Rook 船长解开缆绳，海岸在你身后渐渐远去..
..and Dragon Island rises out of the sea, smoking. Something roars inland.	..龙岛从海中升起，冒着烟。内陆有什么东西在咆哮。
Quest started: The Island of Dragons.	任务开始：龙之岛。
You rummage through the barrel..	你在木桶里翻找..
..only old candle ends and dust.	..只有旧蜡烛头和灰尘。
You rummage through the crate..	你在板条箱里翻找..
..mouldy hymn books, and a mouse who has read them all.	..发霉的赞美诗集，还有一只把它们全读完了的老鼠。
A clue! A big white feather. Not a duck's. Bigger. Angrier. ($Attr.TrouserClues of 3)	一条线索！一根白色的大羽毛。不是鸭子的。更大。更凶。（$Attr.TrouserClues / 3）
A big white feather. Not a duck's. Bigger. Angrier.	一根白色的大羽毛。不是鸭子的。更大。更凶。
A clue! Webbed footprints, heading off towards the duck pond with great purpose. ($Attr.TrouserClues of 3)	一条线索！带蹼的脚印，非常坚定地朝鸭塘走去。（$Attr.TrouserClues / 3）
Webbed footprints, heading off towards the duck pond with great purpose.	带蹼的脚印，非常坚定地朝鸭塘走去。
A clue! The washing line's been snapped, and there's a single honk-shaped dent in the post. ($Attr.TrouserClues of 3)	一条线索！晾衣绳被扯断了，柱子上有一个嘎嘎叫形状的凹痕。（$Attr.TrouserClues / 3）
The washing line's been snapped, and there's a single honk-shaped dent in the post.	晾衣绳被扯断了，柱子上有一个嘎嘎叫形状的凹痕。
The barrel's false bottom lifts out: somebody's share of the takings, kept from the others.	木桶的假底被掀了出来：某个人的那份赃款，瞒着其他人藏起来的。
You've found what was here already.	你已经找到这里的东西了。
You have the pearl. Only a frog can give it back.	你拿到了珍珠。只有一只青蛙能把它还回去。
Under the fish bones, in the very middle of the nest, something round and warm.	在鱼骨头下面，就在巢的正中间，有个圆圆的、温暖的东西。
Sticks, fish bones, and a smell. Nothing you want.	树枝、鱼骨头，还有一股气味。没有你想要的东西。
A heron's nest is no place for a frog. You can't climb, and you'd rather not.	苍鹭的巢可不是青蛙待的地方。你不会爬，也不太想爬。
Deep in the hollow stump: a squirrel's winter store, and under it somebody's purse, green with age. The squirrel was saving it for something.	空心树桩的深处：一只松鼠的过冬储藏，下面是某人的钱袋，已经旧得发绿了。松鼠本来打算拿它做点什么的。
..rotten cabbages, one sock and hobgoblin junk.	..烂卷心菜、一只袜子和大地精的破烂。
..and stuffed down the side, where you missed it before: a scrap of paper, folded small.	..还有塞在侧面、你之前漏掉的：一张折得小小的纸片。
..rags and bones, nothing more.	..破布和骨头，别的什么都没有。
..and there, under the rags: a brass house key. Tobias's! Wrapped round it, a scrap of paper, folded small.	..就在那里，破布下面：一把黄铜房门钥匙。是 Tobias 的！上面裹着一张折得小小的纸片。
..and there, under the rags: a brass house key. Tobias's!	..就在那里，破布下面：一把黄铜房门钥匙。是 Tobias 的！
You prise up the loose board..	你撬起松动的木板..
..and underneath, wrapped in oilcloth: papers in your mother's hand, under a crowned seal.	..下面裹在油布里的：是你母亲亲笔写的文书，盖着王冠印章。
Just a floorboard, nailed down firm.	只是一块地板，钉得很牢。
The board's loose, but this is still Tobias's house: not yours to go prising up its floor.	木板是松的，但这仍是 Tobias 的房子：不该由你去撬它的地板。
Quest complete: Mary's Notes.	任务完成：Mary 的笔记。
Wrapped in oilcloth, under a stone at the water's edge: a sliver of dark glass, and one line. It isn't a weapon and it isn't a gift. It's a scrap of the dragon, taken from the Seam, and it's still growing. It will tell you what you want to hear. Don't answer it.	裹在油布里，压在水边的一块石头下：一片深色的玻璃，还有一行字。它不是武器，也不是礼物。它是龙的一块残片，从缝隙里拿出来的，它还在生长。它会告诉你你想听的话。别回答它。
You close your hand.	你合上了手。
Mud, and a very still sky.	泥巴，还有一片非常静止的天空。
You rummage through the cart. Just rusty nails and rock dust.	你在推车里翻找。只有生锈的钉子和石粉。
The hearthstone of the old beacon lifts: a keeper's tinderbox, wrapped in oilcloth, and a little pouch of Shards. Somebody meant to light it again.	旧烽火台的炉石被掀了起来：一个守塔人的火绒盒，裹在油布里，还有一小袋碎片。有人本想再把它点起来。
He has nothing more to give you, and has been very good about it.	他没有什么能再给你的了，而且他对此表现得非常大度。
Something squeaks inside the stump. A rubber duck! It looks at you as if you were the strange one.	树桩里有东西吱吱叫。一只橡皮鸭！它看着你，好像你才是那个奇怪的家伙。
Five secrets found. You're developing a nose for it.	找到了五个秘密。你开始有嗅觉了。
Ten secrets! People have started hiding things from you on purpose.	十个秘密！人们开始故意对你藏东西了。
Every secret in Brambleton, found. Somewhere, a squirrel weeps.	Brambleton 的每一个秘密，都找到了。某个地方，一只松鼠在哭泣。
You fish out a fortune in wishes. Twenty-five gold, give or take a dream.	你捞出了一大笔愿望。二十五金币，外加或减去一个梦想。
A magpie's stash: a spoon, a thimble, and a sapphire it must have been very proud of.	一只喜鹊的藏宝：一把勺子，一个顶针，还有一颗它一定非常骄傲的蓝宝石。
A statue of a wizard, its hat a lid. Under it: Shards, and a note in Bert's hand: 'IF FOUND, NO YOU DIDN'T.'	一座巫师雕像，他的帽子是个盖子。下面：碎片，还有一张 Bert 笔迹的便条：“如果你找到了这个，不，你没有。”
The barrel's labelled VINEGAR. Under the vinegar, a draught in a waxed bottle. Somebody trusts nobody.	木桶上标着“醋”。醋下面，有一瓶用蜡封着的药剂。有人谁都不信任。
The statue's hand is cupped. In it, worn smooth by years of rain, Shards.	雕像的手捧成杯状。手里是被多年雨水磨光的碎片。
Under the hot stone, a scorpion. And under the scorpion, a purse. The scorpion lets you have it. Today.	热石头下面，有一只蝎子。蝎子下面，有一个钱袋。蝎子让你拿走了它。今天。
A key, wedged behind the statue's ear. It winks at you. Keys shouldn't wink.	一把钥匙，卡在雕像的耳朵后面。它冲你眨了眨眼。钥匙不该眨眼的。
Under the cobble, a little tin with Shards in it and a child's drawing of a dragon. The dragon's smiling.	鹅卵石下面，一个小锡盒，里面有碎片和一张孩子画的龙。龙在微笑。
A false bottom! Thirty gold under it, and a smuggler's apology note.	假底！下面有三十金币，还有一张走私者的道歉便条。
You fish about with a bucket and bring up a handful of wishes: coins gone green, and Shards. Somebody wished very hard.	你用桶捞了捞，捞上来一把愿望：发绿的硬币，还有碎片。有人许愿许得非常用力。
A potion, tucked behind the statue's cloak. It smells of mint and bad decisions.	一瓶药水，塞在雕像的斗篷后面。闻起来有薄荷和糟糕决定的味道。
The well's bottom is lined with Shards, thrown in by people who didn't know what they had.	井底铺着碎片，是那些不知道自己手里有什么的人扔进去的。
The barrel's full of snow, and buried in it, a potion someone was keeping cold.	木桶里装满了雪，雪里埋着一瓶有人想保持冰凉的药水。
Behind the statue of a long-dead king, the words 'I hid it here so my son wouldn't find it'. He didn't.	在一位早已死去的国王的雕像后面，写着“我把它藏在这里，这样我儿子就找不到了”。他确实没找到。
Under the loose cobble: one sock. It's warm. It feels lucky. It's definitely lucky.	松动的鹅卵石下面：一只袜子。它是温的。感觉很幸运。它肯定很幸运。
A silver ring, lost so long ago a mushroom's grown through it. The mushroom gives it up.	一枚银戒指，丢了太久，一朵蘑菇从里面长了出来。蘑菇把它交了出来。
A beggar's savings, hidden better than a banker's. You feel a bit bad. A bit.	一个乞丐的积蓄，藏得比银行家的还好。你有点过意不去。一点点。
You press the statue's loose eye. Click: a little drawer slides out of its plinth, full of Shards.	你按了按雕像松动的眼睛。咔哒：一个小抽屉从它的底座里滑了出来，装满了碎片。
A squirrel's winter larder: nuts, a button, and six Shards it had no use for.	一只松鼠的过冬储藏室：坚果，一颗纽扣，还有六枚它用不上的碎片。
Under the stone, a purse and a note: 'Rainy day.' It's sunny. Close enough.	石头下面，一个钱袋和一张便条：“以备不时之需。”今天是晴天。差不多吧。
The barrel's labelled TURNIPS. It is not full of turnips. It is full of the Steward's 'turnip money'.	木桶上标着“萝卜”。里面装的不是萝卜。里面装满了总管的“萝卜钱”。
Inside the barrel, under a layer of herring: a little pouch of Shards. The herring watches you take it.	木桶里，一层鲱鱼下面：一小袋碎片。鲱鱼看着你拿走了它。
Somebody's savings, hidden in the stump. A note: 'NOT FOR SQUIRRELS.' You're not a squirrel. Probably fine.	某人的积蓄，藏在树桩里。一张便条：“松鼠勿动。”你不是松鼠。应该没问题。
Deep in the well, a raven's hoard: shiny things, and Shards. A raven watches from the roof, taking notes.	井的深处，一只渡鸦的宝藏：亮闪闪的东西，还有碎片。一只渡鸦在屋顶上看着，做着笔记。
You pull up a bottle you can barely see. Somebody threw away an Invisibility Potion. Or did they?	你拉上来一个几乎看不见的瓶子。有人扔掉了一瓶隐身药水。还是说并没有？
Two draughts, tucked in the stump with a note: 'In case of hedgehog.'	两瓶药剂，塞在树桩里，附着一张便条：“以防刺猬。”
Under the cobble, a guard's secret stash. And a list of everyone who owes him a drink. You're on it, somehow.	鹅卵石下面，一个卫兵的秘密藏货。还有一张欠他酒的人的名单。不知怎么，你也在上面。
An owl lives in this stump. An owl with Shards. It looks at you, sighs, and hands them over.	这个树桩里住着一只猫头鹰。一只有碎片的猫头鹰。它看着你，叹了口气，把碎片交了出来。
You lift the lid and rummage under the salted fish...	你掀开盖子，在咸鱼下面翻找...
...it's hollow! A hatch in its bottom, and a ladder going down into the dark.	...它是空心的！它的底部有一个舱口，一架梯子通往下面的黑暗中。
...nothing but fish. Odd, though: it sounds hollow.	...除了鱼什么都没有。不过奇怪：它听起来是空心的。
One piece of it doesn't stop chiming. You pick it up. It's warm.	它有一块一直叮当作响停不下来。你把它捡起来。它是温的。
Shardcraft: work Shard magic. Wake shardstones, break gear down for its Shards, write scrolls, enchant gear with rare shards and shape your pocket of the Shardrealm.	碎片术：运用碎片魔法。唤醒碎片石，把装备分解成碎片，书写卷轴，用稀有碎片为装备附魔，塑造你在碎片界里的那一方天地。
The Warden stands over the anchor: it won't let you near.	守护者站在锚的上方：它不让你靠近。
You raise the Staff of Brambleton and speak Bert's words of Unbinding at the anchor..	你举起 Brambleton 之杖，对着锚念出 Bert 的解缚之言..
..and it shatters. Under it the realm's floor falls away into a hollow, vast and warm, where something huge lies sleeping: every splinter of magic here leans toward it, and it breathes. Far away, in the pass of Galaith, something gives. Tell Bert.	..它碎了。它下面，界域的地面塌陷成一个空洞，巨大而温暖，某个庞大的东西躺在那里沉睡：这里的每一丝魔法都倾向它，它在呼吸。远方，在 Galaith 的山口，有什么东西松动了。告诉 Bert。
Through the hole where the anchor stood you see the pass, small and far, as if through ice. The barrier thins, and beyond it the dragon lifts its head: its hide is grown over with Shards, plates of them, glittering.	透过锚原来所在的那个洞，你看到了山口，又小又远，像隔着冰看一样。屏障变薄了，屏障那边，龙抬起了头：它的皮上长满了碎片，一片片的，闪闪发光。
A voice like a landslide, inside your head: 'The little heir, in my own cellar. Break what you like. My faithful Hand has fed me Shards through that wall, crumb by crumb, since the day it went up, and the sword you won is fast asleep.'	一个像山崩一样的声音，在你脑袋里：“小继承人，在我自己的地窖里。想打碎什么就打碎吧。从那堵墙立起来的那天起，我忠实的灰烬之手就一点一点地把碎片穿过它喂给我，而你赢来的那把剑，正在酣睡。”
Your bare hands won't break it: it needs the Staff of Brambleton.	你空手打不碎它：需要 Brambleton 之杖。
You puff smoke into the skep's door. The humming drops, slow and sleepy.	你往蜂箱的门口吹烟。嗡嗡声低了下来，慢吞吞的，昏昏欲睡。
You need a tinderbox to make smoke.	你需要一个火绒盒才能生烟。
Someone should fix that floorboard; it sounds like it's about to fall through.	该有人修修那块地板了；听起来它快要塌了。
You squeeze through the flap into Bert's secret pantry, and there it is: the biscuit tin. Inside, 25 Shards and a pie with BERT'S on it in icing. You take both. You are a cat. Cats do that.	你从活门挤进 Bert 的秘密食品储藏室，它就在那里：饼干罐。里面有 25 枚碎片，还有一个用糖霜写着“BERT 的”的馅饼。你两样都拿了。你是一只猫。猫就是会这么干。
The biscuit tin is empty. Somebody ate the pie.	饼干罐是空的。有人把馅饼吃了。
The flap is far too small for you. Something small and furry might fit.	活门对你来说太小了。某个毛茸茸的小东西也许能钻进去。
You squeeze in under the cottage. Cobwebs, a dead beetle, Jimmy's smell on everything.. and tucked up between two joists, a little bundle in oilcloth, warm where he's slept on it. You drag it out in your teeth: a tiny portrait in a gold frame.	你挤到小屋下面。蜘蛛网，一只死甲虫，到处都是 Jimmy 的气味.. 在两根托梁之间塞着一个裹在油布里的小包，被他睡得暖暖的。你用牙把它拖出来：一幅镶在金框里的小肖像。
Quest complete: What Jimmy Kept!	任务完成：Jimmy 守着的东西！
You squeeze in under the cottage: dust, cobwebs and a dead beetle. Nothing worth a cat's while.	你挤到小屋下面：灰尘、蜘蛛网和一只死甲虫。没有什么值得一只猫费心的。
The gap is far too small for you. Something small and furry might fit.	那道缝隙对你来说太小了。某个毛茸茸的小东西也许能钻进去。
You step into the ring. The world turns over, green, and puts you down somewhere else entirely, on soft grass, in another ring.	你走进圆环。世界翻转过来，一片碧绿，然后把你放在了一个完全不同的地方，在柔软的草地上，在另一个圆环里。
Somewhere, small voices laugh. You've been round them all, and something of the rings comes with you.	某个地方，细小的声音在笑。你已经走遍了所有的圆环，圆环的某种力量跟着你来了。
You step back through the light, and the world settles round you.	你退回光里，世界在你周围安定下来。
You step into the light. Cold, then everything at once, then glass under your feet: the Shardrealm.	你走进光里。先是冷，然后一切同时涌来，然后脚下是玻璃：碎片界。
The rune of the crown rings, low and long, and the note hangs in the hall.	王冠的符文响了起来，低沉而悠长，那个音符悬在大厅里。
All four notes ring together, and on its dais the Deep Anvil wakes, its runes burning a cold blue.	四个音符一起响起，在它的台座上，深渊铁砧苏醒了，它的符文燃着冰冷的蓝光。
The rune of the crown clanks, flat, and the notes die away into the stone.	王冠的符文叮当一响，走了调，音符消失在石头里。
The rune of the crown hums faintly, remembering.	王冠的符文微微嗡鸣，还记得。
The rune stone answers with a dull knock.	符文石用一声沉闷的敲击回应。
The rune of the hammer rings, low and long, and the note hangs in the hall.	锤子的符文响了起来，低沉而悠长，那个音符悬在大厅里。
The rune of the hammer clanks, flat, and the notes die away into the stone.	锤子的符文叮当一响，走了调，音符消失在石头里。
The rune of the hammer hums faintly, remembering.	锤子的符文微微嗡鸣，还记得。
The rune of the rock rings, low and long, and the note hangs in the hall.	岩石的符文响了起来，低沉而悠长，那个音符悬在大厅里。
The rune of the rock clanks, flat, and the notes die away into the stone.	岩石的符文叮当一响，走了调，音符消失在石头里。
The rune of the rock hums faintly, remembering.	岩石的符文微微嗡鸣，还记得。
The rune of the root rings, low and long, and the note hangs in the hall.	树根的符文响了起来，低沉而悠长，那个音符悬在大厅里。
The rune of the root clanks, flat, and the notes die away into the stone.	树根的符文叮当一响，走了调，音符消失在石头里。
The rune of the root hums faintly, remembering.	树根的符文微微嗡鸣，还记得。
The four notes rang true.	四个音符都对了。
The four notes hang in the air together. Far off, under the Reliquary, something heavy settles open.	四个音符一起悬在空中。远处，在圣物堂下面，某个沉重的东西缓缓打开了。
Quest complete: The Glass Wardens: The Four Notes!	任务完成：玻璃守卫：四个音符！
A hollow knock that goes on knocking. Somewhere, a crystal answers it.	一声空洞的敲击，一直敲个不停。某个地方，一块水晶回应了它。
The note jars against the last one, and the crystals fall silent.	这个音符和上一个格格不入，水晶都安静了下来。
A hollow knock that goes on knocking.	一声空洞的敲击，一直敲个不停。
A low bell, and the embers breathe brighter. Somewhere, a crystal answers it.	一声低沉的钟鸣，余烬亮了起来。某个地方，一块水晶回应了它。
A low bell, and the embers breathe brighter.	一声低沉的钟鸣，余烬亮了起来。

Spells or potions?	法术还是药水？
When the world was young, the Old Ones raised the shardstones and bound the land's magic into them. When the dragon came to Galaith's mountain it drank that magic down, and what it couldn't hold it shattered: Shards. They're everywhere now: in the earth, in the old places, in the beasts that swallow them.	世界还年轻的时候，古人立起了碎片石，把大地的魔法缚在里面。龙来到 Galaith 的山上时，它把那些魔法喝了下去，它装不下的就打碎了：碎片。现在它们到处都是：在泥土里，在古老的地方，在吞下它们的野兽体内。
And Galaith's mountain?	那 Galaith 的山呢？
Tell me more.	再多讲讲。
A shardstone is an Old One's door, and its rune sleeps until it's fed. A few Shards wake it, and once it knows you it remembers you: that's why you pay only the once. The far ones are hungrier.	碎片石是古人的门，它的符文沉睡着，直到被喂食。几枚碎片就能唤醒它，一旦它认识了你，它就会记住你：所以你只需要付一次。远的那些更饿。
Magic draws them, the way a fire draws moths. A wolf that's swallowed a Shard runs faster and bites harder, and when it dies the Shard falls out of it, for whoever picks it up. The worse the beast, the more it's eaten. And sometimes the magic won't die with it: it rises, all splinters and spite, and goes for whoever killed it.	魔法吸引着它们，就像火吸引飞蛾一样。吞下一块碎片的狼跑得更快，咬得更狠，它死的时候碎片会从它身上掉出来，谁捡到就是谁的。野兽越凶，它吃得就越多。有时魔法不肯跟着它一起死去：它会升起来，满是碎屑和怨恨，去找杀死它的人。
And Galaith's mountain was the deepest seam of all. Your forefathers dug its gold, and its Shards, until the dragon came for them. It sits on them still.	而 Galaith 的山是所有矿脉中最深的。你的先祖们开采它的金子和碎片，直到龙来抢夺它们。它现在还坐在上面。
Shards reach out of you: they strike, freeze, carry things, carry you, or bind round you as a ward. What they can't do is get inside a living body. They'll never mend a wound or sharpen an eye.\That's the brewer's art: herbs work from within, on whoever drinks them, and on nothing else. And no herb ever made a Shard.	碎片从你体内伸出去：它们攻击，冰冻，搬运东西，搬运你，或者缠绕在你身边作为护盾。它们做不到的是进入一个活的身体。它们永远治不好伤口，也不能让眼睛更锐利。\那是酿造者的技艺：草药从内部起作用，作用于喝下它们的人，别的都不行。也从来没有哪种草药做出过碎片。

The spring north of the town has fed our well since before there was a town. This spring it failed, and now Corvin sells us water by the cup.\Springs don't just fail. Will you go and see?	镇子北边的泉水在有镇子之前就一直供应着我们的井。今年春天它断了，现在 Corvin 按杯卖水给我们。\泉水不会无缘无故断的。你愿意去看看吗？
The spring that feeds Dunerest's well has failed. Elder Sabra wants to know why: it's north of the town.	供应 Dunerest 水井的泉水断了。Sabra 长老想知道为什么：泉水在镇子北边。
Quest started: The Dry Spring.	任务开始：干涸的泉水。
You pulled down Corvin's dam, and the spring runs to Dunerest's well again.	你拆掉了 Corvin 的水坝，泉水又流向 Dunerest 的井了。
Corvin! Damming our spring to sell us our own water. Well, the well is filling, and the town knows who to thank.\It's not much, but here's 50 gold from all of us.	Corvin！拦住我们的泉水，把我们自己的水卖给我们。好了，井在涨水了，镇上的人知道该谢谁。\不多，但这是我们大家凑的 50 金币。
Thank you, Elder.	谢谢你，长老。
Quest complete: The Dry Spring! You receive 50 gold.	任务完成：干涸的泉水！你获得了 50 金币。
Have you been to the spring? It's north of the town, past the dunes.	你去过泉水那里了吗？在镇子北边，过了沙丘。
Still dry. Whatever you found up there, you never told me. Corvin's never looked happier.	还是干的。不管你在那上面发现了什么，你都没告诉我。Corvin 从来没这么高兴过。
Clean water in the well, and no thanks to Corvin. Bless you.	井里有干净的水了，可不是托 Corvin 的福。保佑你。
The sand gets everywhere. Into the bread, into the beds.	沙子到处都是。钻进面包里，钻进床里。
A quiet chapel. Sit, and rest your feet.	一座安静的礼拜堂。坐下，歇歇脚吧。
Have you tried the bakery? Best bread in Dunerest.	你试过那家面包房吗？Dunerest 最好的面包。
The Grimoire of Unbinding? You're the first in thirty years to ask me that.\The sun priests kept their library under the desert north of here, and the sand took it. I've found the corner of its lintel, sticking out of a dune. Bring a spade and dig it out, and you'll find the steps. Its guardians, mind, never stopped guarding.	解缚魔典？三十年来你是第一个问我这个的人。\太阳祭司把他们的图书馆放在这里北边的沙漠下面，沙子吞没了它。我找到了它门楣的一角，从一座沙丘里露出来。带一把铁锹去把它挖出来，你就会找到台阶。它的守卫者，注意，从来没有停止过守卫。
I'll dig it out.	我去把它挖出来。
Quest started: The Sunken Grimoire.	任务开始：沉没的魔典。
Sand gets everywhere. In the books, in the bread, in the beard.	沙子到处都是。书里，面包里，胡子里。
Well met! Brokk Stonebeard, last of the deep miners.\Gold, gems, good steel: I trade in all of it. And my forge is hot if you've ore to work.	幸会！Brokk Stonebeard，最后一个深矿工。\黄金、宝石、好钢：我都做买卖。如果你有矿石要加工，我的熔炉也是热的。
What do you trade?	你买卖什么？
Can I use your forge?	我能用你的熔炉吗？
Do you need help?	你需要帮忙吗？
Furnace's on the left, anvil on the right. Mind your beard.\Smelt ore into bars at the furnace, then hammer the bars into tools and armour on the anvil.	熔炉在左边，铁砧在右边。小心你的胡子。\在熔炉里把矿石冶炼成锭，然后在铁砧上把锭锤成工具和盔甲。
Help? Aye. The dead walk in my halls: skeletons of the old miners, raised by who knows what.\And a cave troll has taken the crystal vein by the lake. Smash four skeletons and kill the troll, and I'll make it worth your while.	帮忙？是啊。死人在我的大厅里走动：老矿工的骷髅，不知道被什么东西唤起来的。\还有一个洞穴巨魔占领了湖边的水晶矿脉。击碎四具骷髅，杀死巨魔，我会让你觉得值得。
Smash four skeletons in the Deep Hollow.	在深谷击碎四具骷髅。
Then go back to Brokk.	然后回到 Brokk 那里。
Quest started: The Dwarf's Grudge.	任务开始：矮人的怨恨。
Ha! The troll is dead and the bones lie still. My halls are mine again!\Take this blade: dwarf steel, the best there is. And some stones from the vein.\My vault, west of the forge, is open to you now.	哈！巨魔死了，骨头也躺平了。我的大厅又是我的了！\拿着这把刀刃：矮人钢，最好的钢。还有一些矿脉里的宝石。\我的金库，在熔炉西边，现在对你开放了。
Thank you, Brokk.	谢谢你，Brokk。
Quest complete! You receive a steel sword, 2 sapphires, 150 gold and Mining experience. Brokk's vault is open to you.	任务完成！你获得了一把钢剑、2 颗蓝宝石、150 金币和采矿经验。Brokk 的金库对你开放了。
Skeletons smashed: $Variable.2 of 4. Trolls slain: $Variable.3 of 1.\The skeletons wander all over the Hollow; the troll lairs south-east, past the lake.	击碎的骷髅：$Variable.2 / 4。杀死的巨魔：$Variable.3 / 1。\骷髅在深谷各处游荡；巨魔的巢穴在东南边，过了湖。
I'll get back to it.	我会继续的。
My halls are quiet again, friend. Mine the crystal vein any time you like, if you've the skill for it.\And help yourself to what's in my vault, west of the forge.	我的大厅又安静了，朋友。只要你有那个技术，随时都可以来开采水晶矿脉。\还有，我金库里的东西随便拿，在熔炉西边。
Hah! My grudge is with things that bite back, beanpole. Get the King's hobgoblins seen to and some muscle on those arms, and then we'll talk.	哈！我的怨恨是对着那些会还嘴的东西的，竹竿。先把国王的大地精解决了，胳膊上长点肌肉，然后我们再谈。
A fried egg, crisp at the edges, just as I like it! You have your mother's kindness. Thank you.\Let the record show that I am very pleased.	一个煎蛋，边缘焦脆，正是我喜欢的样子！你有你母亲的善良。谢谢你。\请记录在案：我非常满意。
Enjoy it.	请慢用。
Thank you, dear. Now, Brother Aldous left this with us for safekeeping: the key to the trapdoor in the chapel cellar. Mary said that one day you would need it.\Only promise me you won't go down there without a sword and a shield. I've patched up enough folk who went down with a stick and an opinion.	谢谢你，亲爱的。对了，Aldous 修士把这个交给我们保管：礼拜堂地窖里活板门的钥匙。Mary 说过，总有一天你会需要它。\只要答应我，没有剑和盾就别下去。带着一根棍子和一股倔劲下去的人，我已经缝过够多了。
I promise.	我答应。
And take these with you: two healing draughts, brewed from Bess's herbs. Drink one when you're hurt: doctor's orders.\Herbs, mind, not magic. Shards can do a great deal, but they can't mend a body: only a brewer's herbs can. They taste dreadful. That's how you know they work.	把这些也带上：两瓶治疗药剂，用 Bess 的草药酿的。受伤时喝一瓶：医嘱。\注意，是草药，不是魔法。碎片能做很多事，但它们治不好身体：只有酿造者的草药可以。味道糟透了。所以你才知道它管用。
Thank you, Maren.	谢谢你，Maren。
Quest complete: Dinner at Edwin's! Maren gave you the key to the chapel's trapdoor, and two healing draughts.	任务完成：在 Edwin 家吃晚饭！Maren 给了你礼拜堂活板门的钥匙和两瓶治疗药剂。
If you need anything drawn up, a will or a deed, I'm your man. Though I'd rather you didn't need a will for a good long while.	如果你需要起草什么东西，遗嘱或地契，找我就对了。不过我希望你要用遗嘱的那天还很远、很远。
What was my mother like?	我母亲是个什么样的人？
She came to Muddlesey with you in her arms and never said from where. Kind, proud, and far cleverer than she let on. She once argued me out of my own fee, and I thanked her for it.\I always thought there was more to her than a cottage and a cat.	她抱着你来到 Muddlesey，从来没说过是从哪儿来的。善良，骄傲，比她表现出来的聪明得多。有一次她说得我自己免掉了我的费用，我还道了谢。\我总觉得她不只是一间小屋和一只猫那么简单。
Take your time with the letter. It's yours alone. I'm not curious. I am very, very not curious.	慢慢读那封信吧。它只属于你一个人。我不好奇。一点、一点都不好奇。
Mind the third plank. And the fifth. Truth be told, mind all of 'em.	小心第三块木板。还有第五块。说实话，每一块都要小心。
What do you catch out here?	你在这儿钓什么？
Seen anything odd?	看到什么怪事了吗？
Is that eel I can smell?	我闻到的是鳗鱼吗？
I'll leave you to it.	不打扰你了。
Eels. Long ones. A short one's only an eel that hasn't finished. There's a fat lot lie off the end of my jetty, if you've the patience and a rod. I've the patience.	鳗鱼。长的。短的只是还没长完的鳗鱼。我的栈桥尽头有一大群肥的，如果你有耐心和钓竿的话。我有耐心。
Doesn't the damp get to you?	潮气不会让你难受吗？
What damp? (Water runs off the brim of his hat and into his boot.)	什么潮气？（水从他的帽檐流下来，流进了他的靴子。）
There's a bell north of here that rings when nobody's by. Doesn't bother the eels. They say there's lights out west in the Mirefen, too, but they say a lot of things in the Mirefen. They've nobody to stop 'em.	这里北边有一口钟，没人在旁边的时候也会响。鳗鱼不在乎。他们说西边的 Mirefen 也有光，不过 Mirefen 的人说的话多着呢。没人管得住他们。
That's the smell of a good eel. Here. Don't ask me how long it's been smoked, I've lost count and so has it.	那是好鳗鱼的味道。给。别问我它熏了多久，我数不清了，它也数不清了。
You've had yours. The rest are spoken for. I've spoken for them.	你已经拿过你的了。剩下的都有主了。是我做的主。
Three nights now, and every morning my eel traps are cut. Not torn: cut, with a knife.\Find out who's doing it. The reeds east of the village, that's where they'd hide.	已经三个晚上了，每天早上我的鳗鱼笼都被割破了。不是扯破的：是割破的，用刀。\帮我查出是谁干的。村子东边的芦苇丛，他们会躲在那里。
I'll find out.	我会查出来的。
Someone's been cutting Wenna's eel traps in the night. The reeds east of Brinebottom are the place to look.	有人在夜里割 Wenna 的鳗鱼笼。该去 Brinebottom 东边的芦苇丛里找找。
Quest started: The Eel Traps.	任务开始：鳗鱼笼。
You told Wenna it was Tobin cutting her traps. She paid you 60 gold.	你告诉 Wenna 是 Tobin 割了她的笼子。她付了你 60 金币。
Tobin? That little... His mother will hear of this, and so will the whole village.\Here, 60 gold. You've earned it.	Tobin？那个小……他母亲会知道这件事的，全村人都会知道。\给，60 金币。这是你应得的。
Quest complete: The Eel Traps! You receive 60 gold.	任务完成：鳗鱼笼！你获得了 60 金币。
Planks? And you won't say who did it.\...Tobin's father had this spot before me. When he took sick, I moved in. I've thought about that more than I'd like.	木板？而你不肯说是谁干的。\……Tobin 的父亲在我之前就在这个地方。他病倒以后，我就搬了进来。这件事我想得比我愿意的还多。
Share your catch with him.	把你的渔获分给他一些。
You mended Wenna's traps, and she'll share her catch with Tobin's father.	你修好了 Wenna 的笼子，她会把渔获分给 Tobin 的父亲。
Half the catch, till he's on his feet. And whoever was cutting my traps can stop now, can't they?	分一半的渔获，直到他好起来。那个割我笼子的人现在可以收手了，不是吗？
I think they will.	我想会的。
Quest complete: The Eel Traps! Wenna's traps are mended, and Tobin's father eats tonight.	任务完成：鳗鱼笼！Wenna 的笼子修好了，Tobin 的父亲今晚有饭吃了。
Planks for the traps: three of them. You'll find them at any sawmill or carpenter's.	修笼子要木板：三块。任何锯木厂或木匠那里都能找到。
Any sign of who's cutting them? Try the reeds east of the village.	有割笼子的人的线索吗？去村子东边的芦苇丛里找找。
Still looking.	还在找。
The traps are full every morning now. Eels for the market, and some over.	现在每天早上笼子都是满的。鳗鱼拿去集市卖，还有剩的。
Eels! Fresh this morning!	鳗鱼！今天早上新鲜的！
From Morwen? *cough* ...ugh, vile... but... I feel warmer already. Thank her for me.	Morwen 送来的？*咳嗽* ……呃，真难喝……不过……我已经觉得暖和些了。替我谢谢她。
Bog Lights: tell Morwen the Elder has drunk her brew.	沼泽之光：告诉 Morwen，长老喝了她的药。
*cough* Morwen said she'd send something... have you got it?	*咳嗽* Morwen 说过她会送东西来……你拿来了吗？
Welcome to Brinebottom, traveller. Keep to the dry ground.	欢迎来到 Brinebottom，旅人。走在干的地方。
Welcome to The Owl and Ivy! What'll it be?	欢迎来到猫头鹰与常春藤酒馆！要点什么？
The village hall. Dances on a Saturday, meetings on a Monday.	村公所。周六跳舞，周一开会。
You're not from round here, are you? Emberwatch's a friendly place, mostly.	你不是本地人，是吧？Emberwatch 大体上是个友好的地方。
Ah, a visitor! Help yourself to a cabbage or two, there's plenty. And the tilled furrows by the cabbages are free for anyone to sow: buy seeds from me, use them on the soil, and what you sow is yours to harvest.\Just don't go chasing my chickens... unless you're buying.	啊，有客人！随便拿一两个卷心菜，多着呢。卷心菜旁边犁好的地垄谁都可以免费播种：从我这里买种子，用在土上，你种的东西就归你收获。\只是别去追我的鸡……除非你要买。
Seen any goblins?	看到哥布林了吗？
Seen them? They steal my eggs every night!\Their camp is just down the road to the south. The King's Steward ought to do something.	看到？它们每天晚上都偷我的蛋！\它们的营地就在往南的路上。国王的总管该管管了。
I'll speak to the Steward.	我会去和总管说。
They're here now? I'll fight them off!	它们现在就在这儿？我来打退它们！
The farmer points at the hedges: "There! Here they come!"	农夫指着树篱：“那儿！它们来了！”
'Mushrooms! Cave mushrooms, the glowing kind: the only crop that likes the dark as much as I do. Here, have a few. On the house. The house is a hole.'	“蘑菇！洞穴蘑菇，会发光的那种：唯一一种和我一样喜欢黑暗的作物。给，拿几个。我请客。我的家就是个洞。”
'The trick is, you talk to them. Not nicely. Mushrooms don't trust nice.'	“诀窍是，你要和它们说话。不要客气。蘑菇不相信客气。”
'Maud says I can't sell them to the dwarves because there aren't any dwarves. I say that's just what a dwarf would want you to think.'	“Maud 说我不能把它们卖给矮人，因为根本没有矮人。我说那正是矮人想让你以为的。”
Back to Muddlesey? The boats cross together, one from each pier, whenever the bell goes. Step aboard when one's tied up here, and a coin's the fare.\They pass each other halfway. Wave, if you like. Nobody does.	回 Muddlesey？两条船一起渡海，每个码头一条，钟一响就出发。有船停在这里时就上船，一枚硬币就是船费。\它们在半路上相互交错。你愿意的话就挥挥手。没人挥。
Thanks, Wim.	谢谢，Wim。
Drizzleport, is it? Step aboard when a boat's in, and pay a coin. They leave both piers on the bell and pass in the strait.\Nobody's lost a passenger in years. Well. Nobody's lost one this year.	去 Drizzleport，是吗？有船靠岸时就上船，付一枚硬币。它们听钟声从两个码头出发，在海峡里交错。\这些年没丢过一位乘客。嗯。今年没丢过。
Hold up, friend. You're green as grass yet, and you've never had a beast's leavings in your hands, by the look of you. The mainland eats the likes of you: see Brother Aldous first, and come back at level 3.	等等，朋友。你还嫩得跟草一样，看样子你手里还从没拿过野兽留下的东西。大陆会把你这样的人吃掉的：先去找 Aldous 修士，到 3 级再回来。
Papers? Let's have a look, then.\He unfolds them, and stops at the seal, and looks at you for a long while.\Well. That's not my business, and I'll not make it mine. Those'll get you past any guard in Drizzleport.\And when you land: there's a shardstone by the market. Feed it a few Shards and it wakes, and after that it'll carry you to any other shardstone you've woken. Mind your Shards.	文书？那让我看看。\他把文书展开，停在了印章上，盯着你看了很久。\好吧。那不关我的事，我也不会把它变成我的事。有了这些，Drizzleport 的任何守卫都会放你过去。\还有，你上岸以后：集市旁边有一块碎片石。喂它几枚碎片它就会苏醒，以后它就能把你带到你唤醒过的任何其他碎片石。看好你的碎片。
Thank you, Wim.	谢谢你，Wim。
Quest complete: Passage to the Mainland!	任务完成：前往大陆！
Step aboard when a boat's in, and a coin's the fare. They leave both piers on the bell.	有船靠岸时就上船，一枚硬币就是船费。它们听钟声从两个码头出发。
Cross the strait? Papers first, friend. Since the raids, Drizzleport's guards send back anyone off Muddlesey without papers, and I'm the one who has to bring them home again.\Old Tobias always wrote them, him keeping Muddlesey's roll, but nobody's seen him since the hobgoblins came. Ask Bess, up at the farm: she's his daughter.	渡海峡？先要文书，朋友。自从那几次袭击以后，Drizzleport 的守卫会把任何没有文书的Muddlesey 人送回来，而把他们送回家的人是我。\以前一直是老 Tobias 写文书，他管着 Muddlesey 的名册，可自从大地精来了以后就没人见过他了。去问问农场上的 Bess：她是他女儿。
Quest started: Passage to the Mainland.	任务开始：前往大陆。
Got your papers? No? Then no passage, friend. Drizzleport would only send you back, and I'd be the one bringing you. Twice the rowing, and the same fare.	有文书了吗？没有？那就不能渡海，朋友。Drizzleport 只会把你送回来，而把你送回来的人是我。划双倍的桨，收一样的船钱。
Fish are biting today! Bait your hook with a worm and anything in the sea will bite.\Once you're handy with a rod, the trout go mad for it.	今天鱼咬钩！在钩子上挂条虫子，海里什么都会咬。\等你用钓竿熟练了，鳟鱼会疯了一样咬钩。
Any big catches about?	最近有什么大鱼吗？
Cave eels! Pale as ghosts, they live in the black pools deep underground. You'll want real skill (Fishing 8) to land one.\I pay well for them, cooked or raw.	洞穴鳗！白得像鬼一样，它们住在地下深处的黑水池里。你得有真本事（钓鱼 8 级）才能钓上一条。\我出高价收，熟的生的都要。
I'll look for them.	我会去找找。
Caught this morning!	今天早上钓的！
My forge is stone cold, and I've no coal.\Bring me five lumps and we'll talk about the real trouble.	我的熔炉冷冰冰的，我没有煤了。\给我带五块来，我们再谈真正的麻烦。
I'll bring coal.	我会带煤来。
Bring Brann 5 coal (mine coal rocks, Mining 3, or buy some).	给 Brann 带 5 块煤（开采煤矿石，采矿 3 级，或者买一些）。
Quest started: The Frozen Forge.	任务开始：冰封的熔炉。
Bring Brann 5 coal (mine coal rocks, Mining 5, or buy some).	给 Brann 带 5 块煤（开采煤矿石，采矿 5 级，或者买一些）。
Coal! Now, the trouble: a Frost Troll smashed my waterwheel and lairs on the frozen lake east of town. Slay it.	煤！现在，麻烦是：一只冰霜巨魔砸坏了我的水车，它的巢穴在镇子东边的冰湖上。杀了它。
I'll slay it.	我会杀了它。
Five coal. Mine it in the hills, or buy it from the trader.	五块煤。在山里开采，或者从商人那里买。
I'll get it.	我去弄。
The Frost Troll lairs on the frozen lake to the east. Bring warm clothes.	冰霜巨魔的巢穴在东边的冰湖上。带上暖和的衣服。
With the troll gone and the wheel turning... *clang* *clang*... there. A mithril sword, for the one who saved my forge.	巨魔死了，水车转起来了……*铛* *铛*……好了。一把秘银剑，给救了我熔炉的人。
Quest complete: The Frozen Forge! You receive a mithril sword, 250 gold and Crafting experience.	任务完成：冰封的熔炉！你获得了一把秘银剑、250 金币和制作经验。

Can you open a locked chest?	你能打开一个锁着的箱子吗？
I make the things, so I know their tricks. A lock's a few pins on springs: work each one up till it catches, quiet and patient, and the lid's yours (Pick Lock). The better you are at keeping out of sight, the steadier your hands.\Or put your weight on the lid till it splits (Break Open). Loud, mind. And a chest bound in iron won't split for anyone: that one you pick, or find its key.\Not mine, mind. Mine are made properly.	这些东西是我做的，所以我知道它们的窍门。一把锁就是几根装在弹簧上的销子：把每一根往上顶，直到卡住，安静又耐心，箱盖就归你了（撬锁）。你越擅长不被看见，你的手就越稳。\或者把你的重量压在箱盖上，直到它裂开（砸开）。注意，很响。而且铁箍的箱子谁都砸不开：那种你得撬，或者找到钥匙。\别撬我的。我的做得很好。

Baa! Ha, sorry - you talk to sheep all day, you start sounding like one.\Mind you don't spook them. If you're after wool, they'll let you shear them if you're gentle.	咩！哈，抱歉——你整天跟羊说话，说起话来也像羊了。\注意别吓着它们。如果你想要羊毛，只要你温柔点，它们会让你剪的。

Use shears on one - the tinker in Puddleford sells them, or a shop will. Then spin the wool at a spinning wheel, or sell it to a weaver.	对一只羊使用大剪刀——Puddleford 的补锅匠有卖，商店也有。然后在纺车上纺羊毛，或者卖给织工。
Welcome to Muddlesey Store! There's precious little money on Muddlesey, so I keep what folk can't do without. Tools, bait and a bite to eat: that's what folk need here.	欢迎来到 Muddlesey 商店！Muddlesey 上钱少得可怜，所以我只卖大家离不了的东西。工具、鱼饵和吃的：这里的人需要的就是这些。

A door? Under the point? A glass door, sealed?\...You'd best talk to Gran. She tells a story about glass men who walked into the sea. I always thought it was to keep me off the rocks.	一扇门？在岬角下面？一扇封住的玻璃门？\……你最好去和奶奶谈谈。她讲过一个故事，说有些玻璃人走进了海里。我一直以为那是为了让我别去礁石那边。
Where is she?	她在哪儿？
Bryony keeps the cottage on Gull Point with Nell.	Bryony 和 Nell 一起住在 Gull Point 的小屋里。

All right.	好吧。
Nets, glass, nets. Mind the rocks on the north side if you're walking: they've cut better boots than yours.	网，玻璃，网。如果你要走过去，小心北边的礁石：它们割破过比你的好得多的靴子。
The Sun-Forge? Oska sent you, then. My grandmother's grandmother kept that forge, back when it had a roof and a priest who wasn't dead.\The temple's south of the well, half under the dunes. Somebody else's.	太阳熔炉？那是 Oska 派你来的了。我祖母的祖母照看过那座熔炉，那时候它还有屋顶，还有一个没死的祭司。\神殿在井的南边，一半埋在沙丘下面。守护者还在里面，裹着他的金子和他的脾气。自从他跟活人作对以后，就没人点过熔炉了。\我本想自己去，可我忙。主要是山羊。别人家的山羊。
Quest started: The Sun-Forge.	任务开始：太阳熔炉。
The Keeper still keeps it? South of the well, under the dunes. Take water. More than that.	守护者还守着它？在井的南边，沙丘下面。带上水。比那再多带点。
The Keeper's gone? Then it's the mirrors: the forge only burns with the sun on it, and the sun comes down to it by mirrors. My grandmother had a song for them:\'Dawn looks where the sun is born; noon looks down on the dead; dusk looks where the sun lies down.'\She sang it over the pot, too, which never helped the stew.	守护者没了？那就是镜子的事了：熔炉只有被阳光照着才会燃烧，而阳光是靠镜子引下去的。我祖母有一首关于它们的歌：\“黎明望向太阳诞生之处；正午俯视死者；黄昏望向太阳躺下之处。”\她煮锅的时候也唱，不过对炖菜从来没帮上忙。

True.	真的。
A lie.	假的。
