Taste that. No, don't, it's for the King. Well. A little.	尝尝这个。不，别尝，这是给国王的。嗯。尝一点吧。
Your Majesty. Your shirts are drying. They're very nice shirts.	陛下。您的衬衫正在晾着。都是非常好的衬衫。
Sheets, sheets, sheets. Royal people do sleep a great deal.	床单，床单，床单。王室的人可真能睡。
Don't touch the line, the shirts are drying.	别碰晾衣绳，衬衫正在晾着。
Twenty years we washed for an empty palace. Kept it ready. You never know.	二十年来我们给一座空宫殿洗衣服。让它随时准备好。谁知道呢。
Your Majesty! I'll curtsey. There. Did I do it right?	陛下！我来行个屈膝礼。好了。我做得对吗？
Morning! Or is it? I've been up since it was yesterday.	早上好！还是说不是早上？我从昨天就起来了。
Twelve beds to make, and the King's makes itself, he's that tidy.	十二张床要铺，国王的床自己就铺好了，他就是那么整洁。
If you see a cat on the throne, it's not mine.	如果你看到王座上有只猫，那不是我的。
Your Majesty. Will you be wanting anything? Only I'm carrying all this.	陛下。您需要什么吗？只是我正拿着这么多东西。
Lord Ashby wants his shirts starched. Lord Ashby can starch his own shirts.	Ashby 大人想要把他的衬衫上浆。Ashby 大人可以自己给衬衫上浆。
The garden's lovely this time of year. Every time of year. The gardener sees to that.	每年这个时候花园都很美。每个时候都是。园丁会确保这一点。
I've worked here four years and I've never once been in the cellar. Polly says it's haunted. Polly says everything's haunted.	我在这里干了四年，一次都没进过地窖。Polly 说那里闹鬼。Polly 说什么都闹鬼。
Your Majesty! Sorry! I'll get out of the way!	陛下！对不起！我这就让开！
The cellar's colder than a fish's handshake. Mind the third step.	地窖比和鱼握手还冷。小心第三级台阶。
Mrs Brisket says I'm to scrub till I can see my face. I can see my face. It's tired.	Brisket 太太说我要擦到能照见自己的脸为止。我能照见我的脸了。它很累。
There's a barrel down there nobody's allowed to open. I've not opened it. Twice.	下面有一个谁都不许打开的木桶。我没打开过它。两次。
Ah. Another one with a lamp. Don't worry, I'm not the one to worry about down here. I'm Pell. I worked this vein forty years, and I'd still be working it, if it wasn't for that thing in the east tunnel.	啊。又一个提着灯的。别担心，这下面要担心的不是我。我是 Pell。我在这条矿脉干了四十年，要不是东边隧道里的那个东西，我现在还在干。
What thing?	什么东西？
I'll be going.	我该走了。
A wraith. It came up out of the deep one night and I didn't. It has my pick. You'd not think a man could miss a pick so. I can't rest without my hands full.	一个幽魂。有天晚上它从深处冒了上来，而我没能再上去。它拿着我的镐。你想不到一个人会那么想念一把镐。手里不拿着东西我就没法安息。
I'll get your pick back.	我会把你的镐拿回来的。
Sorry.	抱歉。
Quest started: Pell's Pick. Kill the wraith in the Stoneways' east tunnel, and bring Pell his pick.	任务开始：Pell 的镐。杀死 Stoneways 东边隧道里的幽魂，把镐带给 Pell。
It's strong, mind. Stronger than me, and I was strong. Bring friends, or bring something sharp.	注意，它很强。比我强，而我以前可是很强的。带上朋友，或者带上锋利的东西。
I'll manage.	我应付得来。
Quest complete: Pell's Pick! Pell has his pick again.	任务完成：Pell 的镐！Pell 又拿到了他的镐。
My pick. (He takes it, and it goes as see-through as he is.) There. Now I can do a bit more, and then I think I'll stop. Take my good one, the steel: it was always too good for me. It wants a living hand.	我的镐。（他接过它，它变得和他一样透明了。）好了。现在我能再干一会儿，然后我想我就要停下了。拿着我那把好的，钢的：它对我来说一直太好了。它需要一只活人的手。
Rest well, Pell.	好好安息吧，Pell。
East tunnel. It knows these halls. Don't let it get behind you.	东边隧道。它熟悉这些大厅。别让它绕到你身后。
(A miner, grey and see-through, works at the rock with his bare hands. He doesn't look up.)	（一个矿工，灰色而透明，正用双手挖着岩石。他没有抬头。）
(He's working the rock with his old pick, very slowly, making no sound at all. He nods to you, and goes on.)	（他正用他的旧镐挖着岩石，非常缓慢，一点声音都没有。他冲你点点头，继续干。）
Oh! A person. With a face. Sorry, I've been talking to the mushrooms.\Pell Varrow, cartographer to the Crown, or I was. I came down after the Wardens' old charts and found they were wrong about everything except how dangerous it is.	哦！一个人。有脸的。抱歉，我一直在和蘑菇说话。\Pell Varrow，王冠的制图师，或者说曾经是。我下来找守卫们的旧海图，发现它们什么都画错了，除了这里有多危险。
What are you mapping?	你在绘制什么地图？
Good luck down here.	在这下面祝你好运。
All of it, eventually. For now, five places I can't get to without being eaten: the lake, the great bones to the west, the singing stone in the galleries, the Wardens' hold and the pit where the light comes from. Stand in each, and I'll take it from your telling.	最终是全部。目前是五个我不被吃掉就到不了的地方：湖，西边的巨骨，长廊里会唱歌的石头，守卫要塞，还有光从里面冒出来的那个坑。到每个地方站一站，我就照你讲的画下来。
Quest started: Charting the Deep.	任务开始：绘制深渊。
$Attr.DeepChart_Count of five on the chart so far. The rest is still blank, and blank is where the monsters live.	目前海图上已经有 $Attr.DeepChart_Count / 5 处了。剩下的还是空白，而空白就是怪物住的地方。
Five! Look at it. A real map of a real place nobody's ever seen. The Crown will want it. The Crown will pay for it, which is better.\Here: your share.	五处！看看它。一张真实的地图，画的是一个没人见过的真实的地方。王冠会想要它的。王冠会为它付钱，这更好。\给：你的那份。
Quest complete: Charting the Deep!	任务完成：绘制深渊！
Ah, a visitor. Tell me: if a crow lands on a scarecrow and nobody sees it, was the scarecrow ever really scary? ...Also my head's cold. They never give us hats. Twenty years in this field and not one hat.	啊，有客人。告诉我：如果一只乌鸦落在稻草人身上，而没人看见，那稻草人还算真的吓人吗？……还有我的头很冷。他们从来不给我们帽子。在这片田里二十年了，一顶帽子都没有。
I'll find you a hat.	我给你找顶帽子。
Did you just talk?	你刚才说话了？
Everything talks, if you stand still long enough. I've had a lot of time to stand still.	什么东西都会说话，只要你站得够久。我有很多时间站着不动。
Quest started: The Scarecrow's Hat.	任务开始：稻草人的帽子。
No hat? That's all right. I'll just stand here. Thinking. Coldly.	没有帽子？没关系。我就站在这里。思考着。冷冷地。
A chef's hat! I shall finally cook! ...I have no hands. Or a kitchen. Or a mouth. But the THOUGHT of it.	一顶厨师帽！我终于可以做饭了！……我没有手。也没有厨房。也没有嘴。但光是这个想法就够了。
Warm at last. I feel... profound. Here's a thought to take with you: the crows were never afraid of me. They just liked the company.	终于暖和了。我感觉……很深刻。送你一个想法带走：乌鸦从来没怕过我。它们只是喜欢有个伴。

Quest complete: The Scarecrow's Hat!	任务完成：稻草人的帽子！

The smugglers are your friends now. Tell Sergeant Holt about the deal.	走私者现在是你的朋友了。把这笔交易告诉 Holt 中士。
You told Red Meg you'll send Holt to the wrong cove. Go and tell him so.	你告诉红发梅格你会把 Holt 引到错误的小海湾去。去告诉他吧。
Ha! I like you. Tell him the old cove past the point: nobody's used it in years.\And there's a cot by the fire, and a chest to keep your things, whenever you want them.	哈！我喜欢你。告诉他岬角那边的老海湾：好多年没人用过了。\火边有一张床，还有一个放你东西的箱子，你什么时候想用都行。
You've sided with the smugglers. Go and send Sergeant Holt the wrong way.	你站在了走私者一边。去把 Holt 中士引到错误的方向。
Who let you in? Out, before my lads see you - they're not as friendly as me.	谁放你进来的？出去，趁我的手下还没看见你——他们可没我这么友好。
You there! Forgive an old man for shouting: I'm not allowed any nearer. Ambrose, steward of Redrock Manor these fifty years. Well. Steward of the road outside it, these three months.	你在那儿！原谅一个老人大喊大叫：我不许再靠近了。Ambrose，Redrock 庄园的管家，五十年了。嗯。这三个月，是庄园外面那条路的管家。
What happened?	发生了什么事？
Good day to you.	祝你日安。
Bandits. They came in the spring and threw me out by my collar. The old lord, rest him, was a careful man: he built that house so no thief could ever find his way about it. Rooms with no doors. Stairs to nowhere. It took me twenty years to learn it, and I still go the long way round to the kitchen.\They've never found the vault. I can hear them swearing about it from here.	强盗。他们春天来了，揪着我的衣领把我扔了出去。老爷，愿他安息，是个谨慎的人：他把那座房子建得让任何小偷都找不着路。没有门的房间。通向虚空的楼梯。我花了二十年才学会，去厨房我还是要绕远路。\他们一直没找到金库。我在这儿都能听到他们为这个骂骂咧咧。
And you want something from it.	而你想要里面的某样东西。
The family's signet: a ruby in gold, the Redrock seal. If the young master ever comes home, he'll want it, and I'll not have it melted down by a man called Spleen. Bring it to me and you'll be paid. The rest of the old lord's things in there are yours, with my blessing.	家族的图章戒指：金镶红宝石，Redrock 的印章。如果少爷有一天回家，他会想要它的，我可不能让一个叫 Spleen 的人把它熔掉。把它带给我，你会得到报酬。里面老爷的其他东西都归你，我祝福你。
Quest started: The Redrock Signet.	任务开始：Redrock 图章戒指。
The signet! And you came back out again, which is more than the last two did.\Was the old lord's scroll there beside it? The Dread, he called it. He read it at tax collectors. Keep it: it's done its work in this family.\And with the seal in my hand, the law's on my side: the sheriff's men will have those bandits out by nightfall. Come and sit by my fire, any time.	图章戒指！而且你又出来了，比前两个强。\老爷的卷轴在它旁边吗？他管它叫恐惧。他对着收税的念过。留着吧：它在这个家族里已经完成了它的使命。\有了这枚印章在手里，法律就站在我这边了：警长的人天黑前就会把那些强盗赶出去。随时来我的火边坐坐吧。
Quest complete: The Redrock Signet! The manor is Ambrose's again.	任务完成：Redrock 图章戒指！庄园又归 Ambrose 了。
The signet's in the vault, and the vault's where it always was. Mind how you go: the old lord's house is a liar, every wall of it.	图章戒指在金库里，金库还在它一直在的地方。小心点：老爷的房子是个骗子，每一堵墙都是。
Move along, traveller. Nothing here but an old man and his grudge.	走开，旅人。这里只有一个老人和他的怨恨。
I polish it every morning. The young master will come home one day. I'm quite sure of it.	我每天早上都擦它。少爷总有一天会回家的。我非常确定。
Your Majesty. I'll shoe your horse, mend your mail and say nothing of either.	陛下。我会给您的马钉掌，修您的锁子甲，两件事都不会对外说。
Shoes for the horses, buckles for the guard, and a pot for the cook when she throws one.	给马钉掌，给卫兵做扣环，厨娘扔锅的时候再给她做个锅。
Hold that hoof. No, the other one. No, the horse's.	抓住那只蹄子。不，另一只。不，是马的。
Iron's iron, whoever's king. But it's good to have a king again.	铁就是铁，不管谁当国王。不过又有国王了，这很好。
Your Majesty. The roses are blooming for you. Well, they'd bloom anyway. But for you.	陛下。玫瑰为您盛开了。嗯，它们反正也会开的。但这次是为您。
Roses don't care who's king. They just want feeding.	玫瑰不在乎谁当国王。它们只想要养料。
Kept these beds for an empty palace twenty years. Looks like I did it for somebody after all.	二十年来我为一座空宫殿照看这些花坛。看来我终究是为某个人做的。
Mind the hedge. I've just cut it straight.	小心树篱。我刚把它剪直。
Your Majesty! They're all saddled, if you want a ride. Well. Thunder isn't. Thunder never is.	陛下！它们都配好鞍了，如果您想骑的话。嗯。雷霆没有。雷霆从来不配。
Thunder bites. Midnight kicks. Bellflower does both, but sweetly.	雷霆咬人。午夜踢人。风铃草两样都干，不过很温柔。
I sleep up in the loft with the smell of hay. Best bed in Galaith.	我睡在阁楼上，闻着干草的味道。Galaith 最好的床。
Royal horses. They eat better than I do. They're prettier, too.	王室的马。它们吃得比我好。也比我漂亮。
Your Majesty! A letter for you! Oh. No. It's for the cook. Sorry!	陛下！有您的信！哦。不对。是给厨娘的。对不起！
Can't stop! Letters for the Chancellor! Then letters from the Chancellor!	停不下来！给大法官的信！然后是大法官寄出的信！
I've run this courtyard nine times today. I counted.	我今天在这个院子里跑了九趟了。我数过。
Did you know there are a hundred and twelve flagstones from the gate to the door? I've had time to count.	你知道从大门到门口有一百一十二块石板吗？我有时间去数。
Your Majesty! Guard, ATTEN-tion! ...Well. Those that are awake.	陛下！卫兵，立——正！……嗯。醒着的那些。
Boots by the bunk, blades on the rack, and no dice after the bell.	靴子放在床铺边，刀放在架子上，熄灯钟响后不许掷骰子。
The Royal Guard stood at a locked door for twenty years. Kept us sharp, the captain says. Kept us bored, I say.	皇家卫队在一扇锁着的门前站了二十年。队长说，这让我们保持警觉。我说，这让我们无聊透顶。
You want to drill with the lads? Mind the big one. He doesn't know his own strength. Neither does his mother.	你想和小伙子们一起操练？小心那个大个子。他不知道自己有多大劲。他母亲也不知道。
Your Majesty. The household is at your service. The candles are in the second cupboard.	陛下。全府上下为您效劳。蜡烛在第二个橱柜里。
The silver is counted, the linen is folded, and the cook is shouting. All is as it should be.	银器清点好了，亚麻布叠好了，厨娘在大喊大叫。一切如常。
A household of forty, and I am the only one who knows where the candles are kept. It is a heavy burden.	一个四十人的府邸，而我是唯一知道蜡烛放在哪里的人。这是个沉重的负担。
Please don't walk on the paving with mud on your boots. Ever.	请不要穿着沾泥的靴子走在铺石上。永远不要。
They tell me the dragon is dead, and that the one who killed it asks to see me.\Come closer. Let me see your face.	他们告诉我龙死了，杀死它的人要求见我。\走近些。让我看看你的脸。
Show him the amulet.	给他看护身符。
That amulet... where did you get that? I gave it to my wife, the night before... the night she ran.	那枚护身符……你是从哪里得来的？那是我送给我妻子的，在那天晚上之前……她逃走的那天晚上。
Tell him of Mary's will.	告诉他 Mary 的遗嘱。
My mother, Mary, died on Muddlesey, where she raised me. Her last will told me who I am. She asked me to come home to you and tell you of her death.\And to tell you she never stopped loving you.	我的母亲，Mary，死在了 Muddlesey 上，她在那里把我养大。她的遗嘱告诉了我我是谁。她让我回到你身边，告诉你她的死讯。\还要告诉你，她从未停止爱你。
The King comes down from his throne, and holds you, and weeps.	国王从王座上走下来，抱住你，哭了。
Mary. My Mary. She took you away so the dragon couldn't have you, and I let them call it a betrayal. Eighteen years.\My $Player.Son. My $Player.Son.	Mary。我的 Mary。她把你带走，好让龙得不到你，而我任由他们把那叫作背叛。十八年了。\我的 $Player.Son。我的 $Player.Son。
Father.	父亲。
I have held this throne for a city in chains. You broke the chains. It's yours.\He lifts the crown from his head and sets it on yours.\Long live the $Player.King of Brambleton!	我为一座被锁住的城市守着这个王座。你打破了锁链。它是你的了。\他从头上取下王冠，戴在了你的头上。\Brambleton 的 $Player.King 万岁！
Long live Brambleton.	Brambleton 万岁。
Quest complete: The True Heir! You are the $Player.King of Brambleton. The world is yours to explore.	任务完成：真正的继承人！你是 Brambleton 的 $Player.King 了。世界任你探索。
A ginger cat you'd know anywhere has curled up on the throne. Jimmy, how did you get here?	一只你在哪里都认得出的姜黄色猫蜷在王座上。Jimmy，你是怎么到这儿来的？
My $Player.Son, the $Player.King. Your mother would be proud.	我的 $Player.Son，$Player.King。你母亲会骄傲的。
Another stands here who calls me father.	这里还站着另一个叫我父亲的人。
Goodbye, Father.	再见，父亲。
Then the blood has marked more than one of my line, and Brambleton is luckier than it deserves. The Cutting scattered the royal line with the Shards; the dragon only ever counted one of you. Let it be the Order of Heirs, then: every one the Shards know, sworn to the crown. There is room on this throne for whoever keeps the realm.	那么这血脉标记的不止我家族中的一个人，Brambleton 的运气比它应得的还好。切割把王室血脉和碎片一起撒落了；龙只算过你们中的一个。那就让它成为继承人骑士团吧：每一个被碎片认出的人，都向王冠宣誓。这个王座上有位置留给任何守护这个王国的人。
Morning. Mind the weed, it's slippery. (She shakes a bottle at you; nothing in it.) This shore's a bottle shore. The currents bring 'em all in here, sooner or later. Most are empty. Not all.	早上好。小心海草，很滑。（她冲你晃了晃一个瓶子；里面什么都没有。）这片海岸是瓶子海岸。洋流迟早会把它们全都冲到这里来。大多数是空的。不是全部。
What's in the full ones?	满的那些里面有什么？
Good hunting.	祝你找得顺利。
Letters. Three, these last weeks, all the same hand, and I can't get the corks out with these fingers. Wax, see. Find them for me, open them, and read me what they say. I like a story.	信。这几个星期有三封，都是同一个人的笔迹，可我这几根手指拔不出瓶塞。用蜡封着，你看。帮我找到它们，打开，读给我听上面写了什么。我喜欢听故事。
Quest started: The Castaway's Letters. Find the three bottles Salt Agnes has seen on the shore.	任务开始：漂流者的信。找到 Salt Agnes 在海岸上看到的三个瓶子。
Two along this shore, and one further round, south and east, where the grass comes down to the sand.	两个在这片海岸上，还有一个更远一点，往东南，草地伸到沙滩上的地方。
Any letters? Green bottles, sealed with red.	有信吗？绿瓶子，用红蜡封着。
Gerald! Ha! A heap of stones by the shore, south and east: go and see, if you've a spade. He'll not be coming back for it.	Gerald！哈！岸边的一堆石头，往东南：如果你有铁锹，就去看看吧。他是不会回来拿的了。
Quest complete: The Castaway's Letters! Salt Agnes has her story.	任务完成：漂流者的信！Salt Agnes 听到了她的故事。
The strongbox! And he went inland, and good luck to him. I'll keep his letters, if you don't mind. I like a story that ends with somebody walking off.	保险箱！他往内陆去了，祝他好运。如果你不介意，我就留着他的信了。我喜欢以某人离去结尾的故事。
So do I.	我也是。
Nothing in the bottles today. There's always tomorrow.	今天瓶子里什么都没有。总还有明天。
Welcome to The Old Mill! What'll it be?	欢迎来到老磨坊酒馆！要点什么？
It's you! I'm Pip still, but nobody here knows I was ever a soldier. I gut eels now. It's better than trolls.\Here, it's not much, but it's what I've got.	是你！我还是 Pip，不过这里没人知道我当过兵。我现在剖鳗鱼。比打巨魔强。\给，不多，但这是我仅有的。
Keep it, Pip.	留着吧，Pip。
Pip gives you two fish pies.	Pip 给了你两个鱼馅饼。
That's all I've got, honest.	我就只有这些了，真的。
I know. Look after yourself.	我知道。照顾好自己。
You're a strange one. Thank you, though.	你真是个怪人。不过还是谢谢你。
Have you tried the bakery? Best bread in Brinebottom.	你试过那家面包房吗？Brinebottom 最好的面包。
All right! It was me! I cut her traps.\Da had this spot for twenty years. He took sick, and Wenna moved in before he was cold in his bed. He's not dead, but he might as well be, for all she cares.	好吧！是我！是我割了她的笼子。\我爹在这个地方干了二十年。他病倒了，他的床还没凉，Wenna 就搬了进来。他没死，可在她看来跟死了也差不多。
I'll have to tell Wenna.	我得告诉 Wenna。
Your secret's safe. I'll talk to her.	你的秘密很安全。我去和她谈谈。
It's Tobin cutting Wenna's traps: his father's sick, and she took his trapping place. Tell Wenna.	是 Tobin 割了 Wenna 的笼子：他父亲病了，而她占了他下笼子的地方。告诉 Wenna。
Tobin stares at you, then runs off into the reeds.	Tobin 盯着你，然后跑进了芦苇丛。
You'll keep Tobin's secret. Bring Wenna 3 planks to mend her traps, and talk to her about Tobin's father.	你会保守 Tobin 的秘密。给 Wenna 带 3 块木板修她的笼子，和她谈谈 Tobin 的父亲。
You'd do that? I won't cut them again. I swear it.	你愿意这么做？我不会再割了。我发誓。
See that you don't.	最好别再割。
Well? Have you been to Wenna?	怎么样？你去找过 Wenna 了吗？
Soon.	马上。
Wenna brought Da eels last night! Half her catch. I don't know what you said to her.	Wenna 昨晚给我爹送鳗鱼来了！她渔获的一半。我不知道你跟她说了什么。
Look after him.	照顾好他。
Three of my scouts went out on the Ashwold a week ago. The hounds got them, I'm sure of it.\They'd have their tags on them. Bring me three, so their families know.	一个星期前我的三个斥候去了 Ashwold。我敢肯定是猎犬抓住了他们。\他们身上会带着身份牌。给我带三个来，好让他们的家人知道。
I'll bring the tags.	我会把身份牌带来。
Quest started: The Lost Patrol.	任务开始：失踪的巡逻队。
All three... thank you. Take my old bow. I can't look at it any more.	三个都在……谢谢你。拿着我的旧弓吧。我再也不想看到它了。
Quest complete: The Lost Patrol! You receive 200 gold, a yew bow and arrows.	任务完成：失踪的巡逻队！你获得了 200 金币、一把紫杉弓和箭。
Three tags. The hounds roam the heath west and east of town.	三个身份牌。猎犬在镇子东西两边的荒原上游荡。
The patrol can rest now. The heath's a little less cruel for it.	巡逻队现在可以安息了。荒原因此少了一点残酷。
The patrol went up past the Cinder Peaks. Nobody comes back from there at your strength. Train, and then I'll take you.	巡逻队上了 Cinder Peaks 那边。以你的实力，没人能从那里回来。去训练吧，然后我再带你去。
I'll train.	我去训练。
Eyes on the road. What comes down it out of the Ashfall doesn't stop to talk.	盯着路。从 Ashfall 那边下来的东西可不会停下来说话。
Ranger Ash keeps the old watch stones. Something's been nesting in the deep woods to the west...	游侠 Ash 照看着那些古老的守望石。有什么东西一直在西边的密林里筑巢...
The wolves have been bold this year. The shepherd says there's a den out east, and a great black brute leading them.	今年狼很大胆。牧羊人说东边有个狼窝，一头巨大的黑色野兽领着它们。
Old Morwen brews by the bog. Elder Pike's been poorly; maybe she can help.	老 Morwen 在沼泽边酿药。Pike 长老一直身体不好；也许她能帮上忙。
Bandits on the Redrock road again. Captain Hale wants them gone. Their camp's somewhere in the eastern hills.	Redrock 路上又有强盗了。Hale 队长想让他们消失。他们的营地在东边丘陵的某个地方。
Qadir's caravan never arrived. Lost in the Sunscar, west of here, with the scorpions.	Qadir 的商队一直没到。在这里西边的 Sunscar 沙漠里失踪了，那里有蝎子。
Brann's forge has gone cold. Something huge broke the ice on the lake to the east.	Brann 的熔炉冷了。某个巨大的东西打破了东边湖上的冰。
The Druid says the Heartwood is sickening. Even the bears have gone mad. It's out north-east, deep in the trees.	德鲁伊说心木石正在生病。连熊都发狂了。它在东北边，树林深处。
The old king won't stay in his barrow. The Gravekeeper wants someone brave. The barrow's east, out on the downs.	老国王不肯待在他的古冢里。守墓人想找个勇敢的人。古冢在东边，在外面的高地上。
Goblins on the bridge and the guards run off: we thought we'd starve out here. They say it was you who cleared them. Drinks are on the house at every table in Sulkhaven!	哥布林占了桥，守卫跑了：我们还以为要在这里饿死了。据说是你把它们清走的。在 Sulkhaven 的每一张桌子上，酒都算店里的！
The Warden says the cult burnt the shrines first. Then the farms. Now there's a fire in the Cinder Fort every night. Ask her if you want work.	守望者说，邪教先烧了神龛。然后是农场。现在余烬堡垒每天晚上都有火。如果你想找活干就去问她。
Safe travels.	一路平安。
Wolves! Every night another lamb gone. I can't fight them with a crook.\Thin them out for me? Six should make them think twice.	狼！每天晚上都少一只羊羔。我拿着牧羊杖可打不过它们。\帮我把它们杀掉一些？六只应该能让它们三思。
Quest started: Wolves of the Greyflats.	任务开始：Greyflats 的狼。
The wolf den is out east on the flats.	狼窝在东边的平原上。
Six! But they'll be back while their leader lives: a great black brute with a den out east on the flats.\Kill the Alpha and the pack will scatter.	六只！可只要它们的头领还活着，它们就会回来：一头巨大的黑色野兽，窝在东边的平原上。\杀死头狼，狼群就会散开。
I'll find the den.	我会找到狼窝的。
Six wolves, friend. They prowl the whole of the flats.	六只狼，朋友。它们在整片平原上游荡。
I'll keep hunting.	我会继续猎杀。
The den is east, out on the open flats. Be careful: the Alpha is no ordinary wolf.	狼窝在东边，在开阔的平原上。小心：头狼可不是普通的狼。
I'll be careful.	我会小心的。
The Alpha's dead? Then my flock is safe. Take this shield; it was my father's. And the gold, all I've saved.	头狼死了？那我的羊群安全了。拿着这面盾；它是我父亲的。还有这些金币，我所有的积蓄。
Quest complete: Wolves of the Greyflats! You receive 150 gold, a steel shield and Defence experience.	任务完成：Greyflats 的狼！你获得了 150 金币、一面钢盾和防御经验。
Not a wolf howl in a week. Bless you.	一个星期没听到一声狼嚎了。保佑你。
Wolves took three of my ewes this week. I'd ask you to help, but they'd have you just as quick. Get some fight in you first.	这个星期狼叼走了我三只母羊。我本想请你帮忙，可它们抓你也一样快。先让自己有点战斗力吧。
Three lambs. Three! Over the river while I slept, and the river's up. They'll be across the far side by now, or in the wood, or halfway to the sea. I can't leave the rest.	三只羊羔。三只！趁我睡觉的时候过了河，河水又涨了。它们现在应该到了对岸，或者在树林里，或者已经到了去海边的半路上。我不能丢下其余的。
Quest started: Abel's Lambs. Find Abel's three lambs and send them home.	任务开始：Abel 的羊羔。找到 Abel 的三只羊羔，把它们送回家。
They've a blue mark on the rump. Catch one, and it'll run straight home, they always do once they're sorry.	它们屁股上有个蓝色的记号。抓住一只，它就会直接跑回家，它们一知错总是这样。
Any sign? Three lambs, blue on the rump. They're daft enough to be anywhere.	有线索吗？三只羊羔，屁股上是蓝的。它们傻得可能在任何地方。
I'm still looking.	我还在找。
Quest complete: Abel's Lambs! All three lambs are home.	任务完成：Abel 的羊羔！三只羊羔都回家了。
There they are, all three, looking like it was my fault. Here, take this, it's not much. And some wool off their mothers: they owe you that.	它们在那儿，三只都在，一副是我的错的样子。给，拿着这个，不多。还有一些它们母亲身上的羊毛：这是它们欠你的。
They're good as gold now. For a day or two.	它们现在乖得很。乖个一两天吧。
Mind the river.	小心那条河。
I'm the sheriff here. Trouble goes in the cells; you don't look like trouble.	我是这里的警长。惹麻烦的进牢房；你看起来不像麻烦。
Welcome to the Puddleford General Store!\Tools, buckets, fishing gear: if you need it, I've got it. If I haven't got it, you don't need it.	欢迎来到 Puddleford 杂货店！\工具、桶、渔具：你需要什么，我这里都有。我这里没有的，你就不需要。
Show me your wares.	给我看看你的货物。
Sister Linnet of the Mending. "Come in out of the cold, friend! There's soup on. There's always soup on. And the Shard on the table is good company: sit by it a while, and listen. Shoes off if you like. We're very relaxed about shoes."\"The others? Oh, they're at the gathering tonight. I mustn't say where, I'm sorry: it's only for those of the Mending." She smiles at your coat. "Grey suits everyone, you know. We've a robe in every size. We checked."	弥合会的 Linnet 修女。“进来吧，别在外面受冻，朋友！汤煮好了。汤总是煮好的。桌上的碎片是很好的伴：在它旁边坐一会儿，听一听。想脱鞋就脱吧，我们对鞋子很宽容。”\“其他人？哦，他们今晚在聚会。我不能说在哪里，抱歉：那只对弥合会的人开放。”她冲你的外套笑了笑。“灰色适合每个人，你知道的。我们的袍子什么尺码都有。我查过了。”
Where's the gathering?	聚会在哪里？
"Only for the Mending, friend." She's still smiling. She hasn't stopped since you came in.	“只对弥合会的人开放，朋友。”她还在微笑。从你进来起，她就没停过。
Quest started: In Grey.	任务开始：灰衣人。
"Oh! I didn't see you come in, $Player.Brother. You'll be late: the Cantor's speaking tonight, and he does go on. Down to the field with you. The glass at the east end, and mind the lookouts know you."	“哦！我没看到你进来，$Player.Brother。你要迟到了：领唱今晚要讲话。他会讲很久。去吧，下到原野去。东头的玻璃那里，注意让瞭望的人认出你。”
Sister Linnet looks at you a long moment. "You heard it, didn't you? The great Shard. I can see it on you."\"Whatever it said, it was true. It always is. You won't tell anyone about the field, will you? People don't understand."	Linnet 修女久久地看着你。“你听到了，是不是？那块大碎片。我从你身上看得出来。”\“不管它说了什么，都是真的。它说的总是真的。你不会把原野的事告诉任何人吧？人们不会明白的。”
I won't tell a soul.	我不会告诉任何人。
"I knew it. Bless you, $Player.Brother. This house is yours as much as ours, and anything on the shelf is yours at a fair price, whenever you're in grey."	“我就知道。愿你得福，$Player.Brother。这座房子是你的，也是我们的，架子上的东西你随时都能按公道的价钱买，只要你穿着灰衣。”
Thank you, Sister.	谢谢你，修女。
"Anything you need, $Player.Brother?"	“需要什么吗，$Player.Brother？”

Sister Linnet of the Mending. "Come in out of the cold, friend! There's soup on. There's always soup on. And the Shard on the table is good company: sit by it a while, and listen. Shoes off if you like. We're very relaxed about shoes."	弥合会的 Linnet 修女。“进来吧，别在外面受冻，朋友！汤煮好了。汤总是煮好的。桌上的碎片是很好的伴：在它旁边坐一会儿，听一听。想脱鞋就脱吧，我们对鞋子很宽容。”
Evening. You lit the fire, so you know the rules: you didn't see me, I didn't see you, and the prices are good because nobody's paid any duty on them. Buying?	晚上好。火是你点的，那你就懂规矩：你没见过我，我没见过你，价钱好是因为谁都没交过税。买吗？
Who are you?	你是谁？
Not tonight.	今晚不了。
Marro. My father lit that fire for his customers, and his father. The ash is three feet deep under it, if you dig. Don't dig. The tide won't wait long.	Marro。我父亲为他的客人点这堆火，他父亲也是。底下的灰有三尺深，要是你去挖的话。别挖。潮水不会等太久。
Goodnight.	晚安。
Sergeant Holt. Smuggled goods turn up in the market every week - brandy, silk, potions no apothecary made - and not one of my men has seen a cart come in.\The fishermen by the lake talk of an old barrel on the path that nobody ever moves. Will you take a look?	Holt 中士。每个星期集市上都会出现走私货——白兰地、丝绸、哪个药剂师都没做过的药水——可我的人谁也没看见有马车进来。\湖边的渔民说，小路上有个旧木桶，从来没人挪过。你愿意去看看吗？
I'll look into it.	我去查查看。
Quest started: The Smugglers' Run. (H: your journal)	任务开始：走私者之路。（H：你的日志）
Anything? The barrel's on the lake path, south-west of the castle.	有发现吗？木桶在湖边的小路上，城堡西南边。
Well? Where are they bringing it in?	怎么样？他们从哪里运进来？
At the old cove past the point. They'll be long gone by now.	在岬角那边的老海湾。这会儿他们早就走远了。
Quest complete: The Smugglers' Run! (60 gold) Holt's men will find an empty beach.	任务完成：走私者之路！（60 金币）Holt 的人只会找到一片空海滩。
The camp's ours, and their cargo with it. Meg got away, but she won't be back in a hurry. Here: you've earned this.	营地归我们了，他们的货也是。梅格跑了，不过她一时半会儿不会回来了。给：这是你应得的。
Thank you, Sergeant.	谢谢你，中士。
Quest complete: The Smugglers' Run! (60 gold)	任务完成：走私者之路！（60 金币）
My men are waiting at the camp's gate. Go in through the barrel, and clear the camp with them.	我的人在营地门口等着。从木桶进去，和他们一起把营地清干净。
A tunnel, under the shore? Well? What did you find down there?	岸底下有条隧道？怎么样？你在下面发现了什么？
Red Meg offers a truce: her trade off your streets, your men off her cove.	红发梅格提出休战：她的买卖不进你的街道，你的人不去她的小海湾。
Their camp's on the cove, through the barrel. I'll lead your men in.	他们的营地在小海湾，从木桶进去。我会带你的人进去。
A truce with Red Meg... Ha! Fair enough: a quiet market is all I want. Here, you've earned this.	和红发梅格休战……哈！说得过去：我只想要一个安静的集市。给，这是你应得的。
Holt's men wait at the smugglers' camp. Go in with them and take it.	Holt 的人在走私者的营地等着。和他们一起进去，把它拿下。
Then we go tonight. My men will be at their camp's gate: go in through the barrel and take it with them. They'll fight, mind.	那我们今晚就去。我的人会在他们营地的门口：从木桶进去，和他们一起拿下它。他们会反抗的，当心。
I'm ready.	我准备好了。
You've turned on the smugglers. Holt's men wait at their camp.	你背叛了走私者。Holt 的人在他们的营地等着。
One more thing. A fisherman saw Red Meg on a sandbar off the coast, north of the cove, with two of her lads. The watch's boat is on the cove's shore. Will you bring her in?	还有一件事。一个渔民看到红发梅格带着她的两个手下在海岸外的一个沙洲上，在小海湾北边。守卫的船在小海湾的岸边。你能把她抓回来吗？
I'll bring her in.	我会把她抓回来。
Quest started: The Tide Turns.	任务开始：潮水转向。
The boat's on the cove's shore. Mind her lads: they won't come quietly, even if she does.	船在小海湾的岸边。小心她的手下：就算她肯乖乖跟你走，他们也不会。
She gave herself up? To you? Well, well. She'll have a cell with a view of the sea. Here.	她投降了？向你？好啊，好啊。她会有一间能看到海的牢房。给。

Meg's in a cell and the cove's quiet. The watch won't forget it.	梅格进了牢房，小海湾也安静了。守卫不会忘记这件事的。

Bad luck, Sergeant.	运气不好，中士。
Quiet market these days. Good work.	最近集市很安静。干得好。

Sell it to Spleen.	卖给 Spleen。
It's going back to Ambrose.	它要还给 Ambrose。

Quest complete: The Redrock Signet... sold to Spleen. The bandits at Redrock are your friends now.	任务完成：Redrock 图章戒指……卖给了 Spleen。Redrock 的强盗现在是你的朋友了。
"One more thing, friend, since you're one of us now." Spleen shows you a thumb in the eye, a knee where it hurts, a fistful of grit: how a bandit wins a fair fight. "Never fight fair."	“还有一件事，朋友，既然你现在是我们的人了。”Spleen 教你戳眼睛、往要害上顶膝盖、抓一把沙子：强盗就是这样打赢一场公平的架的。“永远别打公平的架。”
Then don't let my lads catch you on the stairs.	那就别让我的手下在楼梯上逮到你。
Spleen. This is my house now. The old man sent you after his ring, I suppose.\Find it, and bring it to me, not him. I pay better.	Spleen。现在这是我的房子。我猜是老头派你来找他的戒指的。\找到它，把它带给我，别给他。我出的价更高。
The Drowned Shrine? It isn't drowned, it's frozen: the lake came up over it one bad winter and never went down again. North-east of here, past the ridge.\Something stands guard at it. Big. White. I measured it once, from a long way off. I'd rather not again.	沉没神殿？它没沉，是冻住了：有一年冬天特别难熬，湖水漫过了它，就再也没退下去。在这里东北边，翻过山脊。\有个东西守着它。大的。白的。我量过它一次，隔得老远。我可不想再量了。
The Drowned Shrine, north-east, past the ridge. Bring a coat, and something sharp.	沉没神殿，东北边，翻过山脊。带件外套，再带点锋利的东西。
Anything else?	还有别的吗？
The finest cloth in the land, made to measure.	全国最好的布料，量身定做。
Tam, the cooper. You'll not have seen my mother? Annis: little, a green shawl, and a stick she won't use.\Since Father died she goes to that quiet house behind Rufus's yard, every day now, and she comes back smiling. She says he talks to her there. Out of a stone.\He's two winters in the ground. Would you go and see her? She won't hear it from me.	Tam，箍桶匠。你没见过我母亲吧？Annis：个子小小的，披一条绿披肩，拄一根她不肯用的拐杖。\自从父亲死后，她就去 Rufus 院子后面那座安静的房子，现在每天都去，回来的时候满脸笑容。她说他在那里跟她说话。从一块石头里。\他已经入土两个冬天了。你能去看看她吗？我说的她听不进去。
Quest started: The Kind Stranger.	任务开始：好心的陌生人。
She's at the quiet house, round the back of Rufus's yard. Be gentle with her.	她在那座安静的房子里，在 Rufus 院子后面。对她温柔点。
She's gone with them. Out to wherever it is they go. She sent word she's happy.\I suppose that's something.	她跟他们走了。去了他们去的不管什么地方。她捎话来说她很快乐。\我想这也算是点什么吧。
I'm sorry, Tam.	我很遗憾，Tam。
She's home. She sat in Father's chair last night and cried, and then she laughed, and then she ate a whole pie. Thank you.	她回家了。昨晚她坐在父亲的椅子上哭了，然后又笑了，然后吃了一整个馅饼。谢谢你。
Look after her.	照顾好她。
Tam, the cooper. Barrels, buckets, tubs: if it holds water, I made it.	Tam，箍桶匠。木桶、水桶、浴盆：只要能盛水，就是我做的。
Don't touch the red one, it's in a mood. (She looks at you properly.) No, go on, touch it. Everyone should hear these once. My grandmother found them: five stones in a hollow, and every one a note.	别碰那块红的，它在闹脾气。（她认真地看着你。）不，去吧，碰一下。每个人都该听一次这些。是我祖母发现它们的：一个洼地里有五块石头，每一块都是一个音。
What do you do with them?	你用它们做什么？
Play them. I ring a tune, you ring it back. There's nobody else up here to do it, and the stones like a second pair of hands. Shall we?	弹它们。我敲一段调子，你敲回来。这上面没别人能做这个了，而石头喜欢有第二双手。来吗？
Ring me one.	给我敲一段。
Back for another? Good. The stones have been talking about you.	又来了？好。石头一直在说你呢。
Ring me another.	再给我敲一段。
Tamsin taps out a short one with her little horn hammer. Listen, then ring it back.	Tamsin 用她的小角锤敲了一段短的。听好，然后敲回来。
There! You've an ear. My grandmother left me a jar of the coins people paid her to hear them, and I've never known what to do with it. Have some. Come back for a longer one.	好了！你有一双好耳朵。我祖母给我留了一罐钱，是人们付给她听石头的，我一直不知道拿它怎么办。拿一些吧。回来听一段更长的。
Tamsin rings a longer one, and doesn't slow down for you.	Tamsin 敲了一段更长的，一点也没为你放慢。
Well! That one took me a winter. Here: the green came out of the hollow when grandmother dug the yellow stone straight. It rings, if you flick it. There's one more, the old one. It's long.	哎呀！那一段我花了一个冬天。给：祖母把黄石头挖正的时候，这块绿的从洼地里冒了出来。弹一下它就会响。还有一段，那段古老的。很长。
I'll be back for it.	我会回来听的。
Tamsin rings the old tune, all of it, and every stone has its say.	Tamsin 把那首古老的曲子从头到尾敲了一遍，每块石头都说了话。
The last note doesn't stop. It hangs in the hollow, then runs down the veins and into the ground, and up into you.	最后一个音没有停下。它悬在洼地里，然后顺着矿脉流进地下，又流进了你的身体。
Did you feel that? They've never done that for me. (She doesn't seem to mind.) Come and ring whenever you like. They know you now.	你感觉到了吗？它们从来没为我这样过。（她好像并不在意。）想敲就随时来吧。它们现在认识你了。
Tamsin rings one for the fun of it.	Tamsin 为了好玩敲了一段。
Lovely. That's how they ought to sound.	真好听。它们就该是这个声音。
Another.	再来一段。
That's enough.	够了。
Never mind. They're stones, they'll wait. So will I.	没关系。它们是石头，它们会等的。我也会。
Again.	再来。
Good day. Pennyworth, of the Crown's Revenue. You are carrying rather more gold than the law finds comfortable. $Tax.Due coins would settle the matter.	日安。我是 Pennyworth，王冠税务署的。你身上带的金子比法律觉得舒服的多了一点。$Tax.Due 枚金币就能了结这件事。
Pay him.	付给他。
Of course. I'll walk with you until it is.	当然。那我就陪你走一段，直到你付为止。
Oh, good.	哦，很好。
Welcome to The Half Moon! What'll it be?	欢迎来到半月酒馆！要点什么？
Morning! Lovely day in Nettlewick, isn't it?	早上好！Nettlewick 今天天气真好，是不是？
Bramble, timber and planks. You look like someone who gets things done.\There's a grove of old oaks south-east of the village: the best wood in the Wildwood, and Ash won't let a soul near it. My men are there now, waiting. Cut my mark into the great oak in the middle of it, and they'll take that as leave to start. There's 150 gold in it, and work for half this village.	Bramble，木材和木板。你看起来像个能把事办成的人。\村子东南边有一片古老的橡树林：Wildwood 最好的木头，可 Ash 不让任何人靠近。我的人现在就在那儿等着。在林子中间那棵大橡树上刻下我的记号，他们就会当作可以动手了。有 150 金币给你，还有村里一半人的活计。
I'll cut your mark: fell the grove.	我去刻你的记号：砍掉那片林子。
I'll hear Ash out first.	我先听听 Ash 怎么说。
You've sided with Master Bramble. Cut his mark into the great oak in the old grove, south-east of the village.	你站在了 Bramble 老板一边。在古老林子里的大橡树上刻下他的记号，在村子东南边。
You've sided with Master Bramble. The great oak is in the old grove, south-east of the village.	你站在了 Bramble 老板一边。大橡树在古老的林子里，在村子东南边。
The great oak, in the middle of the grove. You can't miss it: it's the size of a house.	大橡树，在林子中间。你不会错过的：它有房子那么大。
Thrown in with Ash, have you? My men will remember that. Good day.	跟 Ash 一伙了，是吧？我的人会记住的。日安。
Marked? Then my men start at first light. Here's your 150, and my thanks.\Tom will have a saw-pit going there by the week's end. Come by for planks: you'll pay what I pay.	刻好了？那我的人明天一早就动手。这是你的 150，还有我的感谢。\Tom 这个星期结束前就会在那里开一个锯木坑。来买木板吧：我付多少，你就付多少。
Thank you, Master Bramble.	谢谢你，Bramble 老板。
Quest complete: The Old Grove! Bramble's men are felling the grove, and his sawmill is open to you.	任务完成：古老的林子！Bramble 的人正在砍伐林子，他的锯木厂对你开放了。
The mill's busy, and so is half the village. That's your doing.	锯木厂很忙，村里一半的人也很忙。这是你的功劳。
Timber and planks. Nothing for you just now.	木材和木板。现在没什么给你的。
There's my rescuer! Maren's feeding me up like a Midsummer goose. Edwin's taken to locking the pantry.\The house is yours, mind: I'll not go back, not with those brutes so near it. Keep it well.\There's one thing, though. My savings, in a tin with a T on the lid. They had it off my shelf when they took me, and I'd wager it's in that chest by their fire, up in their camp.	我的救命恩人来了！Maren 把我喂得像只仲夏节的鹅。Edwin 已经开始给储藏室上锁了。\那房子归你了，注意：我不回去了，那些畜生离得那么近。好好照看它。\不过有一件事。我的积蓄，在一个盖子上有个 T 的铁罐里。他们抓我的时候把它从我的架子上拿走了，我敢打赌它就在他们营地火堆旁的那个箱子里。
I found this in the lair.	我在巢穴里找到了这个。
Another time.	下次吧。
Not all boots and elbows, you won't! Two of them keep watch up there now, and a hobgoblin's got ears like a hare.\Keep low and soft (that's sneaking: the slow way, the way you stand), and keep out of the way they're looking. They look one way, mostly, and glance about now and then. Get behind one and it'll never know you were there. Right beside one, mind, it'll smell you whichever way it's facing.\And if one does see you, it shouts, and the other comes. Then you run. I'm too old to show you that part.	你可不能横冲直撞地进去！现在上面有两个在放哨，大地精的耳朵跟野兔一样灵。\压低身子，轻手轻脚（那就是潜行：慢慢走，就是你站的那个姿势），别待在它们看着的方向上。它们大多只看一个方向，时不时四下瞟一眼。绕到一个身后，它就永远不会知道你来过。不过要是就在它旁边，不管它朝哪儿，它都会闻到你。\要是有一个看到了你，它会大喊，另一个就会过来。那就跑。我太老了，这部分没法示范给你看。

The nomads at their well still sing about it, if you can get them to stop singing about everything else.	你点燃了铸造厂！我从小时候起就没见过那种光了。\第二团火？老铁匠们说，在他们之后，那把剑被带到了南边和西边，很远很远，带到了沙子底下的太阳熔炉。井边的游牧民还在唱关于它的歌，只要你能让他们停下来别唱其他所有东西。
Another one come to listen to it? No. No, you're warm. I can feel it from here.\I am Ysolde, of the Glass Wardens. The first king set us to keep watch over the Shard that fell, so nobody would use it, and nothing would wake it. We kept the watch four hundred years.	又一个来听它的？不。不，你是温暖的。我在这里都能感觉到。\我是 Ysolde，玻璃守卫之一。第一位国王派我们看守那块坠落碎片，好让谁都不能用它，什么都不能唤醒它。我们守了四百年。
It sings. Did nobody tell you? It sings all the time, a long slow song with no end, and if you listen long enough you'll do anything it asks.\Marshal Corvane listened longest. He keeps the Great Hall still, and the others with him. End him, warm one. End the watch.	它会唱歌。没人告诉过你吗？它一直在唱，一首长长的、缓慢的、没有尽头的歌，你要是听得够久，它要你做什么你都会做。\Corvane 元帅听得最久。他让大厅保持静止，其他人和他一起。终结他吧，温暖的人。终结这场守望。
Quest started: The Last Watch.	任务开始：最后的守望。
He sits on the dais in the Great Hall, through the east passage. He was kind, once. Don't let that slow your hand.	他坐在大厅的高台上，穿过东边的通道。他曾经很和善。别让这一点让你的手慢下来。
His seal. Then it's done, and he's quiet.\The Reliquary will open to you now, past the chapel. What the Order kept there was always meant for whoever finished the watch. I never thought it would be somebody alive.	他的印章。那就结束了，他安静了。\现在圣物堂会为你打开，就在礼拜堂后面。骑士团藏在那里的东西，本来就是留给完成守望的人的。我从没想过那会是个活人。
Thank you, Ysolde.	谢谢你，Ysolde。
Quest complete: The Last Watch!	任务完成：最后的守望！
He's gone; I felt it. But his seal: you left it with him? Then go back for it. I'll wait. I'm good at waiting.	他走了；我感觉到了。可他的印章：你把它留在他那儿了？那就回去拿。我会等。我很擅长等。
There's one more thing, and I'm sorry for it. Corvane kept the Shard's guardian asleep, in his way. With him gone it's stirring: the Shardborn, crystal grown in the shape of a giant.\Go west through the Watch Gallery, past the cells. Put it down. Then lay your hand on the Shard, and you'll know what we knew.	还有一件事，我很抱歉。Corvane 用他的方式让碎片的守护者一直睡着。他一走，它就开始骚动了：碎片之子，长成巨人形状的水晶。\往西穿过守望长廊，经过那些囚室。把它放倒。然后把你的手放在碎片上，你就会知道我们知道的事。
Quest started: The Fallen Shard.	任务开始：坠落碎片。
West, through the Watch Gallery. The light will show you the way. It always does.	往西，穿过守望长廊。光会给你指路的。它总是这样。
Warm hands, warm feet. You don't belong down here. Go carefully.	温暖的手，温暖的脚。你不属于这下面。小心走。
Welcome to the bank of Sulkhaven. Your valuables are safe with us.	欢迎来到 Sulkhaven 银行。你的贵重物品在我们这里很安全。
Welcome to The Sleeping Giant! What'll it be?	欢迎来到沉睡巨人酒馆！要点什么？
This is Sulkhaven's Town Hall. Births, weddings and deeds are written here.	这里是 Sulkhaven 镇公所。出生、婚礼和地契都在这里登记。
Amos keeps his voice down. "Since the dragon died, folk have come back to Sulkhaven, and I'm glad of every one. But those two Sisters in grey..."\"They pray with the rest, kind as you like. And at night they go down under my chapel floor. There's a stair there I never knew of, and singing under it, and I've been priest here thirty years."\"Would you go down and see? I'm too old, and I'm afraid."	Amos 压低了声音。“龙死了以后，人们回到了 Sulkhaven，每回来一个我都高兴。可是那两个穿灰衣的修女……”\“她们和其他人一起祈祷，要多和善有多和善。可到了晚上，她们就下到我礼拜堂的地板底下去。那里有一道我从来不知道的楼梯，楼梯底下有歌声，而我在这里当了三十年的神父。”\“你能下去看看吗？我太老了，而且我害怕。”
I'll go down.	我会下去。
Quest started: The Eldest of the Mending.	任务开始：弥合会的长老。
Welcome to Sulkhaven's church. All are welcome here.\Amos looks over at the two Sisters in grey, and doesn't say any more.	欢迎来到 Sulkhaven 的教堂。这里欢迎所有人。\Amos 看了一眼那两个穿灰衣的修女，就不再说什么了。
The stair's by the wall, where the floor's lifted. Mind yourself down there.	楼梯在墙边，地板掀起来的地方。在下面小心点。
It's quiet under the floor now, and I sleep. One of the Sisters came to me yesterday and asked if she could just pray, the ordinary way. I said she could.	现在地板底下安静了，我也睡得着了。昨天有个修女来找我，问她能不能就用普通的方式祈祷。我说可以。
It's quiet under the floor now. Mostly. Some nights I'd swear there's still singing.	现在地板底下安静了。大体上。有些晚上我敢发誓，还能听到歌声。
Welcome to Sulkhaven's church. All are welcome here.	欢迎来到 Sulkhaven 的教堂。这里欢迎所有人。
Have you tried the bakery? Best bread in Sulkhaven.	你试过那家面包房吗？Sulkhaven 最好的面包。
You're the one who took the cove with our lads. The watch looks after its own: what do you need?	你就是和我们的小伙子们一起拿下小海湾的那个人。守卫照顾自己人：你需要什么？
The watch's stores. Not for sale, not to you.	守卫的库存。不卖，至少不卖给你。
You've been up at the spring. Don't look like that: I saw you on the dunes.\Here's how it is. Leave the stones be, say the spring's dried up, and there's 100 gold in it for you. Water's a business, friend.	你去过泉水那边了。别那副表情：我在沙丘上看到你了。\事情是这样的。别管那些石头，就说泉水干了，这里有 100 金币给你。水是门生意，朋友。
Done. Give me the gold.	成交。把金子给我。
I'm pulling it down.	我要把它拆了。
You took Corvin's 100 gold to leave his dam be.	你拿了 Corvin 的 100 金币，不去管他的水坝。

Then you're a fool, and a thirsty one.	那你就是个傻瓜，还是个口渴的傻瓜。
A cup of water? A silver a cup. Prices are prices, friend.	一杯水？一杯一个银币。价钱就是价钱，朋友。
You pulled my dam down. Nobody buys water with a full well.	你把我的水坝拆了。井满着的时候谁也不会买水。
Water! Clean water, a silver a cup. Dunerest's well is dry, didn't you hear?	水！干净的水，一杯一个银币。Dunerest 的井干了，你没听说吗？

Old Hob? A spider bite, and he's going wild? Spider venom gets into the blood and calls to the beast in a man.\Here: a calming draught. Get it down him, however you can. It'll bring him back to himself, if anything will.	老 Hob？被蜘蛛咬了，他就发了狂？蜘蛛毒进了血里，会唤醒人身上的野兽。\给：一剂镇静药。不管用什么办法，让他喝下去。如果还有什么能让他恢复本性，就是这个了。
Thank you, Morwen.	谢谢你，Morwen。
The wild man roams the western woods, near the old watch stone. He attacks on sight.	那个野人在西边的树林里游荡，在古老的守望石附近。他见人就攻击。

Lost it already? Here's another. Down his throat, mind, not on the ground.	已经弄丢了？再给你一剂。灌进他喉咙里，注意，别洒在地上。
Heh. A stranger in the bog. Elder Pike is sick with marsh fever, and only my brew will cure him.\Fetch me four sprigs of bog myrtle from the marsh. You'll need a little Alchemy to pick it.	嘿。沼泽里来了个陌生人。Pike 长老得了沼泽热，只有我的药能治好他。\从沼泽里给我采四枝沼泽香桃木来。你得懂一点炼金术才能采。
I'll gather some.	我去采一些。
Bog myrtle grows in the fens round Brinebottom (Alchemy 3 to pick it).	沼泽香桃木长在 Brinebottom 周围的沼泽里（需要炼金术 3 级才能采）。

Quest started: Bog Lights.	任务开始：沼泽鬼火。
Good, good. Into the pot... there. Take this to Elder Pike, and don't you dare sip it.	好，好。放进锅里……好了。把这个带给 Pike 长老，你可别偷喝。
Four sprigs of bog myrtle, dearie. It grows among the reeds.	四枝沼泽香桃木，亲爱的。它长在芦苇丛里。
Off you go! The Elder's never far from the inn.	去吧！长老从不离旅店太远。
He drank it? Then he'll live, the old goat. Here: two of my best potions, and some coin.	他喝了？那他就能活了，那个老山羊。给：我最好的两瓶药水，还有一些钱。
Quest complete: Bog Lights! You receive 2 mega potions, 80 gold and Alchemy experience.	任务完成：沼泽鬼火！你获得了 2 瓶超级药水、80 金币和炼金术经验。
Mind the bog lights, dearie. They lead the foolish astray.	小心沼泽鬼火，亲爱的。它们会把傻瓜引入歧途。
Hee hee. The bog lights want someone who knows their herbs and can hold their own. You're neither yet, dearie. Off you go.	嘻嘻。沼泽鬼火要的是一个懂草药又能自保的人。你两样都还不是，亲爱的。去吧。
Charming.	真迷人。
Look at the shake on you, and that yellow in your eye. Marsh fever, dearie: malaria, the Court's wizards call it, the mosquitoes put it in your blood. It won't leave of itself, and dying won't shift it either.\Here, a tonic, drink it while it's good. It turns in a few days, so there's no keeping it by. Bog myrtle and mangrove bark, if you've to brew your own.	看你抖成这样，眼睛还发黄。沼泽热，亲爱的：巫师庭的巫师们管它叫疟疾，是蚊子把它放进你血里的。它自己不会走，死一次也赶不走它。\给，一剂补药，趁它还好的时候喝。过几天它就会变质，所以没法存着。要是你得自己酿，就用沼泽香桃木和红树皮。
Still shaking? A fever tonic's 45 gold, fresh from the pot.	还在抖？一剂退烧补药 45 金币，刚出锅的。
My pot's not free, dearie. Bog myrtle grows all round you, if you've the hands to brew it.	我的锅可不是白用的，亲爱的。沼泽香桃木在你周围到处都长，要是你有本事自己酿的话。
Magic is a matter of patience, and of not setting your beard on fire.	魔法讲的是耐心，还有别把自己的胡子点着。
Have you seen my husband? About so tall, about so wide, owes everybody money?	你见过我丈夫吗？大约这么高，大约这么宽，欠所有人钱？
The bakery's lovely this time of year. The bakery's lovely every time of year. It's a bakery.	每年这个时候面包房都很可爱。面包房每个时候都很可爱。它是一家面包房。
Lady Pettigrew's lost her hat again. To the geese. Again.	Pettigrew 夫人又把她的帽子弄丢了。给鹅弄丢的。又是。
A Shard Remnant got into my kitchen once. I hit it with a ladle. It's fine. The ladle isn't.	有一次一个碎片残迹钻进了我的厨房。我用汤勺打了它。它没事。汤勺有事。
My son wants to be an adventurer. I told him, get a proper job. Like goose herding.	我儿子想当冒险家。我跟他说，去找份正经工作。比如放鹅。
Somebody keeps pressing the big red button in the wizard's tower. I can tell. My washing turns into chickens.	有人老是去按巫师塔里那个大红按钮。我知道的。我晾的衣服会变成鸡。
You're the one who slew the dragon of Galaith? Then you'll have its scales.\This is the Dragonforge: the only fire in Brambleton hot enough to work dragon scale. Quest started: The Dragonforge.	任务开始：龙炉。

This is the Dragonforge. It's been cold a long time: there's only one dragon left in Brambleton, and it sits before the walls of Galaith.	这里是龙炉。它已经冷了很久了：Brambleton 只剩下一条龙了，它就盘踞在 Galaith 的城墙前。
Stand still the way you would to cast, and listen at the horn. And whatever it says, warm one, don't answer.	像要施法时那样站着别动，在号角边听。不管它说什么，温暖的人，都别回答。
You lay the Sword of Valour on the Deep Anvil and strike it, once..	你把勇气之剑放在深渊铁砧上，敲了一下..
..and the note goes down through the mountain and comes back up as a voice. The Sword of Valour is awake: Shard-light runs along its edge, and it pulls, gently, towards Galaith.	..那声音穿过大山向下传去，又作为一个声音返了上来。勇气之剑醒了：碎片之光沿着它的剑刃流动，它轻轻地朝 Galaith 的方向牵引。
You've nothing the anvil wants.	你没有铁砧想要的东西。
The Deep Anvil's runes burn on, blue and patient.	深渊铁砧上的符文继续燃烧着，蓝色的，很有耐心。
A great anvil of black iron on its dais, its runes dark and cold.	一块巨大的黑铁铁砧立在高台上，上面的符文又暗又冷。

The coals aren't hot enough to touch the blade.	炭火不够热，碰不到剑刃。

..and the blade drinks the light till it's too bright to look at. When it fades, the sword's warm in your hand, as if it had a pulse.	..剑刃吸饱了光，亮得让人没法直视。光芒退去时，剑在你手里是温的，好像它有了脉搏。
You've nothing the forge wants.	你没有熔炉想要的东西。
The Sun-Forge's disc still holds a little of the light.	太阳熔炉的圆盘上还留着一点光。

The three seals sink into the hollows of the door, and it grinds open. Quest complete: The Island of Dragons!	三枚印章沉进门上的凹槽，门嘎吱嘎吱地打开了。任务完成：龙岛！
Three hollows in the door, the shape of seals.	门上有三个凹槽，是印章的形状。
So. The heir of Brambleton comes to Vorgalath at last, and on foot. Eighteen winters late.\Eighteen winters I have waited at these walls. Eighteen winters the city has starved for want of the road, and all for one small debt. With the interest, it is not so small now.	那么。Brambleton 的继承人终于来见 Vorgalath 了，还是走着来的。晚了十八个冬天。\我在这些城墙前等了十八个冬天。十八个冬天，这座城市因为没有路而挨饿，全都是为了一笔小小的债。加上利息，现在可不那么小了。
What debt?	什么债？
A bargain, child, not a curse. Your first king cut me down to a size he could bind, and thought himself clever. I let your line keep the scraps of me, the mountain's gold and its Shards, for the firstborn heir of every generation: royal blood is the lock, and the lock needs feeding. They signed in blood. I kept a copy. I always keep a copy. Your mother broke it, and ran.	一个契约，孩子，不是诅咒。你们的第一位国王把我砍成了他能束缚的大小，还以为自己很聪明。我让你们的血脉保留我的残片，大山的金子和它的碎片，代价是每一代的长子继承人：王室的血就是锁，而锁需要喂养。他们用血签了字。我留了一份副本。我总是留副本。你母亲毁了约，逃走了。
She saved me.	她救了我。
My mother saved my life. The bargain ends today.	我母亲救了我的命。契约今天就结束。
Ends? It ends when I have you.\...That blade. Awake now, is it? Three fires, and the old knights' sting woken after all. How tiresome. No matter: I am more than I was.\Come, then, heir. Let us see whose debt is paid.	结束？等我得到你的时候它才结束。\...那把剑。现在醒了，是吗？三团火，老骑士们的刺终究还是被唤醒了。真麻烦。没关系：我已经不是从前的我了。\来吧，继承人。让我们看看谁的债被偿清。
Fight!	战斗！
The dragon of Galaith rises, wings blotting out the sky!	Galaith 之龙腾空而起，双翼遮天蔽日！
The chapel bell tolls for Mary. The last of the mourners lay their flowers on her grave.	礼拜堂的钟为 Mary 敲响。最后几个送葬的人把花放在她的坟上。
She was a good woman, your mother. Kind to all of us, and never a word about herself.\Beat me at cards every Sunday for sixteen years. I never did find out how.	她是个好女人，你母亲。对我们每个人都很好，从来不提自己。\十六年来每个星期天打牌都赢我。我一直没弄明白她是怎么做到的。
I'm so sorry. Mary was a friend to all of us here.\Come: I'll walk you home. She left something for you, to be given to you alone, in person. Those were her instructions. She underlined them twice.	我很难过。Mary 是我们这里每个人的朋友。\来吧：我陪你走回家。她给你留了东西，只能交给你一个人。那是她的嘱咐。下面画了两道线。
Lead the way.	带路吧。
You walk home together across Muddlesey. Edwin starts to say something legal now and then, and thinks better of it.	你们一起穿过 Muddlesey 走回家。Edwin 不时想说点法律上的话，又改了主意。
Your mother entrusted me with her last will and testament. She told me never to open this letter, and said you should not read it to anyone. It's her last words to you, and only you.\Thirty years a lawyer, and I've never wanted to read a letter more. I didn't. Let the record show.	你母亲把她的遗嘱托付给了我。她告诉我永远不要打开这封信，还说你不应该把它读给任何人听。这是她留给你的最后的话，只给你一个人。\当了三十年律师，我从没这么想读一封信。我没读。请记录在案。
He lays a letter on the table, sealed with your mother's wax.	他把一封信放在桌上，信用你母亲的蜡封着。
Thank you. If you don't mind, I would like to be left alone to read the letter.	谢谢你。如果你不介意的话，我想一个人读这封信。
