[Quests]
Bess the milkmaid, on the farm in the north, would know how to get across to the mainland.	北边农场的挤奶女工 Bess 应该知道怎么渡海去大陆。
Wim won't ferry anyone off Muddlesey without papers. Tobias, Bess's father, wrote them, but the hobgoblins took him. His house in the village is locked, and they have its key.	没有文件，Wim 不会载任何人离开 Muddlesey。Bess 的父亲 Tobias 写好了文件，但大地精把他抓走了。他在村里的房子锁着，钥匙在大地精手里。
Bess's father, Tobias, is locked in a cage in the hobgoblins' lair. Pick its lock to free him. His house key must be in the lair somewhere.	Bess 的父亲 Tobias 被锁在大地精巢穴的笼子里。撬开笼锁救他出来。他的房门钥匙一定在巢穴某处。
Tobias has given you his house. Unlock it with his key and find what he hid under its floor.	Tobias 把他的房子交给了你。用他的钥匙打开房门，找到他藏在地板下的东西。
Show your papers to Wim the ferryman, on Muddlesey's pier.	把你的文件给 Muddlesey 码头的船夫 Wim 看。
Ask the Herbalist in her cottage on the Puddleford farmlands if she needs a hand.	去问问 Puddleford 农田小屋里的草药师是否需要帮忙。
Bring the Herbalist 3 Cave Mushrooms (pick them in the old iron mine) and 2 Spider Silk (dropped by the giant spiders in the mine depths).	给草药师带去 3 个洞穴蘑菇（在旧铁矿里采摘）和 2 份蜘蛛丝（矿井深处的巨型蜘蛛会掉落）。
Find Brokk the dwarf in the Deep Hollow (through the hatch in the hobgoblin warren) and ask if he needs help.	在深谷（穿过大地精洞窟里的活板门）找到矮人 Brokk，问问他是否需要帮助。
Smash four skeletons ($SkeletonsSlain of 4) and slay the cave troll ($TrollSlain of 1) in the Deep Hollow, then return to Brokk.	在深谷击碎四具骷髅（$SkeletonsSlain / 4）并杀死洞穴巨魔（$TrollSlain / 1），然后回到 Brokk 那里。
Speak to Ranger Ash in Acornby, in the Wildwood south of King Rupert's Keep.	与 Acornby 的游侠 Ash 交谈，那里位于鲁珀特王堡以南的 Wildwood。
Visit the three old watch stones of the Wildwood ($WatchStones of 3): one by the north road, one in the western woods and one by the river.	拜访 Wildwood 的三块古老守望石（$WatchStones / 3）：一块在北路旁，一块在西边的树林里，一块在河边。
Clear the spider nest in the deep woods west of Acornby: slay five giant spiders ($WildSpidersSlain of 5), then return to Ranger Ash.	清除 Acornby 以西深林中的蜘蛛巢：杀死五只巨型蜘蛛（$WildSpidersSlain / 5），然后回到游侠 Ash 那里。
Ranger Ash and a timber merchant in Acornby are at odds over the old oaks south-east of the village.	游侠 Ash 和 Acornby 的一位木材商为村子东南边的古橡树起了争执。
Master Bramble wants the old oaks south-east of Acornby felled for timber; Ranger Ash wants them kept. Side with one of them.	Bramble 老板想砍掉 Acornby 东南边的古橡树做木材；游侠 Ash 想保留它们。站到其中一方。
Cut Bramble's mark into the great oak in the old grove, south-east of Acornby. His men will do the rest.	在 Acornby 东南边古林中的大橡树上刻下 Bramble 的记号。剩下的交给他的人。
Bramble's woodcutters are in the old grove already. Drive them off ($GroveCutters of 2).	Bramble 的伐木工已经在古林里了。把他们赶走（$GroveCutters / 2）。
The old grove is felled. Bramble's sawmill is busy, and half the village has work.	古林被砍光了。Bramble 的锯木厂很忙，村里一半的人有了活干。
The old grove stands. The rangers keep a lodge there, and half the village is still poor.	古林保住了。游侠们在那里守着一座小屋，村里一半的人仍然很穷。
Speak to the shepherd in Dullford, on the Greyflats.	与 Greyflats 上 Dullford 的牧羊人交谈。
Thin out the Greyflats' wolves: slay six wolves ($WolvesSlain of 6), then tell the shepherd.	减少 Greyflats 的狼群：杀死六只狼（$WolvesSlain / 6），然后告诉牧羊人。
Find the wolf den out east on the flats and slay the great Alpha Wolf.	找到平原东边的狼窝，杀死巨大的头狼。
Return to the shepherd in Dullford.	回到 Dullford 的牧羊人那里。
Speak to Old Morwen the witch in Brinebottom, in the fens.	与沼泽地 Brinebottom 的老女巫 Morwen 交谈。
Gather 4 bog myrtle in the fens (Alchemy 3) and bring them to Morwen.	在沼泽地采集 4 株沼泽香桃木（炼金 3 级），带给 Morwen。
Take Morwen's brew to Elder Pike in Brinebottom.	把 Morwen 的药汤带给 Brinebottom 的 Pike 长老。
Tell Morwen that the Elder has drunk her brew.	告诉 Morwen 长老已经喝下了她的药汤。
Speak to Captain Hale in Chillingham, on the Redrock road.	与 Redrock 路上 Chillingham 的 Hale 队长交谈。
Slay five bandits ($BanditsSlain of 5) on the roads and hills, then report to Captain Hale.	在道路和山丘上杀死五名强盗（$BanditsSlain / 5），然后向 Hale 队长报告。
Find the bandits' camp in the eastern hills and take back the stolen goods from their strongbox.	找到东部山丘中的强盗营地，从他们的保险箱里拿回被盗的货物。
Bring the stolen goods to Captain Hale in Chillingham.	把被盗的货物带给 Chillingham 的 Hale 队长。
You brought the stolen goods back to Captain Hale.	你把被盗的货物还给了 Hale 队长。
You sold the stolen goods to Red Meg. Chillingham's carts went without.	你把被盗的货物卖给了红发梅格。Chillingham 的货车落了空。
Speak to Qadir the merchant in Dunerest, on the edge of the Sunscar Desert.	与 Sunscar 沙漠边缘 Dunerest 的商人 Qadir 交谈。
Search the desert west of Dunerest for Qadir's lost caravan.	在 Dunerest 以西的沙漠中搜寻 Qadir 失踪的商队。
Slay the scorpions at the wreck ($ScorpionsSlain of 4), find the caravan's ledger and bring it to Qadir.	杀死残骸处的蝎子（$ScorpionsSlain / 4），找到商队的账本并带给 Qadir。
Speak to Brann the forgemaster in Chillingham, under Frostpeak.	与 Frostpeak 山下 Chillingham 的锻造大师 Brann 交谈。
Bring Brann 5 coal to light his forge.	给 Brann 带 5 块煤来点燃他的熔炉。
Slay the Frost Troll on the frozen lake east of Chillingham, which smashed Brann's waterwheel.	杀死 Chillingham 东边冰湖上砸坏 Brann 水车的冰霜巨魔。
Bring Brann 2 mithril bars (smelt mithril ore at a furnace).	给 Brann 带 2 根秘银锭（在熔炉里冶炼秘银矿石）。
Find the hermit at his camp in Muddlesey's western woods.	在 Muddlesey 西边树林的营地里找到隐士。
Bring the hermit 5 oak logs (chop oak trees: Woodcutting 5).	给隐士带 5 根橡木原木（砍伐橡树：伐木 5 级）。
Speak to the Druid in Nettlewick, in the Elderwood over the old bridge east of the Greyflats.	与 Nettlewick 的德鲁伊交谈，那里位于 Greyflats 以东、过了老桥的 Elderwood。
Find the Heartwood, the old heart stone of the Elderwood, north-east of Nettlewick, and touch it.	找到心木石，Elderwood 古老的心之石，在 Nettlewick 东北边，触摸它。
The blight has maddened the bears: slay four of them ($BlightBears of 4), then return to the Druid.	枯萎病让熊发了狂：杀死四只（$BlightBears / 4），然后回到德鲁伊那里。
Return to the Druid in Nettlewick.	回到 Nettlewick 的德鲁伊那里。
Speak to the Gravekeeper at King Rupert's Keep, on the Barrowdowns.	与 Barrowdowns 上鲁珀特王堡的守墓人交谈。
Find the old king's barrow, out on the downs west of King Rupert's Keep.	找到古王的坟冢，就在鲁珀特王堡以西的丘陵上。
Lay the Barrow King to rest.	让古冢之王安息。
Return to the Gravekeeper at King Rupert's Keep.	回到鲁珀特王堡的守墓人那里。
Old Hob of Acornby went missing, and his house is boarded up. One of its shutters hangs loose...	Acornby 的老 Hob 失踪了，他的房子被木板封了起来。其中一扇百叶窗松了...
Old Hob's diary: a giant spider bit him and he feels himself going wild. Ask Morwen the witch in Brinebottom for a cure.	老 Hob 的日记：一只巨型蜘蛛咬了他，他觉得自己正在变得疯狂。去问 Brinebottom 的女巫 Morwen 有没有治疗的办法。
Find the wild man in the western woods of the Wildwood and use Morwen's calming draught on him.	在 Wildwood 西边的树林里找到野人，对他使用 Morwen 的镇静药剂。
Hob is himself again! Talk to him.	Hob 恢复正常了！和他谈谈。
Hob is himself again, and his door is open to you.	Hob 恢复正常了，他的家门向你敞开。
You kept Hob's savings, and the madness took him again.	你私吞了 Hob 的积蓄，疯病又找上了他。
Speak to the Scout in Emberwatch: her patrol went out on the Ashwold and didn't come back.	与 Emberwatch 的斥候交谈：她的巡逻队去了 Ashwold，再也没有回来。
Recover three Scout's Tags from the ember hounds on the Ashwold and bring them to the Scout.	从 Ashwold 的余烬猎犬身上夺回三块斥候铭牌，带给斥候。
Speak to the settler by the shardstone in Puddleford.	与 Puddleford 碎片石旁的定居者交谈。
Bring the settler 10 planks and 4 stone blocks. Make them at her carpenter's bench: planks from logs, blocks from stone you mine from rocks with a pickaxe.	给定居者带 10 块木板和 4 块石块。在她的木工台上制作：木板用原木做，石块用你拿镐从岩石上采的石头做。
Sergeant Holt, at the King's castle's south gate, wants to know how smuggled goods get into town.	在国王城堡南门的 Holt 中士想知道走私货物是怎么进城的。
The fishermen talk of an old barrel on the lake path, south-west of the castle, that nobody ever moves. Search it.	渔夫们说，城堡西南边的湖边小路上有个旧木桶，从来没人挪动过。搜查它。
The barrel hides a tunnel to a smugglers' camp on the cove. Get to their leader without being seen: they only see ahead of them, never through cargo, tents or walls.	木桶下藏着一条通往海湾走私者营地的地道。不被发现地接近他们的首领：他们只能看见前方，看不穿货物、帐篷或墙壁。
Red Meg offered a truce: her trade off the town's streets, the watch off her cove. Go back to Sergeant Holt at the castle's south gate.	红发梅格提出休战：她的买卖不进城里的街道，巡防队不进她的海湾。回到城堡南门的 Holt 中士那里。
You carried Red Meg's truce to Sergeant Holt. The market's quiet.	你把红发梅格的休战提议带给了 Holt 中士。市场安静了下来。
You sent Sergeant Holt to the wrong beach. Red Meg owes you, and the watch doesn't quite trust you.	你把 Holt 中士引到了错误的海滩。红发梅格欠你一个人情，巡防队却不太信任你。
You led the watch into the smugglers' camp. It's theirs now, but Red Meg got away by sea.	你带着巡防队冲进了走私者营地。营地现在归他们了，但红发梅格从海上逃走了。
Sergeant Holt at the castle's south gate has word of Red Meg.	城堡南门的 Holt 中士有了红发梅格的消息。
Red Meg is hiding on a sandbar off the coast. Row the watch's boat out from the cove's shore, deal with her guards, and bring her in.	红发梅格躲在海岸外的一片沙洲上。从海湾的岸边划着巡防队的船出去，对付她的守卫，把她带回来。
Red Meg has given herself up. Tell Sergeant Holt.	红发梅格投降了。告诉 Holt 中士。
Red Meg, at her camp on the cove, has a job for a friend.	在海湾营地的红发梅格有件事要托付给朋友。
Carry a cask of Red Meg's brandy to Pell, the cellarer, in the courtyard of the Wizards' Court. The watch walks there now: if they see you with it, they'll take it.	把一桶红发梅格的白兰地送给巫师庭庭院里的酒窖管理员 Pell。现在巡防队在那里巡逻：如果他们看见你带着它，就会没收。
The Warden in Emberwatch has had word from the Ashwold.	Emberwatch 的守望者收到了来自 Ashwold 的消息。
The Warden says the Ashen Hand's burnt shrines on the Ashwold are lit again, and the fire's blue. There are three of them. Go and see.	守望者说，灰烬之手在 Ashwold 上被烧毁的神龛又亮了起来，而且火是蓝色的。一共有三座。去看看。
All three shrines burn cold and blue, and the Hand never lit a fire like that. Footprints lead from them to the Hand's camp on the Ashwold. Get near enough to hear what's said there, without being seen.	三座神龛都燃着冰冷的蓝火，灰烬之手从来没点过那样的火。脚印从那里通向 Ashwold 上灰烬之手的营地。靠近到能听见那里说话的地方，别被发现。
Strangers in grey are paying the Hand in Shards, to push them through the wall "for the one beyond the wall". Tell the Warden.	穿灰衣的陌生人正用碎片收买灰烬之手，要他们把碎片推过墙去，“为了墙那边的那位”。告诉守望者。
Tam, the cooper in Puddleford, is worried about his mother.	Puddleford 的箍桶匠 Tam 很担心他的母亲。
Tam's mother Annis goes every day to a quiet house behind Rufus's yard in Puddleford, where she says her late husband talks to her. Go and see her.	Tam 的母亲 Annis 每天都去 Puddleford 中 Rufus 院子后面的一座安静房子，她说她过世的丈夫在那里和她说话。去看看她。
Annis won't hear a word against the Shard from you. Cael, the penitent at the Cinder Chapel on the Ashwold, was fooled by strangers in grey once: he might find the words.	Annis 听不进你说碎片的任何坏话。Ashwold 上余烬礼拜堂的忏悔者 Cael 曾被穿灰衣的陌生人骗过：他也许能找到合适的话。
Cael wrote a few lines for Annis. Take them to her, at the quiet house in Puddleford.	Cael 为 Annis 写了几行字。把它们带给在 Puddleford 那座安静房子里的她。
You read Annis what Cael wrote, and walked her home to Tam.	你给 Annis 读了 Cael 写的话，然后陪她走回 Tam 的家。
You left Annis with her Shard. She's gone to the Mending's hall.	你让 Annis 留着她的碎片。她去了弥合会的会堂。
The watch brought Annis home when they shut the Mending's house.	巡防队查封弥合会的房子时，把 Annis 送回了家。
Sister Linnet of the Mending keeps a quiet house in Puddleford.	弥合会的 Linnet 修女在 Puddleford 守着一座安静的房子。
Sister Linnet won't tell an outsider where the Mending gathers. In one of their grey robes, she might take you for one of them.	Linnet 修女不会告诉外人弥合会在哪里聚会。穿上他们的灰袍，她也许会把你当成自己人。
Sister Linnet took you for one of the Mending. They gather under Shardglass Field, through a slab of glass at its east end. Keep the robe on, and they'll take you for one of their own.	Linnet 修女把你当成了弥合会的一员。他们在碎片玻璃原野下聚会，入口是原野东端的一块玻璃板。穿着长袍，他们就会把你当成自己人。
Cantor Ivo of the Mending says the great Shard waits in the sanctum, past the Font, for the faithful. Listen to it.	弥合会的领唱 Ivo 说，伟大的碎片在圣所里等待着信徒，就在圣泉池后面。去聆听它。
The great Shard spoke to you in your mother's voice, and now you know what the Mending wants: the King's Seam opened, under Galaith. Tell Sheriff Flo in Puddleford, or tell Sister Linnet you'll keep their secret.	伟大的碎片用你母亲的声音对你说话，现在你知道弥合会想要什么了：打开 Galaith 地下的王之缝隙。告诉 Puddleford 的治安官 Flo，或者告诉 Linnet 修女你会保守他们的秘密。
You told Sheriff Flo. The Mending's house in Puddleford is shut, and Galaith's watch knows of the hall.	你告诉了治安官 Flo。弥合会在 Puddleford 的房子被查封了，Galaith 的巡防队也知道了那座会堂。
You kept the Mending's secret. Their house is open to you, in grey.	你保守了弥合会的秘密。只要你穿着灰袍，他们的房子就向你敞开。
An iron gate shuts the Mending's sanctum, and it opens to those who pour their Shards into the Font. A lock can be picked, too, and the Font's keeper is a big one.	一道铁门封住了弥合会的圣所，只为把碎片倒进圣泉池的人打开。锁也可以撬，而圣泉池的看守是个大家伙。
You poured your Shards into the Font, and the gate opened for one of the faithful.	你把碎片倒进了圣泉池，大门为一位信徒打开了。
You picked the sanctum's lock.	你撬开了圣所的锁。
You broke the Font's keeper, and went through by force.	你击溃了圣泉池的看守，强行闯了过去。
Amos, the priest of Sulkhaven, doesn't sleep well since the Sisters came.	自从修女们来了以后，Sulkhaven 的神父 Amos 就一直睡不好。
Amos says the two Sisters in grey in his chapel go down under its floor at night, where there's a stair he never knew of, and singing. Go down and see.	Amos 说，他礼拜堂里那两位穿灰衣的修女晚上会走到地板下面去，那里有一道他从来不知道的楼梯，还有歌声。下去看看。
The Eldest of the Mending is dead. The great Shard is still singing.	弥合会的长者死了。伟大的碎片仍在歌唱。
You broke the great Shard. The Mending's scattered, and some of them have gone home.	你打碎了伟大的碎片。弥合会四散了，其中一些人回家了。
You kept a splinter of the great Shard: Mary's Whisper.	你留下了伟大碎片的一块碎屑：Mary 的低语。
Read the letter your mother left you, on the table in your cottage.	阅读你母亲留给你的信，就在你小屋的桌上。
Edwin the lawyer asked you to dinner. He and his wife Maren, Muddlesey's doctor, live in the brick house in Muddlesey's north-west, by the sea.	律师 Edwin 请你去吃晚饭。他和他的妻子、Muddlesey 的医生 Maren 住在 Muddlesey 西北边海边的砖房里。
Chop 3 logs for Maren's range with the axe she lent you (Chop a tree), and bring them to her.	用 Maren 借给你的斧头为她的炉灶劈 3 根原木（砍树），带给她。
Take an egg from the hen coop on Bess's farm, in the north, and fry it on Maren's range, upstairs in their house (use the egg on the range).	从北边 Bess 农场的鸡舍里拿一个蛋，在他们家楼上 Maren 的炉灶上煎熟（对炉灶使用鸡蛋）。
Give the fried egg to Edwin, at his house.	把煎蛋送给在家里的 Edwin。
Bess the milkmaid, on the farm in the north, knew your mother well.	北边农场的挤奶女工 Bess 很了解你的母亲。
Bess's husband Frank, the blacksmith, will help you make a sword and a shield. His smithy is by the dairy.	Bess 的丈夫、铁匠 Frank 会帮你做一把剑和一面盾。他的铁匠铺在牛奶房旁边。
Make a wooden sword and a wooden shield at Frank's workbench, then show them to him.	在 Frank 的工作台上做一把木剑和一面木盾，然后拿给他看。
Wield your sword and shield, and train until your Attack and Defence are level 2. Then ask Brother Aldous, at the chapel, about the trapdoor in its cellar.	手持你的剑和盾，训练到攻击和防御都达到 2 级。然后去问礼拜堂的 Aldous 修士关于地窖里那扇活板门的事。
Something of your mother's lies in the caves under the chapel.	你母亲的某样东西就在礼拜堂下面的洞穴里。
Something gold glints behind iron bars, out of reach of any hand.	铁栏后面有金色的东西在闪光，任何手都够不着。
Speak to the city guard at Drizzleport's port.	与 Drizzleport 港口的城市守卫交谈。
Go to Drizzleport's City Hall, where Brambleton's Parliament sits until Galaith opens again.	前往 Drizzleport 的市政厅，在 Galaith 重新开放之前，Brambleton 的议会就设在那里。
Take the Parliament's letter to the Mayor of Puddleford, in Puddleford's Town Hall.	把议会的信带给 Puddleford 镇公所的 Puddleford 镇长。
Goblins raid the farms east of Puddleford. Drive them off: wait for them at the farm's crossroads.	哥布林袭击了 Puddleford 东边的农场。把它们赶走：在农场的十字路口等它们。
Tell the Mayor the goblins are beaten.	告诉镇长哥布林已经被打败了。
Show the Mayor's pass to the King's Steward, at King Rupert's Keep, south of Puddleford.	把镇长的通行证给 Puddleford 以南鲁珀特王堡的国王总管看。
Slay five hobgoblins ($HobgoblinsSlain of 5) in the old iron mine, the mine depths or the warren, then return to the Steward.	在旧铁矿、矿井深处或洞窟里杀死五只大地精（$HobgoblinsSlain / 5），然后回到总管那里。
Find Bert, the great wizard, at the top of the Wizards' Court's tower.	在巫师庭塔顶找到伟大的巫师 Bert。
Bring Bert the Staff of Brambleton, the Grimoire of Unbinding and the Ember Ring.	给 Bert 带去 Brambleton 之杖、解缚魔典和余烬戒指。
Ask the Steward at King Rupert's Keep about the King's staff.	向鲁珀特王堡的总管询问国王的权杖。
Climb to the King's solar at the top of the keep (the stair in the keep's hall: the Steward's key) and take the Staff of Brambleton.	爬上王堡顶层国王的日光室（王堡大厅里的楼梯：总管的钥匙），拿走 Brambleton 之杖。
Ask the scholar Yusra, in Dunerest, about the Grimoire of Unbinding.	向 Dunerest 的学者 Yusra 询问解缚魔典。
The Grimoire lies in the sun priests' buried library, in the desert north of Dunerest. Dig out its entrance (a spade).	魔典在太阳祭司被掩埋的图书馆里，位于 Dunerest 以北的沙漠中。挖出它的入口（用铁锹）。
Take the Grimoire of Unbinding from its lectern in the buried library.	从被掩埋的图书馆的诵经台上拿走解缚魔典。
Ask the Warden in Emberwatch about the Ashen Priest's ring.	向 Emberwatch 的守望者询问灰烬祭司的戒指。
The Ashen Priest wears the Ember Ring, in the Cinder Fort high in the Cinder Peaks. Take it from him.	灰烬祭司戴着余烬戒指，在 Cinder Peaks 高处的余烬堡垒里。从他手上夺过来。
Find Captain Rook, the only captain mad enough to sail to Dragon Island, on the shore below King Rupert's Keep.	在鲁珀特王堡下方的海岸找到 Rook 船长，唯一一个疯狂到敢驶向龙岛的船长。
Captain Rook won't sail the moving reefs round Dragon Island without the chart of Orrin, the navigator who climbed the Sundered Tower and never came down.	没有领航员 Orrin 的海图，Rook 船长不会驶过龙岛周围移动的暗礁，而 Orrin 爬上裂塔后就再也没有下来。
Orrin's ghost haunts the Sundered Tower. His Shadow walks off with his face and his chart: break it.	Orrin 的鬼魂在裂塔中徘徊。他的影子带着他的脸和海图走了：击碎它。
Orrin's Shadow is broken. Go back up to Orrin.	Orrin 的影子被击碎了。回到上面的 Orrin 那里。
Take Orrin's chart to Captain Rook.	把 Orrin 的海图带给 Rook 船长。
Orrin steered by the Lodestar, and Captain Rook won't sail without it.	Orrin 靠天磁石掌舵，没有它 Rook 船长不肯出航。
Orrin's chart is read by the Lodestar, and its shrine is somewhere in the Rime. Ask the surveyor at Frostwatch Lodge.	Orrin 的海图要靠天磁石来读，而它的神龛在 Rime 的某处。去问问 Frostwatch 小屋的勘测员。
Nils says the Drowned Shrine lies frozen north-east of the lodge, past the ridge, with something big and white on guard. Bring back the Lodestar.	Nils 说，沉没神殿冻结在小屋东北边，翻过山脊就是，有个又大又白的东西在守着。把天磁石带回来。
Take the Lodestar to Captain Rook. (His hull wants 10 planks and 20 nails before he'll sail.)	把天磁石带给 Rook 船长。（出航之前他的船体需要 10 块木板和 20 根钉子。）
Captain Rook has Orrin's chart and the Lodestar to steer by. Now his hull wants mending.	Rook 船长有了 Orrin 的海图和用来掌舵的天磁石。现在他的船体需要修补。
Bring Captain Rook 10 planks and 20 nails to mend his hull.	给 Rook 船长带 10 块木板和 20 根钉子来修补船体。
Sail to Dragon Island with Captain Rook.	和 Rook 船长一起驶向龙岛。
The Temple of Valour is sealed. Take three Valour Seals from the island's drakes ($ValourSeals of 3), and open its door.	勇气神殿被封住了。从岛上的幼龙身上夺取三枚勇气印章（$ValourSeals / 3），打开它的门。
Enter the Temple of Valour and speak to its guardian.	进入勇气神殿，与它的守护者交谈。
Defeat the Guardian of Valour.	击败勇气守护者。
Draw the Sword of Valour from its stone.	从石中拔出勇气之剑。
The barrier still stands in the pass of Galaith. Raise the Staff of Brambleton against it.	屏障仍然挡在 Galaith 的山口。对它举起 Brambleton 之杖。
The barrier held: something beyond the world feeds it. Ask Bert, at the top of the Wizards' Court's tower.	屏障撑住了：世界之外有东西在供养它。去问巫师庭塔顶的 Bert。
Build a Shardgate in the old ring of standing stones on the hill north of the Wizards' Court: 4 silver bars, 2 sapphires, a Remnant's core and Shards, steadied by 8 active shardstones (Bert's plans).	在巫师庭以北山丘上的古老立石圈中建造一座碎片之门：4 根银锭、2 颗蓝宝石、一颗残迹核心和碎片，由 8 块已激活的碎片石稳住（Bert 的图纸）。
Step through the Shardgate into the core of the Shardrealm. Get past its Warden, and shatter the barrier's anchor with the Staff of Brambleton.	穿过碎片之门进入碎片界的核心。越过它的守望者，用 Brambleton 之杖击碎屏障的锚。
The anchor is shattered. Tell Bert.	锚被击碎了。告诉 Bert。
The barrier at the pass only thins now its anchor's gone.	锚没了，山口的屏障如今只是变薄了。
The dragon wears a hide of Shards now, and it laughed at the Sword of Valour. Ask Bert why.	那条龙现在披着一身碎片的皮，它嘲笑了勇气之剑。去问 Bert 为什么。
The Sword of Valour was made in three fires, and sleeps. Bert says the first was the Ashen Foundry, and the warden at Ashfall Outpost keeps watch over it.	勇气之剑是在三处火焰中锻成的，现在沉睡着。Bert 说第一处是灰烬铸造厂，Ashfall 前哨的守望者在看守它。
Warden Oska says the Forgemother holds the Foundry. Break it.	守望者 Oska 说铸炉之母占据着铸造厂。击溃它。
Light the Foundry's great hearth with its bellows, hot but not too hot, and temper the Sword of Valour in it.	用风箱点燃铸造厂的大熔炉，要热但不能太热，在里面淬炼勇气之剑。
The sword stirred in the Foundry's fire. Tell Warden Oska.	剑在铸造厂的火中苏醒了一些。告诉守望者 Oska。
Warden Oska says the sword's second fire was the Sun-Forge, under the sands; the nomads at their well still sing about it.	守望者 Oska 说剑的第二处火焰是沙下的太阳熔炉；井边的游牧民至今还在歌唱它。
Nomad Tarek says the Sun Keeper still holds the temple south of the well. Break it.	游牧民 Tarek 说太阳守护者仍然占据着井以南的神殿。击溃它。
The Sun-Forge burns only with the sun on it, brought down by its three mirrors. Tarek's grandmother had a song for them.	太阳熔炉只有在阳光照射时才会燃烧，阳光由它的三面镜子引下来。Tarek 的祖母有一首关于它们的歌。
The Sun-Forge blazes. Temper the Sword of Valour in it.	太阳熔炉熊熊燃烧。在里面淬炼勇气之剑。
The sword drank the sun. Tell Tarek.	剑吸饱了阳光。告诉 Tarek。
Tarek says the sword's last fire was the dwarves' Deep Anvil, under the mountains, and the delvers' camp is digging after it. Ask for Maud.	Tarek 说剑的最后一处火焰是矮人在山下的深渊铁砧，掘洞人的营地正在挖掘寻找它。去找 Maud。
Maud says the Stone King sits the old dwarf hall's throne. Break it.	Maud 说石王坐在古老矮人大厅的王座上。击溃它。
The dwarves locked the Deep Anvil with four rune stones. Maud's people sing a work-song older than any of them.	矮人用四块符文石锁住了深渊铁砧。Maud 的人唱着一首比他们任何人都古老的劳动歌。
The Deep Anvil is awake. Wake the Sword of Valour on it.	深渊铁砧苏醒了。在它上面唤醒勇气之剑。
The Sword of Valour is awake. Tell Maud.	勇气之剑苏醒了。告诉 Maud。
Go west to the pass of Galaith, in the West Range. Break the barrier with Bert's spell (use the Staff of Brambleton on it).	向西前往西部山脉中 Galaith 的山口。用 Bert 的法术打破屏障（对它使用 Brambleton 之杖）。
Kill the dragon before Galaith's walls. Only the Sword of Valour, wielded, can reach its heart.	在 Galaith 城墙前杀死那条龙。只有手持勇气之剑才能伤到它的心。
Ask the captain of the Royal Guard, at the palace door, to let you see the King.	请皇家卫队的队长在宫殿门口让你觐见国王。
The Lord Chancellor must give his leave. Find him in Galaith's Parliament.	必须得到大法官的许可。在 Galaith 的议会找到他。
Show your mother's amulet to the Royal Archivist, in Galaith's City Hall.	把你母亲的护身符给 Galaith 市政厅的皇家档案官看。
Take the Archivist's word to the Lord Chancellor, in the Parliament.	把档案官的证言带给议会的大法官。
Show the Chancellor's warrant to the captain at the palace door.	把大法官的手令给宫殿门口的队长看。
Go to King Rupert, in the throne room of the palace, and tell him of your mother.	去宫殿王座厅见鲁珀特国王，告诉他你母亲的事。
The Royal Archivist at Galaith's City Hall has something on her mind.	Galaith 市政厅的皇家档案官有心事。
The Archivist believes the Chancellor hoarded the city's grain while Galaith starved. His ledger would prove it: he keeps a strongbox in the City Hall.	档案官相信，在 Galaith 挨饿的时候，大法官囤积了城里的粮食。他的账本可以证明这一点：他在市政厅里有一个保险箱。
You have the Chancellor's ledger. Captain Hart, at the palace door, answers to the King, not to the Chancellor.	你拿到了大法官的账本。宫殿门口的 Hart 队长只听命于国王，而不是大法官。
You sold the Chancellor his ledger back. He's richer, you're richer, and the Archivist won't speak to you.	你把账本卖回给了大法官。他更富了，你也更富了，而档案官不会再和你说话。
You gave the ledger to Captain Hart. The Chancellor is in a cell, and the Archivist sits in his chair.	你把账本交给了 Hart 队长。大法官进了牢房，档案官坐上了他的位子。
An old woman at Drizzleport's landing looks lost.	Drizzleport 码头上的一位老妇人看起来迷路了。
Walk Granny Wren to her daughter Percy's door in Puddleford, east along the road. Go slowly: she can't keep up with you. (Lose her, and she'll be back at the landing.)	送 Wren 奶奶沿着大路向东走到 Puddleford 她女儿 Percy 的家门口。慢慢走：她跟不上你。（把她弄丢了，她就会回到码头。）
A painter at an easel in Puddleford's market square looks fed up.	Puddleford 集市广场上一位站在画架前的画家看起来很烦。
Get Mo, the black-and-white cat in Puddleford's market square, to keep still for Ottilie's painting: stroke Mo while Mo is sitting down.	让 Puddleford 集市广场上的黑白猫 Mo 为 Ottilie 的画保持不动：在 Mo 坐着的时候抚摸它。
Mo is sitting still at last. Tell Ottilie.	Mo 终于坐着不动了。告诉 Ottilie。
Speak to Mayor Hollis in Puddleford's Town Hall.	与 Puddleford 镇公所的 Hollis 镇长交谈。
Goblins hold the bridge to Sulkhaven, south-east of Puddleford, below its peninsula, and its guards have fled. Clear the goblins off the bridge ($BridgeGoblins of 4).	哥布林占据了通往 Sulkhaven 的桥，就在 Puddleford 东南边、半岛下方，桥上的守卫都逃走了。把哥布林从桥上清除（$BridgeGoblins / 4）。
Tell Mayor Hollis the bridge is clear.	告诉 Hollis 镇长桥已经清理干净了。
Speak to the Wyrmsmith in Sulkhaven.	与 Sulkhaven 的龙铁匠交谈。
Bring the Wyrmsmith of Sulkhaven three scales of Galaith's dragon.	给 Sulkhaven 的龙铁匠带三片 Galaith 那条龙的鳞片。
Magister Quill, in the Wizards' Court's great hall, studies the Shards.	巫师庭大厅里的 Quill 大学士在研究碎片。
Bring Magister Quill fifteen Shards for the Court's study (talk to him to give them).	给 Quill 大学士带十五枚碎片供巫师庭研究（和他交谈来交给他）。
Magister Quill wonders how a Shard holds the shape of a spell.	Quill 大学士想知道一枚碎片是怎样保持一个法术的形状的。
Bring Magister Quill the core of a Shard Remnant. They rise, now and then, from a beast's magic when it dies; killed, one leaves its core.	给 Quill 大学士带一颗碎片残迹的核心。野兽死去时，它们偶尔会从野兽的魔力中升起；杀死一个，就会留下它的核心。
Pick up twenty more Shards where beasts fall, then tell Magister Quill how it felt.	在野兽倒下的地方再捡起二十枚碎片，然后告诉 Quill 大学士那是什么感觉。
Someone in the Wizards' Court's great hall keeps saying hello. There's no one there.	巫师庭大厅里有人不停地打招呼。可那里没有人。
Bring Pip what he needs to be seen again: an egg, a feather and two cave mushrooms.	给 Pip 带来他重新被看见所需的东西：一个蛋、一根羽毛和两个洞穴蘑菇。
A lady on Goose Green, east of Drizzleport, is shouting at some geese.	Drizzleport 东边 Goose Green 上的一位夫人正在冲一群鹅大喊。
Get Lady Pettigrew's hat back from the geese's nest on Goose Green. They are not reasonable geese: go unseen, or go as a goose.	从 Goose Green 的鹅窝里拿回 Pettigrew 夫人的帽子。它们不是讲道理的鹅：要么悄悄去，要么变成鹅去。
An old woman on Nettlewick's green is looking for someone to practise a hex on.	Nettlewick 草地上的一位老妇人正在找人练习下咒。
Granny Bramble has hexed you weak as a kitten. Clear the rats round Nettlewick's green anyway ($HexRats of 4): your sword arm's no use. A spell, a potion, or someone else's body.	Bramble 奶奶给你下了咒，让你弱得像只小猫。不管怎样，还是要清除 Nettlewick 草地周围的老鼠（$HexRats / 4）：你握剑的手臂没用了。用法术、药水，或者别人的身体。
A scarecrow outside Acornby is deep in thought. Out loud.	Acornby 外的一个稻草人正在沉思。还说出声来了。
Find the philosophical scarecrow outside Acornby a hat. Any hat. He's not fussy. He says he's not fussy.	给 Acornby 外那个爱思考的稻草人找一顶帽子。什么帽子都行。他不挑剔。他说他不挑剔。
A cow outside Puddleford has something to say. Literally.	Puddleford 外的一头牛有话要说。字面意义上的。
Bring Duchess the cow three bunches of herbs: 'the good grass'. She has standards.	给奶牛 Duchess 带三束草药：“好草”。她是有标准的。
Constable Dobbs, in Puddleford's square, has a case. A very embarrassing case.	Puddleford 广场上的警员 Dobbs 接了个案子。一个非常尴尬的案子。
The Mayor's trousers are gone. Look round Puddleford's square for three clues, then tell Constable Dobbs who did it.	镇长的裤子不见了。在 Puddleford 广场周围找三条线索，然后告诉警员 Dobbs 是谁干的。
You've found the clues. Tell Constable Dobbs who took the Mayor's trousers.	你找到了线索。告诉警员 Dobbs 是谁拿走了镇长的裤子。
An old man in a faded livery waits on the road by Redrock Manor's gate.	一位穿着褪色制服的老人在 Redrock 庄园大门旁的路上等着。
Bring Ambrose, the last steward of Redrock Manor, the family's signet from the manor's vault. The old lord built the house so that no thief could find his way about it.	从庄园的金库里把家族印章带给 Redrock 庄园最后的管家 Ambrose。老领主把房子建得让任何小偷都摸不清方向。
You sold the Redrock signet to Spleen. The bandits keep the manor, and you're welcome in it.	你把 Redrock 印章卖给了 Spleen。强盗们占着庄园，而你在那里受欢迎。
You gave the signet back to Ambrose. The manor is his again.	你把印章还给了 Ambrose。庄园又是他的了。
Osric, in Sulkhaven, is standing outside his own front door, looking at it.	Sulkhaven 的 Osric 站在他自家的前门外，盯着门看。
Osric has bolted himself out of his house. Old Hilde next door says she knows a way in.	Osric 把自己闩在了房子外面。隔壁的老 Hilde 说她知道一条进去的路。
Hilde has opened the hatch in her floor, and says her gran's cellar never did stop at her wall.	Hilde 打开了她地板上的活板门，说她外婆的地窖从来就没在她家墙边停下。
You've drawn the bolts on Osric's door. Tell him.	你拉开了 Osric 门上的门闩。告诉他。
An old woman on the edge of the Sallow Waste is shouting about rats. Big ones.	Sallow 荒原边上的一位老妇人正在大喊老鼠的事。很大的老鼠。
Old Sal wants 6 Dust Rat dead.	老 Sal 想要 6 只尘鼠的命。
Done. Go back to Old Sal.	完成了。回到老 Sal 那里。
A prospector out by the Sallow Waste has more claim than friends.	Sallow 荒原那边的一位探矿人，矿权比朋友还多。
Prospector Hale wants 5 Scavenger dead.	探矿人 Hale 想要 5 个拾荒者的命。
Done. Go back to Prospector Hale.	完成了。回到探矿人 Hale 那里。
A wizard's apprentice is looking for someone who isn't afraid of the Reach.	一个巫师学徒在找一个不怕 Reach 的人。
Apprentice Ione wants 5 Wild Mage dead.	学徒 Ione 想要 5 个狂野法师的命。
Done. Go back to Apprentice Ione.	完成了。回到学徒 Ione 那里。
Magister Brack has lost something at the Reach. Something that walks.	Brack 大学士在 Reach 丢了东西。一个会走路的东西。
Magister Brack wants 4 Shard Golem dead.	Brack 大学士想要 4 个碎片魔像的命。
Done. Go back to Magister Brack.	完成了。回到 Brack 大学士那里。
A royal surveyor is on the Rime, counting yetis. He'd like some help with the sums.	一位皇家勘测员在 Rime 上数雪人。他想找人帮忙算数。
Surveyor Nils wants 4 Yeti dead.	勘测员 Nils 想要 4 只雪人的命。
Done. Go back to Surveyor Nils.	完成了。回到勘测员 Nils 那里。
Emberwatch's watch is short of hands, up in the Ashfall.	Emberwatch 的巡防队在 Ashfall 上人手不足。
Warden Oska wants 5 Ash Golem dead.	守望者 Oska 想要 5 个灰烬魔像的命。
Done. Go back to Warden Oska.	完成了。回到守望者 Oska 那里。
Nomads in the Scar are looking for someone foolish enough to fight a drake.	Scar 的游牧民在找一个傻到敢和幼龙打的人。
Nomad Tarek wants 2 Sand Drake dead.	游牧民 Tarek 想要 2 只沙地幼龙的命。
Done. Go back to Nomad Tarek.	完成了。回到游牧民 Tarek 那里。
A delver at the Stoneways' mouth lost her crew, and wants the reason dead.	Stoneways 入口处的一位掘洞人失去了她的队伍，想要元凶的命。
Delver Maud wants 2 Cave Horror dead.	掘洞人 Maud 想要 2 只洞穴恐魔的命。
Done. Go back to Delver Maud.	完成了。回到掘洞人 Maud 那里。
Your mother's hand has turned up somewhere you didn't expect.	你母亲的笔迹出现在了你没想到的地方。
Your mother left notes, wherever she went. There must be more of them.	你母亲无论走到哪里都会留下笔记。一定还有更多。
Seven notes, and laid edge to edge they make a map. It ends on Muddlesey, at something still.	七张笔记，边对边拼在一起就是一张地图。它的终点在 Muddlesey 上，在某个静止的东西那里。
A fisherwoman at Gull Point, the tip of Puddleford's peninsula, has been pulling glass out of the sea. It glows.	Gull Point（Puddleford 半岛的尖端）的一位渔妇一直从海里捞出玻璃。它会发光。
Nell Tarrow's nets come up full of glass that glows. Find out where it comes from.	Nell Tarrow 的渔网捞上来满满的发光玻璃。查明它从哪里来。
Under Gull Point, a door of black glass, sealed, a small round hollow in its middle. Tell Nell.	Gull Point 下面有一扇封住的黑玻璃门，中间有一个小小的圆形凹槽。告诉 Nell。
Nell says her grandmother Bryony remembers the old stories. Ask her.	Nell 说她的祖母 Bryony 记得那些古老的故事。去问她。
Bryony gave you a Warden's signet, her husband's find. It's cold: wake it at the door, with Shards and the knack (Shardcraft 15).	Bryony 给了你一枚守望者印章，是她丈夫找到的。它是冷的：在门口用碎片和诀窍唤醒它（碎片术 15 级）。
A cartographer has a camp in the Lantern Wood, deep under the sea, and wants someone to walk where she can't.	一位制图师在深海之下的灯笼森林里有个营地，想找个人去她去不了的地方。
Pell Varrow wants five places of the deep set on her chart: the Brinewater, Leviathan's Rest, the singing stone, the Wardens' Hold and the Shardfall. Stand in each.	Pell Varrow 想把深海的五个地方标在她的海图上：盐水湖、利维坦安息地、歌唱之石、守卫要塞和碎片坠落地。在每一处站一站。
Every place is on the chart. Take it back to Pell.	每个地方都标上了海图。把它带回给 Pell。
An angler in the Lantern Wood fishes the Brinewater, and there's something in it he'd like dead.	灯笼森林里的一位钓鱼人在盐水湖钓鱼，湖里有个东西他想让它死。
Morrow wants the Brine Mother dead: she took his boat, and his partner in it. She keeps to the Brinewater's north shore.	Morrow 想要盐水之母的命：她夺走了他的船，还有船上他的伙伴。她待在盐水湖的北岸。
The Brine Mother is dead. Tell Morrow.	盐水之母死了。告诉 Morrow。
Something keeps the gate of the Wardens' Hold, deep under the sea. It isn't alive, and it isn't hostile.	有个东西守着深海之下守卫要塞的大门。它不是活物，也没有敌意。
Ysolde, the last of the Glass Wardens, asks you to end Marshal Corvane, who listened to the Shard too long. He keeps the Great Hall.	玻璃守卫的最后一人 Ysolde 请你终结听碎片的话听得太久的 Corvane 元帅。他占据着大厅。
The Hollow Marshal is dead. Take his seal to Ysolde.	空壳元帅死了。把他的印章带给 Ysolde。
With the Marshal gone, the Wardens' charge is stirring. Ysolde has one more thing to ask.	元帅死了，守卫们守护的东西开始骚动。Ysolde 还有一件事要请求。
Ysolde says the Shardborn, the Fallen Shard's own guardian, is waking. Put it down, in the Shardfall, west of the Hold through the Watch Gallery.	Ysolde 说，碎片之子，坠落碎片自己的守护者，正在苏醒。在碎片坠落地把它打倒，它在要塞以西，穿过守望长廊。
The Shardborn is broken. Lay your hand on the Fallen Shard.	碎片之子被击碎了。把你的手放在坠落碎片上。
Pell Varrow, in the delvers' camp, has read three of the Glass Wardens' journals, and they don't make sense in threes.	掘洞人营地里的 Pell Varrow 读了三本玻璃守卫的日志，可它们三本三本地读说不通。
Pell wants all nine of the Wardens' journals read. They're on lecterns all over the Deep, where the Wardens left them.	Pell 想让人读完守卫们全部九本日志。它们放在深渊各处的诵经台上，是守卫们留下的。
All nine of the Wardens' journals read. Tell Pell.	守卫们的九本日志都读完了。告诉 Pell。
The singing stone in the Glimmering Galleries sings four notes, over and over.	微光回廊里的歌唱之石一遍又一遍地唱着四个音。
The Wardens left four chimes: water at the Brinewater, bone at Leviathan's Rest, flame in their chapel and glass at the Shardfall's rim. Strike them in the Singing Geode's order; it changes with the seasons.	守卫们留下了四个编钟：盐水湖的水、利维坦安息地的骨、他们礼拜堂里的火，以及碎片坠落地边缘的玻璃。按歌唱晶洞的顺序敲响它们；顺序随季节变化。
Ysolde, the last of the Glass Wardens, has something she never finished.	玻璃守卫的最后一人 Ysolde 有一件从未完成的事。
Go down into the Listening Room under the Reliquary. Stand still in the Channelling stance at the horn, and listen. Whatever it says, don't answer.	下到圣物堂下面的聆听室。在号角旁以引导架势静静站着，聆听。无论它说什么，都不要回答。
A cultist of the Hand prays apart from the others at the Cinder Chapel.	一名灰烬之手的教徒在余烬礼拜堂里独自祈祷，远离其他人。
Cael, of the Cinder Chapel, asks you to take his letter to his sister, Warden Oska of Ashfall Outpost.	余烬礼拜堂的 Cael 请你把他的信带给他的姐姐，Ashfall 前哨的守望者 Oska。
Something out on Shardglass Field asks riddles, they say, in your own voice.	人们说，碎片玻璃原野上有个东西用你自己的声音出谜语。
The Wandering Echo of Shardglass Field has three questions nobody has stayed to answer.	碎片玻璃原野的游荡回声有三个问题，从来没有人留下来回答。
Old Skadi at Frostwatch Lodge has never told a lie, she says.	Frostwatch 小屋的老 Skadi 说她从没说过谎。
Old Skadi tells three tales. Say which are true and which are lies.	老 Skadi 讲了三个故事。说出哪些是真的，哪些是谎话。
Abel, a shepherd on the river meadows west of Wildwood, has lost something.	Wildwood 以西河边草地上的牧羊人 Abel 丢了东西。
Abel's three lambs got over the river. Find them (blue on the rump) and catch them. None caught yet.	Abel 的三只小羊过了河。找到它们（屁股上有蓝色记号）并抓住它们。还一只都没抓到。
One of Abel's lambs is home. Two to find.	Abel 的一只小羊回家了。还要找两只。
Two of Abel's lambs are home. One to find.	Abel 的两只小羊回家了。还要找一只。
All three lambs are home: tell Abel.	三只小羊都回家了：告诉 Abel。
A collier is burning charcoal in a clearing in the woods south of Wildwood.	一个烧炭人在 Wildwood 以南树林的空地上烧木炭。
Hode the collier's clamp wants feeding: bring him 5 logs.	烧炭人 Hode 的炭堆需要添料：给他带 5 根原木。
Where the road stops in the woods, somebody still keeps the toll.	在树林里道路到头的地方，还有人在收过路费。
The toll-keeper wants three riddles answered. One answered.	收费人要你回答三个谜语。答对了一个。
The toll-keeper wants three riddles answered. Two answered.	收费人要你回答三个谜语。答对了两个。
A beekeeper keeps skeps in a high meadow in the West Range.	一位养蜂人在西部山脉的高山草甸上养着蜂箱。
Bring Merrin 3 honeycombs. Smoke a skep with a tinderbox first, then take its honey quickly.	给 Merrin 带 3 块蜂巢。先用火绒盒熏一熏蜂箱，然后迅速取走蜂蜜。
An old woman lives on the marsh's edge, in the far west where it meets the sea.	一位老妇人住在沼泽边上，在遥远的西边、沼泽与大海相接的地方。
Mother Natter made you a frog. Talk to the Frog King while you're one.	Natter 妈妈把你变成了青蛙。趁你还是青蛙，去和青蛙王说话。
The heron took the Frog King's pearl to her nest. A frog can't climb: search the nest as yourself.	苍鹭把青蛙王的珍珠叼回了自己的窝。青蛙爬不上去：以你自己的样子去搜窝。
You have the pearl. Mother Natter can make you a frog again, to give it back.	你拿到了珍珠。Natter 妈妈可以再把你变成青蛙，好把它还回去。
Somebody is working the rock in the Stoneways, without a sound.	有人在 Stoneways 里开凿岩石，却没有一点声音。
Pell the miner's ghost wants his pick: the wraith in the Stoneways' east tunnel has it.	矿工 Pell 的鬼魂想要他的镐：在 Stoneways 东边隧道里的怨灵拿着它。
You have Pell's pick. Take it back to him.	你拿到了 Pell 的镐。把它还给他。
An old beachcomber walks the sand in the far south of the desert coast.	一位老拾荒人在沙漠海岸遥远的南边沿着沙滩走。
Salt Agnes has seen three bottles washed up: two along her shore, one further round to the south-east. None found yet.	盐婆 Agnes 看到三个瓶子被冲上岸：两个在她的海岸边，一个更远，绕到东南边。还一个都没找到。
One of the castaway's letters found. Two to go.	找到了遇难者的一封信。还有两封。
Two of the castaway's letters found. One to go.	找到了遇难者的两封信。还有一封。
The castaway buried his strongbox under a cairn on the grass by the shore. Dig it up (a spade).	遇难者把他的保险箱埋在了海边草地上的一个石堆下。把它挖出来（用铁锹）。
You dug up the castaway's strongbox. Tell Salt Agnes.	你挖出了遇难者的保险箱。告诉盐婆 Agnes。
Sergeant Brask, at the King's Keep, is looking for someone.	王堡的 Brask 中士在找一个人。
You told Sergeant Brask where Pip was hiding. The boy's back in the levy.	你告诉了 Brask 中士 Pip 藏在哪里。那孩子回到了征兵队。
You paid Pip's way south on a carter's cart. The Keep is a deserter short, and Brask would like to know why.	你出钱让 Pip 搭一辆车夫的马车南下。王堡少了一个逃兵，Brask 很想知道为什么。
Elder Sabra of Dunerest is worried about the town's water.	Dunerest 的 Sabra 长老在担心镇上的水。
The spring that feeds Dunerest's well has failed, and Corvin sells water by the cup. Elder Sabra wants to know why. The spring is north of the town.	供应 Dunerest 水井的泉水干了，而 Corvin 按杯卖水。Sabra 长老想知道为什么。泉水在镇子北边。
You took Corvin's gold to leave his dam be. Dunerest still buys its water by the cup.	你收了 Corvin 的金币，没去碰他的水坝。Dunerest 仍然按杯买水。
Wenna, an eel-catcher in Brinebottom, has trouble with her traps.	Brinebottom 的捕鳗人 Wenna 的捕鳗笼出了问题。
Someone's been cutting Wenna's eel traps in the night. Find out who: the reeds east of the village are the place to look.	有人在夜里割坏了 Wenna 的捕鳗笼。查明是谁：该去村子东边的芦苇丛里找找。
You told Wenna it was Tobin. His mother's sent him to an uncle inland.	你告诉 Wenna 是 Tobin 干的。他母亲把他送到了内陆的叔叔那里。
You mended Wenna's traps, and she shares her catch with Tobin's father now.	你修好了 Wenna 的捕鳗笼，现在她会把渔获分给 Tobin 的父亲。
Bryony, Mirefold's healer, has her doubts about a doctor.	Mirefold 的治疗师 Bryony 对一位医生心存怀疑。
Pallister's cure is swamp water and salt. Bryony wants him gone. He's on the boardwalk.	Pallister 的药是沼泽水加盐。Bryony 想让他走人。他在木栈道上。
You took Doctor Pallister's gold, and he still sells his cure in Mirefold.	你收了 Pallister 医生的金币，他仍在 Mirefold 卖他的药。
You ran Doctor Pallister out of Mirefold. Bryony sells a real tonic now.	你把 Pallister 医生赶出了 Mirefold。Bryony 现在卖真正的补药。
Pip, in the Wizards' Court's great hall, is squinting at everyone through somebody else's spectacles.	巫师庭大厅里的 Pip 正透过别人的眼镜眯眼看着每个人。
Pip's good spectacles went missing the afternoon a cat had his body. Ask Inkwell, the Court's cat, what she did with them. She won't talk to people: ask her as an animal.	Pip 的好眼镜在一只猫占据他身体的那个下午不见了。去问巫师庭的猫 Inkwell 她把眼镜怎么了。她不跟人说话：变成动物去问她。
Inkwell remembers better with a fish inside her. Feed her one, then ask her again as an animal.	Inkwell 肚子里有条鱼时记性更好。喂她一条，然后再变成动物去问她。
Inkwell has had her fish. Ask her again, as an animal.	Inkwell 吃过鱼了。再变成动物去问她。
Inkwell dropped Pip's spectacles in the courtyard, and Biscuit, the dog outside the Court's gate, ran off with them. Ask Biscuit, as an animal.	Inkwell 把 Pip 的眼镜掉在了庭院里，巫师庭大门外的狗 Biscuit 叼着它跑了。变成动物去问 Biscuit。
Biscuit gave the 'shiny stick' to his friend Henrietta, a hen by the pond outside the Court's gate. Ask her, as an animal.	Biscuit 把那根“亮晶晶的棍子”送给了他的朋友 Henrietta，巫师庭大门外池塘边的一只母鸡。变成动物去问她。
Henrietta has been sitting on Pip's spectacles in her nest by the pond, hoping they'd hatch. Have a rummage in the nest.	Henrietta 一直坐在池塘边窝里的 Pip 的眼镜上，希望它们能孵出来。在窝里翻一翻。
Take Pip's spectacles back to him in the Wizards' Court's great hall.	把 Pip 的眼镜还给巫师庭大厅里的他。
Brother Aldous, at the chapel, kept something of your mother's for you.	礼拜堂的 Aldous 修士替你保管着你母亲的一样东西。
Mary told Brother Aldous: 'Jimmy knows where.' Read her charm by Jimmy at the cottage, by day, and ask him as a cat.	Mary 告诉 Aldous 修士：“Jimmy 知道在哪儿。”白天在小屋旁的 Jimmy 身边念她的咒语，变成猫去问他。
Jimmy says your mother hid something under the cottage, through his way in: a gap low in its front wall. Squeeze through it as a cat.	Jimmy 说你母亲把一样东西藏在了小屋下面，要从他的入口进去：前墙下方的一个缝隙。变成猫挤进去。
Tobias is staying with Edwin and Maren since you freed him.	自从你救出 Tobias 后，他就住在 Edwin 和 Maren 家里。
The hobgoblins took Tobias's savings tin. It'll be in the chest by the fire in their camp, south-east of the village. Two of them keep watch: sneak, and keep out of the way they're looking.	大地精拿走了 Tobias 的存钱罐。它会在村子东南边他们营地火堆旁的箱子里。有两个在放哨：悄悄行动，避开他们看着的方向。
You have Tobias's tin. Take it back to him at Edwin and Maren's.	你拿到了 Tobias 的存钱罐。把它还给在 Edwin 和 Maren 家的他。

A Warden's journal, open on its lectern where it was left.	一本守卫的日志，在诵经台上摊开着，就像被留下时那样。
A Warden's journal	一本守卫的日志
Warden's Signet	守卫图章戒指
A ring of grey metal set with a chip of violet glass, cold as the sea it came out of.	一枚灰色金属戒指，镶着一片紫色玻璃，冷得像它出自的大海。
A Warden's ring	一枚守卫的戒指
Marshal's Seal	元帅印章
Marshal Corvane's seal of office: the Glass Wardens' shard, in silver.	Corvane 元帅的官印：银制的玻璃守卫碎片。
The Marshal's seal	元帅的印章
Raw Glowfin	生光鳍鱼
A glowfin, its scales still giving off light. Cook it on a fire or a range (Cooking 22).	一条光鳍鱼，鳞片还在发光。在火堆或炉灶上烹饪（烹饪 22 级）。
Cooking 22	烹饪 22 级
Cooked Glowfin	熟光鳍鱼
Glowfin, cooked: rich and strange, and it warms you to your toes. Heals 20.	煮熟的光鳍鱼：浓郁而奇特，能让你从头暖到脚趾。恢复 20。
Shardglass Blade	碎片玻璃之刃
A blade of the Fallen Shard's own glass, grown, not forged. It hums when you swing it.	一把用坠落碎片自身的玻璃做成的刀刃，是长出来的，不是锻造的。挥动时会嗡鸣。
Wardens' Aegis	守卫之盾
The Glass Wardens' shield, kept for whoever finished their watch.	玻璃守卫的盾牌，为完成他们守望的人而保留。
Brine Pearl Ring	盐水珍珠戒指
A pearl as big as a thumbnail, out of the Brine Mother. It's cold, and it never gets warmer.	一颗拇指甲那么大的珍珠，来自盐水之母。它是冷的，而且永远不会变暖。
Lantern Tree	灯笼树
A mushroom as tall as a house, its green cap glowing from underneath. Spores drift down from it, slow as snow.	一棵房子那么高的蘑菇，绿色的菌盖从下面发着光。孢子从上面飘落，慢得像雪。
A giant glowing mushroom	一棵发光的巨型蘑菇
Shard Spire	碎片尖塔
A spire of crystal twice your height. It rings, very faintly, when you stand close.	一座两倍于你身高的水晶尖塔。站得近时，它会非常轻微地鸣响。
A crystal spire	一座水晶尖塔
A mushroom as tall as a house, its orange cap glowing from underneath. Spores drift down from it, slow as snow.	一棵房子那么高的蘑菇，橙色的菌盖从下面发着光。孢子从上面飘落，慢得像雪。
A mushroom as tall as a house, its blue cap glowing from underneath. Spores drift down from it, slow as snow.	一棵房子那么高的蘑菇，蓝色的菌盖从下面发着光。孢子从上面飘落，慢得像雪。
Royal Horse	皇家战马
A royal charger, groomed till it shines. It looks at you as if you might be an apple.	一匹皇家战马，梳理得闪闪发亮。它看着你，好像你可能是个苹果。
A royal charger	一匹皇家战马
Royal Horse Black	皇家黑马
A black royal charger with a white blaze, and opinions.	一匹额头有白斑的黑色皇家战马，很有主见。
Royal Easel	皇家画架
The King, half painted. He's in it twice: the painter hasn't decided which one stays.	国王，画了一半。画里有两个他：画家还没决定留下哪一个。
A portrait of the King	一幅国王的肖像
Seam Hammer-Head	缝隙锤头
The head of a hammer, cold and heavy, that broke the Seam. It is not a weapon. It is what is left of a cut that is not coming back.	打破缝隙的那把锤子的锤头，冰冷而沉重。它不是武器。它是一道不会再回来的切口所剩下的东西。
A hammer's head, cold and heavy	一个锤头，冰冷而沉重
Water Chime	水之钟
Strike	敲击
One of the Wardens' chimes: a bronze bowl of still water on a stand, a striker hung beside it.	守卫们的一座编钟：架子上一只盛着静水的青铜碗，旁边挂着一根敲棒。
A Wardens' chime	一座守卫的编钟
Bone Chime	骨之钟
One of the Wardens' chimes: bones as long as your arm, hung from a frame of whalebone, a striker beside them.	守卫们的一座编钟：和你手臂一样长的骨头，挂在鲸骨做的架子上，旁边有一根敲棒。
Flame Chime	火之钟
One of the Wardens' chimes: a bell of dark iron over a dish of embers that never go out.	守卫们的一座编钟：一口黑铁钟，下面是一盘永不熄灭的余烬。
Glass Chime	玻璃之钟
One of the Wardens' chimes: a rod of the Shard's own glass hung from a post, a striker beside it.	守卫们的一座编钟：一根碎片自身的玻璃做的棒子，挂在柱子上，旁边有一根敲棒。
Listening Horn	聆听号角
A great horn of black glass, its mouth to the wall, its narrow end at the height of an ear. The stone round it is worn smooth by people leaning.	一只巨大的黑玻璃号角，口对着墙，窄的一端在耳朵的高度。它周围的石头被人倚靠得光滑。
A horn of black glass	一只黑玻璃号角
The Voice	那个声音
Nothing. Nothing at all.	什么都没有。什么都没有。
Nothing	什么都没有
Ysolde	Ysolde
Ysolde, the last of the Glass Wardens, hardly there at all.	Ysolde，最后一位玻璃守卫，几乎已经不在这里了。
The last of the Glass Wardens	最后一位玻璃守卫
Odile's Log	Odile 的日志
A book in an oilskin, out of the Brine Mother's lake, its pages stuck together.	一本裹在油布里的书，来自盐水之母的湖，书页粘在一起。
A book, stuck shut	一本粘住的书
Pell's Book	Pell 的书
Pell Varrow's fair copy of the Wardens' journals: the first true history of the Glass Wardens.	Pell Varrow 誊清的守卫日志：玻璃守卫第一部真正的历史。
A history of the Glass Wardens	玻璃守卫的历史
Singing Remnant	歌唱残迹
A piece of the Shardborn that won't stop chiming. It's warm.	碎片之子的一块碎片，不停地鸣响。它是温热的。
A piece that won't stop chiming	一块不停鸣响的碎片
Quiet Draught	安神药剂
A grey-blue draught of glowfin and cave mushroom. It stills a voice in your head that isn't yours.	一瓶用光鳍鱼和洞穴蘑菇做的灰蓝色药剂。它能平息你脑中一个不属于你的声音。
Stills a voice that isn't yours	平息不属于你的声音
Wardens' Lantern	守卫之灯
Ysolde's lantern, its glass the Shard's own. Carried, it lights the dark a long way round you.	Ysolde 的灯笼，灯罩是碎片自身的玻璃。带在身上，它能照亮你周围很远的黑暗。
Lights the dark a long way round you	照亮你周围很远的黑暗
Blaze	大火
Wreckage burning. The heat is fierce.	燃烧的残骸。热浪逼人。
Burning	燃烧中
Keep back	退后
Ferry	渡船
The strait ferry: a broad, flat boat that's been tarred more times than anyone can count. Its ferryman leans on the steering oar and watches the water, not you.	海峡渡船：一艘宽阔的平底船，涂过的焦油多得数不清。船夫靠在舵桨上，看着水面，而不是你。
Orrins Shadow	Orrin 的影子
Orrin's face, Orrin's coat, and nothing at all behind the eyes. It hums the old sailing songs, wrong.	Orrin 的脸，Orrin 的外套，眼睛后面却什么都没有。它哼着古老的航海歌，调子是错的。
Shadow	影子
Rime Warden	霜白守卫
The shrine's guardian, frozen into the job. Icicles hang off its brows like a fringe.	神龛的守护者，被冻在了这份差事里。冰柱像刘海一样挂在它的眉毛上。
Guardian	守护者
Forgemother	铸炉之母
Slag and spite, poured into the shape of a smith. Its hammer-hands are still hot.	炉渣和怨恨，浇铸成一个铁匠的形状。它的锤子手还是热的。
Mother of the forge	熔炉之母
Sun Keeper	太阳守护者
A priest of the Sun-Forge, wrapped in gold leaf and a very bad temper, still at his post.	太阳熔炉的祭司，裹着金箔和一副极坏的脾气，仍然守在岗位上。
The Sun-Forge's priest	太阳熔炉的祭司
Stone King	石王
It sits the dwarves' throne as if it built it. It didn't. It ate the ones who did.	它坐在矮人的王座上，好像王座是它造的。不是。它吃掉了造王座的人。
On the dwarves' throne	坐在矮人的王座上
Scavenger King	拾荒者之王
Crowned in spoons and bottle tops. The knife's real.	戴着用勺子和瓶盖做的王冠。刀倒是真的。
King of the scavengers	拾荒者之王
Old Whitefang	老白牙
The oldest wolf in the Rime, white to the gums. Its scars have scars.	Rime 最老的狼，连牙龈都白了。它的伤疤上还有伤疤。
The oldest wolf	最老的狼
Slag Wyrm	熔渣巨龙
A drake that went swimming in the lava and liked it. It glows where it's cracked.	一只在岩浆里游过泳、而且喜欢上了的幼龙。它裂开的地方会发光。
Sand Mother	沙之母
Every scorpion in the desert is her child, and she's come about it.	沙漠里的每一只蝎子都是她的孩子，而她是为此而来的。
Mother of scorpions	蝎子之母
Orrin Ghost	Orrin 的鬼魂
A navigator's ghost in a salt-stained coat, still looking out to sea.	一位领航员的鬼魂，穿着被盐渍浸透的外套，仍然望着大海。
Orrin, a navigator's ghost	Orrin，一位领航员的鬼魂
Wandering Echo	游荡回声
Nobody, standing in the glass. When it moves, the glass hums.	谁也不是，站在玻璃里。它一动，玻璃就嗡鸣。
Penitent	忏悔者
A cultist of the Hand in a robe gone grey, praying where the others can't see him.	一个灰烬之手的教徒，长袍已经褪成灰色，在别人看不见的地方祈祷。
A cultist, praying	一个正在祈祷的教徒
One of the Mending	弥合会的一员
A traveller in a plain grey robe, a sliver of glass at the collar, smiling at you as if you'd met before.	一个穿着朴素灰袍的旅人，领口有一片玻璃，对你微笑，好像你们以前见过。
A traveller of the Mending	一位弥合会的旅人
Cael's Lines	Cael 的几行字
A few lines in Cael's hand, for Annis of Puddleford.	Cael 亲笔写的几行字，给 Puddleford 的 Annis。
A few lines, for Annis	几行字，给 Annis
Mending House Shard	弥合会之家的碎片
A Shard the size of a loaf, on a cloth on the table. It's warm, and there's a hum in it, like someone about to speak.	一块面包那么大的碎片，放在桌上的布上。它是温热的，里面有一种嗡鸣，像有人正要开口。
A warm Shard	一块温热的碎片
Undercroft Stair	地下室楼梯
A flagstone lifted out of the chapel floor, and steps going down. Cold blue light comes up them.	礼拜堂地板上被掀起的一块石板，台阶通往下面。冰冷的蓝光从下面涌上来。
Steps, going down	向下的台阶
Glass Slab	玻璃板
A slab of the field's glass, smoother than the rest, with a hairline crack all the way round it. Put your ear to it and something under it is singing.	原野上的一块玻璃板，比别处更光滑，四周有一道细如发丝的裂缝。把耳朵贴上去，下面有东西在歌唱。
A slab of glass	一块玻璃板
Washing Line Bare	空晾衣绳
A washing line with a cloth on it, and an empty peg where a robe was.	一根晾衣绳，上面挂着一块布，还有一个空夹子，原来夹着一件长袍。
A washing line	一根晾衣绳
Washing Line	晾衣绳
Take Robe	拿走长袍
A washing line behind the house: a cloth, and a grey robe of the Mending pegged out to dry.	房子后面的一根晾衣绳：一块布，还有一件弥合会的灰袍晾着。
Cold Shrine	冰冷的神龛
The Hand's altar, its candles lit again. The flames are blue, and give no warmth at all.	灰烬之手的祭坛，蜡烛又被点燃了。火焰是蓝色的，一点也不暖。
An altar, lit	一座点亮的祭坛
Great Shard	伟大的碎片
A Shard as tall as a man and a half, cold to stand near. Something in it is listening back.	一块一人半高的碎片，站在旁边都觉得冷。它里面有个东西也在聆听。
A great Shard	一块伟大的碎片
Shard Font	碎片圣泉池
Pour Shards	倒入碎片
A stone basin of still water with a cold blue light in it. The water doesn't move when you breathe on it.	一个盛着静水的石盆，里面有一道冰冷的蓝光。你朝它吹气，水也不动。
A basin of still water	一盆静水
Mary's Whisper	Mary 的低语
A splinter of the great Shard, no longer than a finger, on a cord. Hold it and it speaks in your mother's voice.	伟大碎片的一片碎屑，不比一根手指长，系在一根绳子上。握住它，它会用你母亲的声音说话。
A splinter of Shard on a cord	绳子上的一片碎片
Robe of the Mending	弥合会长袍
A grey robe of the Mending, rough wool, a sliver of glass sewn at the collar. In it, they'd take you for one of their own.	一件弥合会的灰袍，粗羊毛，领口缝着一片玻璃。穿上它，他们会把你当成自己人。
Eldest of the Mending	弥合会长老
An old man, mostly glass, who smiles as if he's known you all your life.	一个老人，大半已成了玻璃，笑得像是认识了你一辈子。
Glassbound	玻璃缚者
More glass than cloth now, and slow with it. Whatever it hears, it's listened to for a long time.	如今玻璃比布还多，也因此变得迟缓。不管它听见了什么，它已经听了很久了。
Cantor of the Mending	弥合会领唱
One of the Mending's singers. The air round her hums a little, and is colder than it should be.	弥合会的歌者之一。她周围的空气微微嗡鸣，而且比应有的更冷。
A singer of the Mending	一位弥合会的歌者
Acolyte of the Mending	弥合会侍僧
A grey robe, a kind face, and glass growing out of the shoulders like frost. He looks glad to see you.	一件灰袍，一张和善的脸，玻璃像霜一样从肩膀上长出来。他看起来很高兴见到你。
An acolyte of the Mending	一位弥合会的侍僧
Sister of the Mending	弥合会修女
A woman in a plain grey robe, a sliver of glass at her collar. She has the kindest voice you've heard in a long while.	一个穿着朴素灰袍的女人，领口有一片玻璃。她的声音是你很久以来听过的最温柔的。
A sister of the Mending	一位弥合会的修女

Collier	烧炭工
A collier, black to the elbows and the eyebrows. He smells like a bonfire's morning after.	一个烧炭工，从手肘到眉毛都是黑的。他身上有篝火第二天早上的味道。

Combs the beach	在海滩上拾东西
Night Smuggler	夜间走私者
A smuggler with a lantern shuttered down to a slit. He's in no hurry, and watches the road the whole time.	一个提着灯笼的走私者，灯罩只留一条缝。他一点也不急，一直盯着路。

The Shardfall Deep	碎片坠落深渊
Hit things in a fight, with a weapon or your bare hands: the harder the blow, the more you learn. Forcing a chest open teaches you a little too. Better at it, you land more blows and hit harder, and you can wield better weapons. In the Fighting stance some of what you learn in a fight comes here.	在战斗中击打东西，用武器或赤手空拳都行：打得越重，学到的越多。撬开宝箱也能学到一点。越熟练，你命中的次数越多、打得越重，还能使用更好的武器。在战斗架势下，你在战斗中学到的一部分会归到这里。
Defence	防御
Take blows in a fight, and block or dodge them: you learn from every blow, landed or turned aside. Better defended, you're hit less often and for less, and you can wear better armour. Out of any stance (Cautious) some of what you learn in a fight comes here.	在战斗中承受攻击，格挡或闪避它们：每一击，无论命中还是被挡开，你都能从中学到东西。防御越好，你被击中的次数越少、伤害越低，还能穿更好的护甲。不在任何架势中时（谨慎），你在战斗中学到的一部分会归到这里。
Channeling	引导
Cast spells: every cast teaches you something, a harder spell more. The better you are, the harder a spell hits, the more surely it takes and the fewer Shards each cast drinks.	施放法术：每次施法都能学到一些东西，越难的法术学到越多。你越熟练，法术打得越重、越容易生效，每次施法消耗的碎片也越少。
Shardcraft	碎片术
Work Shard magic: wake shardstones, absorb the Shard Remnants beasts leave behind, break gear down for its Shards, enchant gear with a rare shard and write a spell of your own.	运用碎片魔法：唤醒碎片石，吸收野兽留下的碎片残迹，把装备拆解成碎片，用稀有碎片为装备附魔，以及书写你自己的法术。
Cook what you catch, hunt and grow, over a fire or at a range, and make meals of what you've cooked.	烹饪你捕到、猎到和种出的东西，在火堆或炉灶上都行，还能用烹饪好的东西做成饭菜。
Smelt ore and forge at a furnace and anvil, shear sheep, and tan, spin and make jewellery at a workbench. Mending worn gear on an anvil teaches you too.	在熔炉和铁砧上冶炼矿石和锻造，剪羊毛，在工作台上鞣皮、纺线和制作珠宝。在铁砧上修理磨损的装备也能学到东西。
Fish with a rod and bait where the water ripples (each catch takes a bait). Better fish bite for better fishers, in harder places.	在水面泛起涟漪的地方用钓竿和鱼饵钓鱼（每钓一条用一份鱼饵）。越好的钓手在越难的地方能钓到越好的鱼。
Mine rocks with a pickaxe, in the mines and caves. Digging with a spade teaches you a little too.	在矿井和洞穴里用镐开采岩石。用铁锹挖掘也能学到一点。
Chop trees with an axe.	用斧头砍树。
Pick herbs, berries and mushrooms, and brew draughts and potions from them at an alchemy table.	采摘草药、浆果和蘑菇，并在炼金台上用它们酿造药剂和药水。
Build on your homestead, and sow and harvest crops: in your own soil, or the farmer's furrows by Puddleford.	在你的家园上建造，播种和收获作物：在你自己的土地上，或者 Puddleford 旁农夫的田垄里。
Stealth	潜行
Go unseen and light-fingered: sneak past those watching, strike before you're seen, pick pockets and locks, lift wares from shelves and valuables from houses, and spot traps and secrets (and disarm the traps).	不被看见，手脚灵巧：从监视者身边溜过，在被发现前出手，扒窃和撬锁，从货架上顺走商品、从房子里拿走贵重物品，还能发现陷阱和秘密（并拆除陷阱）。

Hold the Line	坚守阵线

Reckless	鲁莽
The Arena Master looks you up and down. "I've watched you. You fight like you've nothing to lose, so lose the rest of it: forget your guard and finish them. One down, and on to the next before they know it."	竞技场主管上下打量着你。“我看过你打。你打起来像是什么都不怕失去，那就把剩下的也丢掉：忘了防守，干掉他们。倒下一个，在他们反应过来之前就打下一个。”
Duellist	决斗者
More of your blows land, and now and then you turn one of theirs aside.	你更多的攻击会命中，偶尔还能挡开对方的一击。
"A guard of the palace fights with a point, not a club," Captain Hart says, and shows you: the blade always between you and them, a flick of the wrist to turn a blow.	“宫殿的卫兵用剑尖打，不用棍棒，”Hart 队长说，并演示给你看：剑刃始终在你和对手之间，手腕一抖就能挡开一击。
Dirty Fighting	卑鄙打法
Stormcaller	唤雷者
Your spells much stronger, and dearer in Shards.	你的法术强得多，消耗的碎片也多得多。
"The gate taught me something," Bert says. "Don't sip the Shards. Pull. Pull everything you've got through at once. It costs, oh, it costs, but you should have seen the cat's face."	“那扇门教会了我一件事，”Bert 说。“别小口抿碎片。要拽。把你所有的碎片一次全拽过来。代价很大，哦，代价很大，可你真该看看那只猫的表情。”
Wardkeeper	护盾守护者
Your wards last twice as long, and drains and poisons hardly touch you.	你的护盾持续时间加倍，吸取和毒素几乎伤不到你。
"The Wardens kept the watch for a hundred years," Ysolde says. "This is how we kept ourselves: the Shards held close, like a cloak against the cold."	“守卫们守望了一百年，”Ysolde 说。“我们就是这样守护自己的：把碎片紧紧裹在身上，像一件御寒的斗篷。”
Listening	聆听
You hear Shards: remnants, clusters and Shard secrets near you show.	你能听见碎片：附近的残迹、碎片簇和碎片秘密都会显现。
"You heard it, down there, and didn't answer," Ysolde says. "Then you can learn what we learnt: to hear the Shards without them hearing you."	“你在下面听见了它，却没有回应，”Ysolde 说。“那你就能学会我们学会的东西：听见碎片，而不让它们听见你。”
Drinking	汲饮
Something in the Shards says your name, very softly.	碎片里有个东西在非常轻柔地念着你的名字。
The Shards in you are warm. They like it here.	你体内的碎片是温热的。它们喜欢这里。
More, says something at the edge of hearing. There's always more.	更多，听觉边缘有个东西在说。永远还有更多。
For a moment you remember a place you've never been: a hall of glass, and singing.	有那么一瞬间，你想起了一个你从没去过的地方：一座玻璃的大厅，还有歌声。
Sister Linnet takes your hands and closes them round a Shard. "Don't fight it, $Player.Brother. Let it in. It only ever wanted to be part of you." It goes into your palms, warm, and something in you is thirstier than it was.	Linnet 修女握住你的双手，把它们合拢在一块碎片上。“别抗拒它，$Player.Brother。让它进来。它一直只想成为你的一部分。”它温热地进入你的掌心，你体内有什么东西比以前更渴了。
Cutpurse	扒手
Better hands for pockets and shelves, and those watching look the other way more.	扒口袋和顺货架的手法更好，监视者也更常看向别处。
Meg grins and lifts your purse, and gives it back, all in one move. "Watch the eyes, not the pocket. When they look away, you're already done."	梅格咧嘴一笑，一个动作就把你的钱袋拿走又还了回来。“盯着眼睛，别盯着口袋。等他们看向别处时，你已经得手了。”
Harder to see and to hear, and a harder blow from where they never saw you.	更难被看见和听见，而且从他们看不见的地方出手会打得更重。
"Forty years I caught thieves," Ambrose says, "and I know how they're seen: by moving when you're looked at. Be still when eyes are on you, and you're a shadow."	“我抓了四十年小偷，”Ambrose 说，“我知道他们是怎么被发现的：在被人看着的时候动了。有人看着你时就别动，你就是一道影子。”
Ranger's Tread	游侠步法
"You kept the grove, so I'll keep nothing from you," Ash says. "Walk on the edges of your feet, where the moss is, and the deer won't even look up."	“你守住了古林，所以我对你毫无保留，”Ash 说。“用脚的外侧走，踩在长苔藓的地方，连鹿都不会抬头。”
Shield Wall	盾墙
Captain Hart watches you square up, and nods. "Good: you don't charge in. Now hold it properly. Shield high, chin down, and let them break on you."	Hart 队长看着你摆好架势，点了点头。“很好：你不会冲进去。现在好好举着。盾牌举高，下巴压低，让他们在你身上撞碎。”
Sure Step	稳步
Quicker on foot, and out of a fight sooner (you start to heal sooner after one).	脚步更快，也能更快脱离战斗（战斗后更快开始恢复）。
"You walk like a landsman, matey," Rook says. "Light on the feet: a deck pitches, and so does a fight. Step back, breathe, and it's behind you."	“你走路像个旱鸭子，伙计，”Rook 说。“脚步要轻：甲板会颠簸，战斗也会。退一步，喘口气，它就过去了。”
Riposte	反击
"You held off those bandits better than my own lads," Hale says. "Here's one more thing: when you turn a blow, don't wait. Answer it, while they're still open."	“你挡住那些强盗挡得比我自己的手下还好，”Hale 说。“再教你一件事：挡开一击之后，别等。趁他们还露着破绽，立刻还手。”
Folk say there's a man on Drizzleport's docks after dark who doesn't ask questions.	人们说，天黑以后 Drizzleport 的码头上有个不问问题的人。
Try the docks after dark, if you must. Not here.	非要的话，天黑后去码头试试吧。别在这儿。
Old Sully, in the tavern's corner, if you must. Not here.	非要的话，去找酒馆角落里的老 Sully。别在这儿。

Sage Patch	鼠尾草地
A clump of sage, soft and grey-green. It smells clean and sharp. (Alchemy 3)	一丛鼠尾草，柔软的灰绿色。气味清新而辛辣。（炼金 3 级）
The sage has been picked. It will grow back.	鼠尾草被采过了。它会重新长出来。

Warden Wall	守卫之墙
Dressed stone, set without mortar, every block cut to fit.	方整的石头，不用砂浆砌成，每一块都切得严丝合缝。
A wall of pale dressed stone, cut and laid by careful hands, a long time ago.	一堵浅色方石墙，很久以前由细心的手切割、砌成。
A rune of glass is set in the stone. It glows, faintly, when you look away.	石头里嵌着一个玻璃符文。你移开视线时，它会微微发光。
Warden Seal	守卫封印
A door of black glass in a frame of dressed stone. Runes glow round it. In its middle, a small round hollow.	方石门框里的一扇黑玻璃门。符文在它周围发光。门中间有个小小的圆形凹槽。
The Wardens' Seal, open: a way into the dark.	守卫封印，已打开：一条通往黑暗的路。
Warden Door	守卫之门

Shard Crystal	碎片水晶
Crystals growing out of the rock, violet and warm.	从岩石里长出来的水晶，紫色而温热。
A tall crystal, pale blue, ringing faintly if you breathe on it.	一根高高的淡蓝色水晶，朝它吹气会微微鸣响。
A thicket of crystals as tall as you are, humming together.	一丛和你一样高的水晶，一起嗡鸣。
Rose-white crystals, cold to the touch.	玫瑰白的水晶，摸上去冰冷。
Lantern Cap	灯笼菇
A mushroom taller than you are. Its gills give off a soft green light.	一个比你还高的蘑菇。它的菌褶散发着柔和的绿光。
A great orange mushroom, glowing like a lantern left on.	一个巨大的橙色蘑菇，像一盏没关的灯笼一样发光。
A blue-capped mushroom as tall as a door, humming with spores.	一个和门一样高的蓝顶蘑菇，孢子嗡嗡作响。
Glow Moss	发光苔藓
Moss that glows where you step on it.	你踩上去会发光的苔藓。
Deep Kelp	深海海带

A rib, longer than a ship's mast, curving over you.	一根肋骨，比船的桅杆还长，弯在你头顶。

Its backbone: each bone of it the size of a barrel.	它的脊梁：每一节骨头都有木桶那么大。
Warden Statue	守卫雕像
A Glass Warden in stone, a shard held up. The shard is real, and still glows.	一尊玻璃守卫的石像，举着一块碎片。碎片是真的，还在发光。
Warden Brazier	守卫火盆
A brazier of violet fire that gives off no heat at all.	一个燃着紫色火焰的火盆，一点热量都没有。
Warden Tomb	守卫之墓

I barked at a leaf today. It was very threatening. You're welcome.	我今天对着一片叶子叫了。它非常有威胁性。不用谢。
Ribbit. I'm a prince, actually. Long story. Don't kiss me, it doesn't work, I've checked.	呱。其实我是个王子。说来话长。别亲我，没用的，我试过了。
Arf. The fish here are fine. The fish up north are better. I don't make the rules.	嗷。这里的鱼还行。北边的鱼更好。规矩又不是我定的。
Snort. They say a horse can't count. I can count. I count every sugar lump I don't get.	噗噜噜。人们说马不会数数。我会。我在数我没得到的每一块方糖。
Carrying people is fine. It's the talking I mind. Nobody asks how my day was.	驮人我不介意。我介意的是聊天。从来没人问我今天过得怎么样。
Neigh. That means 'no'. They always take it as 'yes'.	咴儿。意思是“不”。大家总以为是“好”。
I am a royal horse. I have a royal saddle and a royal blanket. The flies don't care.	我是王室的马。我有王室的马鞍和王室的毯子。苍蝇根本不在乎。
One brushes the mane, then the tail. Never the other way. One isn't a peasant.	先梳鬃毛，再梳尾巴。绝不能反过来。我又不是农夫。
The white one gets the parades. I get the rain. I've made my peace with it. I haven't.	白的那匹去游行。我去淋雨。我已经接受了。我没接受。
Mrrp. People say I'm bad luck. I've never had any bad luck. Funny, that.	喵。人们说我不吉利。我自己从没倒过霉。真奇怪。
I crossed your path on purpose. You looked like you needed something to blame.	我是故意从你面前走过的。你看起来需要点东西来怪罪。
Mrrp. I fell off the roof this morning. On purpose. It was a test. Of the ground.	喵。今天早上我从屋顶上掉下来了。故意的。那是个测试。测试地面。
Have you got food? You've got the face of someone who's got food.	你有吃的吗？你长着一张有吃的脸。
Mrrp. I'm not fat. I'm built for winter. Every winter. All at once.	喵。我不胖。我是为冬天打造的。所有的冬天。一次全上。
Three houses feed me. None of them knows about the other two. Don't you tell them.	三户人家喂我。没有一家知道另外两家。别说出去。
Mrrp. I'm dressed for dinner. I'm always dressed for dinner. Where is dinner.	喵。我穿好了去吃晚宴的衣服。我一直穿着去吃晚宴的衣服。晚宴在哪儿。
I knocked a cup off the table to see if it would fall. It did. I'll check again tomorrow.	我把一个杯子从桌上推下去，看看它会不会掉。它掉了。明天我再检查一遍。
Grr. You smell like a sheep. No offence. Some of my best meals were sheep.	呜嗷。你身上有羊的味道。别介意。我吃过的最好的几顿饭里，有几顿是羊。
We howl at the moon. The moon has never once howled back. Rude.	我们对着月亮嚎叫。月亮从没嚎回来过。真没礼貌。
The pack's a family. A family that argues about who gets the leg.	狼群就是家人。为谁得到那条腿而打架的家人。
Grr. Cold? This isn't cold. Cold is when your howl freezes and falls on your feet.	呜嗷。冷？这不叫冷。冷是你的嚎叫冻住了，掉在你脚上。
White fur. Very good for hiding in snow. Very bad for hiding in anything else.	白毛。藏在雪里很好用。藏在别的地方都很糟。
I lead the pack. Mostly I lead it to arguments about the leg.	我领导狼群。大多数时候是领着大家为那条腿吵架。
Grr. Being in charge is all howling and no thanks.	呜嗷。当头狼全是嚎叫，没人说谢谢。
Grumph. I was asleep. For four months. I was having a lovely time.	呼噜。我在睡觉。睡了四个月。睡得很好。
Honey is the best thing in the world. Bees are the worst. It's a hard life.	蜂蜜是世界上最好的东西。蜜蜂是最坏的东西。日子难过啊。
Everyone runs when they see me. I only wanted to say hello. And maybe eat their lunch.	大家看见我都跑。我只是想打个招呼。顺便吃个午饭。
Squeak. The cellar's ours. The humans just store things in it for us.	吱吱。地窖是我们的。人类只是替我们往里面放东西。
I'm not dirty. I wash six times a day. The cellar's dirty. Different thing.	我不脏。我一天洗六次。脏的是地窖。那是两回事。
Cheese is a lie made up by people who've never met a rat. Give me bread.	奶酪是没见过老鼠的人编的谎话。给我面包。
Squeak. I'm not upside down. You're upside down.	吱。不是我倒挂着。是你倒过来了。
I see by shouting at things and listening for the echo. Very rude, I know. It works.	我靠对着东西尖叫、听回声来看。很没礼貌，我知道。我照样这么做。
Oh! Hello! I was a caterpillar last month. I don't want to talk about it.	哦！你好！上个月我还是条毛毛虫。我不想谈这个。
Flower. Flower. Flower. Oh, that one's a hat. Flower.	花。花。花。哦，那是一顶帽子。花。
I'm blue. On purpose. The brown ones say I'm showing off. I am showing off.	我是蓝色的。故意的。棕色的那些说我爱炫耀。我就是爱炫耀。
Time's up! You snap back into your own body.	时间到了！你弹回了自己的身体。

Arm wrestling, for a stake: take on the innkeeper, or wait for another player to take you on. Push as fast as you can: the stronger you are, the more each push counts.	掰手腕赌一把：挑战旅店老板，或等待其他玩家来挑战你。尽快用力：你越强壮，每次用力就越有效。
A coin under one of three cups. Find it, and you get two and a half times your stake back.	三个杯子中的一个下面有一枚硬币。找到它，你就能拿回两倍半的赌注。
The innkeeper shuffles the cups round and round...	旅店老板把杯子转来转去...
Which cup hides the coin?	哪个杯子下面藏着硬币？
There's the coin! You win {1} gold.	硬币在这儿！你赢得 {1} 金币。
Empty! The coin was under the {1} cup.	空的！硬币在{1}杯子下面。
The innkeeper rolls a die. Will the next roll be higher, or lower? Right, and you get 1.4 times your stake back.	旅店老板掷了一个骰子。下一次会更大还是更小？猜对了，你就能拿回 1.4 倍的赌注。
The innkeeper rolls...	旅店老板掷骰...
A {1}. Higher, or lower? (The same again, and the house wins.)	是 {1}。更大还是更小？（一样的话，庄家赢。）
A {1}! You called it, and win {2} gold.	是 {1}！你猜对了，赢得 {2} 金币。
the same again: the house wins	又是一样的：庄家赢
not this time	这次没猜中
The dice tumble across the baize...	骰子在台呢上翻滚...
{1} {2} in, at {3} gold each. The dice roll in {4} seconds.	已有 {1} {2}加入，每人 {3} 金币。骰子将在 {4} 秒后掷出。
{1} wins the pot of {2} gold!	{1} 赢得了 {2} 金币的奖池！
A tie! {1} share the pot of {2} gold.	平局！{1} 平分 {2} 金币的奖池。
You take {1} gold.	你拿走 {1} 金币。
Put a stake down and start a round: everyone at the tavern has 20 seconds to match it. The highest roll of three dice takes the pot.	下注开一局：酒馆里的每个人有 20 秒跟注。三颗骰子点数最高的人拿走奖池。

Left-click buys, sells, banks or makes one. Right-click for more (Buy 5, Sell All, Value, Inspect...).	左键点击购买、出售、存入或制作一个。右键点击查看更多（购买 5 个、全部出售、估价、查看...）。

Point at one: its level. Right-click: progress to the next. Left-click: its guide. How to level each, and what it opens: the journal's Skills tab.	指向一项：显示其等级。右键点击：距下一级的进度。左键点击：其指南。每项如何升级，以及解锁什么：见日志的“技能”页。

The arena	竞技场
Farming	耕种
Use a spade anywhere on open ground: now and then you turn up gold or something useful, and sometimes Shards.	在任何空地上使用铁锹：时不时会挖出金币或有用的东西，有时还有碎片。
Spells and Shards	法术与碎片
Shardstones	碎片石
A shardstone stands dormant till you activate it, once, with a few Shards. Travel from an active one and the world map opens with every stone you've activated on it: pick one to go there.	碎片石在你用少量碎片激活它之前一直处于休眠状态，只需激活一次。从一块已激活的碎片石出发，世界地图会打开并显示你激活过的每一块：选择一块即可前往。
The working of Shard magic: activating shardstones, dismantling gear (now and then a rare shard is left in the dust), writing a spell of your own, and enchanting. Use a rare shard on a weapon or armour in your pack (Enchant) to bind it in for good.	碎片魔法的运用：激活碎片石、拆解装备（时不时会在尘土中留下稀有碎片）、编写你自己的法术，以及附魔。对行囊中的武器或护甲使用稀有碎片（附魔），即可将其永久嵌入。
Raids	袭击
Now and then monsters attack a town in waves. Fight them off together: gold for each one you kill, and a reward for everyone who holds out.	时不时会有怪物成波攻击城镇。大家一起击退它们：每杀死一只都有金币，坚持到最后的每个人都有奖励。
Shop signs	商店招牌
Every shop has a sign by its door with a picture of what it sells.	每家商店门边都有一块招牌，上面画着它卖的东西。
Dropping things	丢弃物品
Your homestead	你的家园
The settler by Puddleford's homestone gives you an Access Crystal for a little building work. Touch a homestone (by every shardstone) to go home, or to visit a friend's. At home its homestone says who may visit: only you, friends, or anyone.	Puddleford 家园石旁的定居者会在你完成一点建造工作后给你一颗通行水晶。触摸家园石（每块碎片石旁都有）即可回家，或拜访好友的家园。在家时，家园石决定谁可以来访：只有你、好友，或任何人。
